### [1] [Quantifying Uncertainty In Wide Two-Layer Neural Networks: On The Law Of The Limiting Fluctuation Process](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05982)
*Arnaud Descours,Arnaud Guillin,Geoffrey Lacour,Manon Michel,Boris Nectoux,Paul Stos*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种利用随机偏微分方程信息直接计算宽两层神经网络预测不确定性的方法，避免了深度集成方法需要训练多个网络的高计算成本，并推导出了网络输出波动的极限方差解析表达式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经网络预测中的不确定性量化是实际应用的关键问题，但深度集成方法需要训练多个网络，计算成本高昂。本文旨在通过利用偏微分方程信息直接评估渐近方差来降低计算成本。

Method: 基于宽两层神经网络的轨迹中心极限定理，将随机波动刻画为加权Sobolev空间对偶中的中心高斯过程，推导出通过光滑函数检验得到的有限维分布的闭合协方差表示。该协方差通过一个非局部源项的后向输运方程的解来表达，其系数由平均场轨迹驱动。

Result: 获得了网络输出波动的极限方差解析表达式，并通过一维回归数值示例验证了结果。

Conclusion: 该方法为不确定性量化提供了一种计算高效的替代方案，无需训练多个网络，且理论上严格刻画了随机梯度下降训练下宽两层神经网络的渐近波动特性。

Abstract: Uncertainty quantification in neural networks prediction is a main issue for usual applications. Our approach seeks at reducing computation costs by directly evaluating uncertainty using PDE's information on the asymptotic variance, rather than the deep ensemble method which may be seen as a Monte Carlo estimation of the prediction, requiring the training of multiple networks. We thus study the law of the limiting process describing the random fluctuations around the mean-field limit of wide two-layer neural networks trained by stochastic gradient descent in a weak-noise regime. Building on a recent trajectorial central limit theorem, in which this limit is characterized as the weak solution of a linear stochastic evolution equation, we identify its law explicitly. More precisely, we show that it is a centered Gaussian process in the dual of a weighted Sobolev space, and we derive a closed covariance representation for the finite-dimensional distributions obtained by testing it against smooth functions. This covariance is expressed through the solution of a backward transport equation with a nonlocal source term, whose coefficients are driven by the mean-field trajectory. As a consequence, by testing against the activation function at a fixed input, we obtain an expression for the limiting variance of the corresponding network-output fluctuations. We illustrate this result numerically on a one-dimensional regression example.

</details>


### [2] [Hub-Aware Hybrid Search: Accelerating the Locally Aligned Ant Technique](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06198)
*Simone Vilardi,Reynier Peletier,Felipe Contreras,Kerstin Bunte*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 针对LAAT算法中密集枢纽导致计算开销过大的问题，提出两阶段解决方案：快速预处理定位枢纽并用似然模型替代，随后采用混合似然-信息素策略引导蚁群高效穿越密集区域，在合成数据和宇宙网N体模拟中验证了检测效率和鲁棒性的提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在天文观测和模拟数据中，检测纤维、流（1D）、壁（2D）和团簇（3D）等流形结构对理解宇宙演化至关重要。LAAT算法虽能有效恢复微弱多维结构，但密集枢纽（如节点或球状星团）会主导蚂蚁活动，造成不必要的计算开销，需要优化。

Method: 提出两阶段解决方案：1）快速预处理步骤定位密集枢纽，并用定制的似然模型替代；2）采用混合似然-信息素策略引导蚂蚁高效穿越密集区域。

Result: 在合成数据和大型宇宙网N体模拟中，验证了改进后的LAAT在检测效率和鲁棒性方面均有提升。

Conclusion: 两阶段优化方案有效解决了LAAT在密集枢纽区域的计算瓶颈问题，提升了算法在噪声高维点云中检测流形结构的性能，对宇宙大尺度结构研究具有实用价值。

Abstract: Finding manifold structures in noisy and high-dimensional point clouds is a challenging but important problem. In astronomical observation survey and simulation data the detection of filaments, streams (1D), walls (2D) and clusters (3D) gives rise to deeper understanding of the evolution of our universe. The Locally Aligned Ant Technique (LAAT) uses biologically inspired agents to efficiently recover faint and multidimensional structures. However, very dense hubs (e.g. nodes or globular clusters) dominate the ants' activity, creating unnecessary computational overheads. In this paper we propose a two-stage solution. First a fast preprocessing step locates the hubs and replaces them with a tailored likelihood model. Subsequently, a mixed likelihood-pheromone strategy guides the ants to efficiently bridge the dense regions. We demonstrate improvements in detection efficiency and robustness of LAAT with synthetic and a large-scale astronomical N-body simulation of the cosmic web.

</details>

### [3] [Incremental Computation for Efficient Programmable Inference in Probabilistic Programs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05348)
*Fabian Zaiser,Jack Czenszak,Martin C. Rinard,Vikash K. Mansinghka,Alexander K. Lew*

Main category: cs.PL

TL;DR: 本文提出将概率程序编译为确定性密度函数，再通过增量λ-演算技术对其进行增量计算，从而在蒙特卡洛推理中高效共享中间结果，避免重复计算，并支持非参数模型，同时通过模块化分解保证正确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 概率程序推理需要评估大量执行轨迹以找到后验密度高的路径，但现有系统在处理大数据集时中间结果重复计算开销巨大，且现有的增量推理方法缺乏模块化正确性保证，容易引入bug。

Method: 首先将概率程序编译为计算其密度函数的确定性程序；然后基于增量λ-演算，开发一种通用的组合式增量化技术应用于密度函数；通过将密度计算与增量化步骤分离，分别用指称逻辑关系论证其正确性；在Julia原型上实现。

Result: 该方法在多种模型和推理算法上实现了数据集规模维度的渐近运行时间改进，支持现有系统难以处理的非参数模型，并通过模块化分解保证了正确性。

Conclusion: 增量计算技术可以有效加速概率程序推理，且将密度计算与增量化分离的设计使得正确性论证变得模块化，是解决现有系统中增量推理正确性问题的可行路径。

Abstract: Inference in probabilistic programs generally requires evaluating many possible program executions to find those of high posterior density. To scale inference to large datasets, it is crucial that expensive intermediate results are shared across these many evaluations, rather than recomputed from scratch. This paper presents a new approach to realizing this sharing, based on \textit{incremental computation}, a technique for efficiently recomputing (deterministic) program outputs when program inputs change. First, we show how expressive probabilistic programs can be compiled to deterministic ones that compute their density functions. Then, building on the incremental $λ$-calculus, we develop a general technique for compositionally incrementalizing expressive functional programs, and apply it to these densities. The resulting incremental densities can be used to accelerate a broad range of Monte Carlo inference algorithms, including for nonparametric models not well supported by existing systems. Furthermore, our decomposition of incremental density computation into separate density and incrementalization steps allows for modular reasoning about correctness -- a key pain point in existing systems, where ad-hoc incrementalization features are a known source of soundness bugs. We develop denotational logical relations arguments for the correctness of each step independently, and implement the approach in a Julia prototype, finding that it leads to asymptotic runtime improvements in the size of the dataset on a range of models and inference algorithms.

</details>


### [4] [Look Before You Leap: Checking in on Type Tag Checking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05466)
*Stephen M. Watt*

Main category: cs.PL

TL;DR: 本文重新评估了动态语言和符号计算系统中几种通用值标记策略（badged对象头、低位标记、两种NaN-boxing布局）在现代AArch64和x86-64架构上的性能表现，发现低位标记在符号型负载中依然最快最简，而NaN-boxing在浮点密集型场景中能避免堆分配开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器架构和工作负载不断演化，关于boxed值、immediate值和类型标记的旧有经验需要重新校准，因此有必要在现代平台上系统性地重新评估不同标记策略的性能。

Method: 通过实验分离两种效应：避免堆分配常见标量值所节省的开销，以及从值字中获取标签信息而非执行堆读取所节省的开销。在多个厂商的AArch64和x86-64平台上对比了badged对象头、低位标记和两种NaN-boxing布局。

Result: 多次局部位操作通常比打开堆对象获取标签或小值更廉价。低位标记在符号型工作负载中依然是最简单且通常最快的选择；NaN-boxing的访问成本与之接近，并能避免普通浮点值的堆分配时间和空间开销。

Conclusion: 不同标记策略各有适用场景：低位标记适合符号型负载，NaN-boxing适合浮点密集负载；开发者应根据实际工作负载特性选择合适的通用值标记方案。

Abstract: Tagging of generic dynamic values is important in symbolic-computation and dynamic-language systems, but the trade-offs change as machine architectures and workloads evolve. In particular, old folklore about boxed values, immediate values, and type tags must be recalibrated from time to time. We revisit the performance of badged object headers, low-bit tagging, and two NaN-boxing layouts on a range of platforms in use today, including AArch64 and x86-64 architectures from different manufacturers. The experiments isolate two distinct effects: the cost avoided by not heap-allocating common scalar values, and the cost avoided by obtaining tag information from the value word rather than by performing a heap read. The results show that several local bit operations are often cheaper than opening a heap object to obtain a tag or small value. Low-bit tagging remains the simplest and usually fastest choice for mostly symbolic workloads, while NaN-boxing is close in access cost and avoids the time and space of heap allocation for ordinary floating-point values.

</details>


### [5] [CASS-RTL: Correctness-Aware Subspace Steering for RTL Generation with LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05680)
*Mohammad Akyash,Nowfel Mashnoor,Kimia Azar,Hadi Kamali*

Main category: cs.PL

TL;DR: CASS-RTL提出了一种通过识别和干预大语言模型中与RTL代码正确性相关的注意力头机制，在不需额外标注或重训练的情况下，提升硬件代码生成准确率10%-20%的新框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM生成RTL代码的方法存在严格周期精度和并发性要求，微小逻辑错误会导致电路不可用或不安全，而先前工作多依赖外部验证、微调等，忽视了LLM内部注意力机制与RTL正确性的内在关联。

Method: (i) 识别能区分正确与错误RTL的注意力头；(ii) 构建捕捉正确性相关信号的低维子空间；(iii) 设计轻量级、几何感知的推理时干预方法，模型无关且无需额外监督或重训练。

Result: 在多个模型上，VerilogEval的pass@1/5/10准确率提升10%-20%，CVDP提升5%。

Conclusion: CASS-RTL通过探索和利用LLM内部正确性感知组件，有效提升RTL生成可靠性，同时保持模型效率，无需大规模标注数据集进行微调。

Abstract: Recent advances in large language models (LLMs) have enabled the automatic synthesis (generation) of register-transfer level (RTL) code from natural language instructions, offering a promising pathway to accelerate chip design. Unlike typical natural language (and software coding) tasks, LLM-based RTL code generation demands strict cycle accuracy with concurrency, where minor logical errors can render a circuit unusable or insecure. While prior work has explored hallucination mitigation via external verification, self-evaluation prompts, retrieval-augmented prompting, domain specific fine-tuning, agentic solutions, and reasoning, these approaches largely overlook the attention-oriented internal mechanisms of LLMs that may inherently correlate with RTL correctness. This work proposes CASS-RTL, a first-of-its-kind framework for discovering and leveraging LLMs' correctness-aware components to guide RTL generation toward functionally accurate outputs. We (i) identify attention heads whose activation patterns consistently differentiate correct from incorrect RTL; (ii) construct a low-dimensional subspace capturing correctness-relevant signals; and (iii) design a lightweight, geometry-aware intervention that steers the model at inference time. CASS-RTL is fully model-agnostic, requires no additional supervision or retraining, and readily integrates into existing models. Empirically, we evaluate CASS-RTL on multiple models and observe 10%-20% improvement in pass@1/5/10 accuracy on VerilogEval and 5% improvement on CVDP, demonstrating the effectiveness of our method in enhancing reliability without sacrificing model efficiency or requiring a large labeled dataset for fine-tuning.

</details>

### [6] [QDAG: Declarative Composition of Reusable Analytics Methodologies at LinkedIn](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05662)
*Peter Ho,Praveen Chaganlal,Tianle Zhang,Endong Zhu*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: LinkedIn提出了QDAG系统，将分析方法论表示为声明式的有向无环图（DAG），取代传统的命令式胶水代码，从而提升复用性、可测试性和跨产品一致性，并在超过500台主机和100多个生产用例中部署，仅增加约10毫秒的中位开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生产分析产品常依赖可复用的方法论（如人员增长、技能增长等），但这些方法论通常以命令式胶水代码实现，导致团队重复编写编排代码、跨产品定义不一致、难以测试和分析。

Method: QDAG将分析方法论表示为声明式的有向无环图，节点可执行Apache Pinot查询、下游服务调用、内存SQLite连接、jq转换、条件逻辑、差分隐私聚合或调用其他QDAG。引擎采用按需驱动、记忆化、剪枝和并行化策略，在每次请求的分析中间层执行。

Result: QDAG已部署超过500台主机和100多个生产用例，中位编排开销约10毫秒，99百分位低于50毫秒。

Conclusion: 声明式方法论能够改善复用性、可测试性和跨产品一致性，同时保持交互式延迟。

Abstract: Production analytics products often depend on reusable methodologies: multi-step definitions such as headcount growth, top-skill growth, or differentially-private impression distributions. Although these methodologies define business-critical numbers, they are commonly implemented as imperative glue around OLAP queries, service calls, joins, transformations, and conditional logic. As a result, teams duplicate orchestration code, definitions drift across products, and methodologies are difficult to test or analyze.
  We present QDAG, a production system at LinkedIn that represents an analytics methodology as a declarative directed acyclic graph of typed steps. Nodes may execute Apache Pinot queries, downstream service calls, in-memory SQLite joins, jq transformations, conditionals, differentially-private aggregations, or calls to other QDAGs. The engine evaluates graphs demand-driven, memoized, pruned, and parallelized in the per-request analytics mid-tier. QDAG is deployed across more than 500 hosts and over 100 production use cases, adding roughly 10 ms median orchestration overhead and under 50 ms at the 99th percentile. Our experience shows that making methodologies declarative improves reuse, testability, and cross-product consistency while preserving interactive latency.

</details>


### [7] [Data Flow Control: Data Safety Policies for AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05679)
*Charlie Summers,Eugene Wu*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文提出数据流控制（DFC）框架，在DBMS查询中声明式指定并保证元组级数据流的策略执行，通过Passant重写层实现接近零开销的规模化策略实施，无需物化溯源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作关注SQL查询正确性，但忽略了安全性——语义正确的查询可能违反法规、隐私或业务约束。需要将数据安全从提示词和事后检查转移到数据基础设施中。

Method: 形式化数据安全为溯源单项式上的聚合谓词，提出Passant——一种可移植的查询重写层，在不物化溯源的情况下执行DFC策略。策略语言是优化器无关但可规模化高效执行的。

Result: 在DuckDB、Umbra、PostgreSQL、DataFusion和SQLServer五个DBMS引擎上，Passant实现约0%开销，比替代方案快数个数量级。

Conclusion: 数据流控制是将数据安全从提示词和事后检查转移到数据基础设施的第一步，可开源获取。

Abstract: Agents increasingly generate SQL, orchestrate pipelines, and automate data analysis on behalf of users. While recent work improves query correctness, correctness is not safety. A query may be semantically valid yet violate regulatory, privacy, or business constraints that govern how data may be combined and released. We argue that enforcing such constraints is fundamentally a data infrastructure problem.
  This paper introduces Data Flow Control (DFC), a framework to declaratively specify and guarantee policy enforcement over tuple-level data flows within a DBMS query. A key challenge is defining a policy language that is optimizer-invariant yet efficient to enforce at scale. We formalize data safety as aggregate predicates over provenance monomials and present Passant, a portable query rewriting layer that enforces DFC policies without materializing provenance. Across five DBMS engines -- DuckDB, Umbra, PostgreSQL, DataFusion, and SQLServer -- Passant achieves ~0% overhead and outperforms alternatives by orders of magnitude. As a result, Data Flow Control is the first step towards moving data safety from prompts and post-hoc checks into the data infrastructure. Data Flow Control is available open source at https://github.com/dataflowcontrol/data-flow-control.

</details>


### [8] [Causal Scaffolding for Physical Reasoning: A Benchmark for Causally-Informed Physical World Understanding in VLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05966)
*Tianyi Tang,Zhuoyi Lin,Zeyu Feng,Tianyi Ma,Yew-Soon Ong,Ivor Tsang,Haiyan Yin*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文提出CausalPhys基准测试（包含3000+视频/图像问题及专家标注的因果图），并基于此设计了因果图 grounded 的评估指标，以及因果推理对齐微调方法CRFT，系统性地提升视觉-语言模型在物理因果推理上的准确性与可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前最先进的视觉-语言模型在物理因果推理上仍存在系统性缺陷，常产生看似合理但实际错误的答案，缺乏对因果依赖关系的正确捕获能力。

Method: 1) 构建CausalPhys基准，包含3000+视频/图像问题及专家标注的因果图；2) 提出因果图 grounded 的评估指标，量化模型思维链与正确因果关系的对齐程度；3) 提出Causal Rationale-informed Fine-Tuning (CRFT)，显式地将VLM的推理过程与因果结构对齐。

Result: 对主流VLMs的全面分析揭示了其在捕获因果依赖关系上的系统性差距；CRFT在多个模型骨干上显著提升了推理准确性和可解释性。

Conclusion: CausalPhys通过数据集构建、因果评估与因果感知学习的统一框架，为推动VLMs向因果 grounded 的物理推理发展奠定了坚实基础。

Abstract: Understanding and reasoning about the physical world is the foundation of intelligent behavior, yet state-of-the-art vision-language models (VLMs) still fail at causal physical reasoning, often producing plausible but incorrect answers. To address this gap, we introduce CausalPhys, a benchmark of over 3,000 carefully curated video- and image-based questions spanning four domains: Perception, Anticipation, Intervention, and Goal Orientation. Each question is paired with an expert-annotated causal graph capturing object-attribute-event dependencies, enabling interpretable and fine-grained evaluation of causal understanding. Building on this, we formulate a causal-graph-grounded metric that quantitatively measures how well a model's chain-of-thought reasoning aligns with the correct causal relations, moving beyond answer-only accuracy and enabling systematic diagnosis of VLMs' causal reasoning failures. Using this metric, we conduct a comprehensive analysis of leading VLMs, revealing systematic gaps in capturing causal dependencies and underscoring the need for causality-aware learning. To address these limitations, we further propose Causal Rationale-informed Fine-Tuning (CRFT), which explicitly aligns VLM reasoning with causal structures. Extensive experiments demonstrate that CRFT substantially enhances both reasoning accuracy and interpretability across multiple model backbones. By unifying dataset curation, causal evaluation, and causality-informed learning, CausalPhys establishes a strong foundation for advancing modern VLMs toward causally grounded physical reasoning.

</details>


### [9] [Validation of graph databases against PG-Schema](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06127)
*Jacek Ciszewski,Jakub Kłos,Maxime Jakubowski,Dominik Tomaszuk,Filip Murlak*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文研究了PG-Schema图类型对图数据库实例的验证问题。无完整性约束时，联合复杂度为NP完全，数据复杂度为PTIME。当适当限制类型组合与联合的交替时，联合复杂度降至PTIME。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究图数据库实例验证的复杂度界限，特别是PG-Schema图类型的验证问题，以理解其计算可行性。

Method: 通过复杂度理论分析，分别考察联合复杂度和数据复杂度，并研究对类型组合与联合交替进行限制后的复杂度变化。

Result: 无完整性约束时，联合复杂度为NP完全，数据复杂度为PTIME。限制类型组合与联合的交替后，联合复杂度降至PTIME。

Conclusion: PG-Schema图类型的验证问题在数据复杂度上是易处理的，且通过合理限制语法结构可在联合复杂度上也达到易处理性。

Abstract: The problem of validating a given graph database instance against a given PG-Schema graph type without integrity constraints is NP- complete in terms of combined complexity and in PTIME in terms of data complexity. The combined complexity drops to PTIME when the alternation between type combinations and unions is suitably restricted

</details>


### [10] [TOKI: A Bitemporal Operator Algebra for Contradiction Resolution in LLM-Agent Persistent Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06240)
*Ziming Wang*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文提出TOKI，将LLM代理持久化记忆中的四种矛盾消解启发式方法形式化为双时间戳操作符族，明确其隔离级别、写时异常和审计溯源契约，并通过四个可靠性定理保证回放一致性，实验表明TOKI在保持LM法官的同时排除了三种写时异常，而现有基线均存在至少一种缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM代理的持久化记忆系统使用四种矛盾消解启发式方法（最后写入者获胜、证据加权合并、等待确认、按规则策略），但没有声明其假设的隔离级别或允诺的写时异常。矛盾消解本质上是写时并发控制，但缺乏明确的正确性契约，导致系统在回放一致性、信念漂移偏差和审计擦除方面存在未知风险。

Method: 将四种启发式方法类型化为双行模式上的双时间戳操作符族，每个操作符具有隔离前置条件和保留失败事实的审计行溯源注解；通过四个可靠性定理（soundness theorems）在隔离性、模式、溯源之间建立契约，并推广到n元冲突集；紧致性证明表明在关系调度模型中，裁决者的键控日志记录对于回放一致性是必要的。

Result: 在八个系统的判定矩阵中，所有在写入路径上保留LM法官的基线都存在至少一种写时异常；基于内容寻址的引擎层比较器通过移除法官避免了异常，而TOKI是唯一在保留法官的同时排除所有三种异常的系统。在LoCoMo自然工作负载切片上，审计行防御提升了0.86，消融TOKI类型化记忆层在1444个可回答问题上损失0.49准确率。

Conclusion: 本文的贡献是提出了一个明确的写时正确性规范契约，在隔离性、模式和溯源之间通过定理证明确保可靠性，揭示了每个生产启发式方法隐含但未被任何部署系统明确声明的一致性保证。

Abstract: Persistent memory for an LLM agent is a write-heavy substrate: every belief update is a versioned write, and a new claim may contradict a stored one. Production systems use four resolution heuristics (last-writer-wins, evidence-weighted merge, await-confirmation, per-rule policy), yet none declares the isolation level it assumes or the write-time anomalies it admits. We show that contradiction resolution is write-time concurrency control and make the missing contract explicit. TOKI types the four heuristics as one family of bitemporal operators over a dual-row schema, each with an isolation precondition and a provenance annotation that preserves the losing fact in an audit row. Four soundness theorems close the contract across isolation, schema, and provenance, lift the guarantees to operator pipelines, and extend the fold operators to n-ary conflict sets. A tightness companion proves that, within the relational schedule model, keyed logging of the adjudicating judge is necessary for replay consistency, which every audited baseline omits. A verdict matrix over eight systems localizes the gap: every baseline that keeps a language-model judge on the write path admits at least one of three write-time anomalies (replay inconsistency, belief-drift skew, audit erasure); a content-addressed engine-layer comparator avoids them only by removing the judge, and TOKI alone excludes all three while keeping it. On its one natural-workload slice the audit-row defence moves LoCoMo by 0.86, and ablating the typed memory layer removes 0.49 accuracy on 1,444 answerable LoCoMo questions; the cross-system comparison stays underpowered and claims no superiority. The contribution is the contract: a write-time correctness specification, proved sound across isolation, schema, and provenance, pinning the guarantee every production heuristic assumes but no deployed system makes explicit.

</details>

### [11] [The Evaluation Blind Spot: A Stereological Theory of Benchmark Coverage for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05169)
*Jason Z Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文建立了LLM基准覆盖的体视学理论，证明基准套件的有效维度d_eff在2.86-4.80之间，结构盲点比排名差距大两个数量级；通过子模贪心算法可在4个基准上找到稳定核心，7/12的基准即可达到90%覆盖率；此外独立解决了Gardner问题1.5，建立了C^2支撑函数的最优恢复极值率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM基准测试缺乏系统的覆盖理论，无法量化基准套件的盲区大小和排名不确定性，亟需从高维几何和体视学角度建立严格的覆盖度量标准。

Method: 提出体视学理论框架，将基准覆盖问题映射为凸能力轮廓间的可见Hausdorff距离；通过有效维度d_eff量化套件结构；采用子模贪心算法（Nemhauser 1-1/e保证）选择最优基准子集；利用卡方投影模型进行先验敏感性分析；在12个内部基准和27个Chatbot Arena类别上验证特征结构预测能力。

Result: 三个独立排行榜的d_eff均在[2.86,4.80]区间；结构盲点超过实际排名差距两个数量级；500次随机分裂中92%的试验导致第一名排名交换；4个基准构成稳定核心，7个基准达到90%覆盖率；特征值结构预测基准不可替代性（ρ=-0.69, p=0.013）和新信息引入能力（ρ=+0.38）。

Conclusion: LLM基准套件存在显著的结构盲区，排名对基准选择的敏感性极高，需要更多样化的评估体系；子模贪心算法可有效识别核心基准；同时解决了一个经典的凸体几何最优恢复问题。

Abstract: We give a stereological theory of LLM benchmark coverage. For any suite with effective dimensionality d_eff, the visible Hausdorff distance between two convex capability profiles consistent with the same scores is bounded by epsilon + C R m^(-1/(d_eff-1)), with matching Lipschitz lower bound. Empirically, three independent leaderboards (Open LLM v2, an extended 12-benchmark suite, LiveBench) all have d_eff in [2.86, 4.80] on their competitive frontier; the structural blind spot exceeds the observed runner-up score gap by two orders of magnitude and dominates statistical noise by 52-127x. Under a chi-squared projection model, the isotropic prior is the optimistic case; across six hidden-capability priors and four ambient dimensions the simulated half-split swap rate of the top two models stays in [0.38, 0.49], and a 500-trial random visible/held-out split shows that 92% of trials swap the top-1 ranking with on average 2.83 of 5 top-5 models changing. A submodular greedy algorithm with the Nemhauser (1 - 1/e) guarantee finds a stable core of 4 benchmarks; 7 of 12 suffice for 90% coverage, and the trained subset transfers across temporal quarters with 93-97% retention. A counterfactual validation across 12 internal benchmarks and 27 Chatbot Arena categories confirms that the eigenstructure predicts which evaluations are irreplaceable (rho = -0.69, p = 0.013 for removal disruption) and which external evaluations bring new information (rho = +0.38). As a second, independent theoretical contribution, we resolve Gardner's Problem 1.5 (1995) for C^2 support functions, establishing the minimax rate Theta(R/(kappa m^(2/(D-1)))) in general dimension via optimal recovery theory on S^(D-1).

</details>


### [12] [ERRORQUAKE: Heavy-Tailed Error Severity Distributions in Open-Weight Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05170)
*Jason Z Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Errorquake-10k基准，用连续0-4严重性评分替代标量错误率评估LLM。发现不同模型在相同准确率下错误严重性分布显著不同，证明严重性分布与错误率信息非冗余，建议将其作为独立维度报告。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有幻觉基准将所有错误等同对待（标量错误率），但"错误日期"与"捏造法庭裁决"严重性相差数个数量级。需要一种能区分错误严重性的评估方法。

Method: 构建Errorquake-10k基准（10,000查询，8领域5难度），采用0-4连续严重性评分；对21个开源模型拟合严重性分布指数（Gutenberg-Richter b值）；进行519项三标注者人类验证；证明非冗余性定理；提出严重性机制分类法。

Result: 210个模型对中85个在匹配准确率下b值95%置信区间不重叠；ICC(2,k=3)=0.85，LLM裁判排序rho=0.89；I(b; model | epsilon)=1.56 bits，64.5%的b方差不能被error rate解释；严重性机制分类kappa=0.83，低严重性错误71%为检索错误，高严重性错误39%为捏造。

Conclusion: 严重性分布应作为准确率的补充指标独立报告，它携带准确率无法捕捉的判别信息，能更全面反映模型的实际可用性。

Abstract: At matched accuracy, open-weight LLMs differ substantially in the shape of their error severity distribution -- a difference invisible to the scalar error rate. Hallucination benchmarks report a single error count and treat all errors as equivalent, yet a wrong date and a fabricated court ruling differ by orders of magnitude. We introduce Errorquake-10k, a 10,000-query benchmark scoring each response on a continuous 0-4 severity scale across 8 domains and 5 difficulty tiers, and we fit per-model severity distributions for 21 open-weight models. For each model we estimate a severity distribution index (b, the Gutenberg-Richter upper-tail slope) with 95% bootstrap confidence intervals. Headline: across the 210 model pairs, 85 have disjoint 95% b confidence intervals at matched accuracy (|Delta epsilon| < 0.05) on human-consensus scoring, e.g. deepseek-v3.2 vs. ministral-14b at epsilon = 0.586 and Delta b = 0.47. A 519-item three-rater human validation study confirms measurement reliability (ICC(2,k=3) = 0.85), validates the LLM-judge ranking (rho = 0.89), and confirms the dense-model scaling correlation on human data (rho_s = -0.86). We prove a Non-Reducibility Theorem showing that severity profile and error rate are informationally non-redundant (I(b; model | epsilon) = 1.56 bits; 64.5% of cross-model b variance is unexplained by epsilon). A severity mechanism taxonomy (kappa = 0.83) reveals that error type shifts categorically with severity: low-severity errors are retrievals (71%); high-severity errors are fabrications (39%) -- and this composition differs by model size (p < 0.0001). Severity distribution should be reported alongside accuracy; it carries discriminative information that the error rate cannot.

</details>

*Evan Ye*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过40个受控实验，追踪一个192维GRU系统从纯预测到区分自我因果与世界因果的转变过程，发现了四个必须按严格顺序满足的条件，并提出"能动性增益"（Agency Gain）作为量化指标。


<details>

Motivation: 探索仅做世界预测的系统如何发展出区分自身因果影响与其他因素的能力，即自我表征如何从预测系统中涌现。

Method: 设计40个受控实验组成发育序列，在192维GRU中逐步添加组件，追踪系统能否区分自我原因与世界原因导致的改变。提出能动性增益（A = Err_world - Err_self）作为度量指标。

Result: 发现四个必须严格顺序满足的条件：(1)持久状态形成稳定吸引子，(2)因果行动循环连接输出到输入，(3)本体感觉反馈使隐性因果知识显性化，(4)异步唤醒——感知学习先于行动学习。自我感知预测器在周期和混沌环境中均优于盲预测器。仅前向采样行动选择产生有意义的能动性增益，梯度方法失败。12个假设被证伪。

Conclusion: 自我表征的涌现需要特定的发育顺序和架构条件，仅靠预测编码不足以产生自我表征；能动性增益是有效的量化追踪指标。

Abstract: How does a system that merely predicts the world come to distinguish its own causal influence from everything else? We trace this transition in a minimal 192-dimensional GRU through 40 controlled experiments arranged as a developmental sequence, adding components one at a time and tracking whether the system can distinguish self-caused from world-caused changes.
  The developmental path reveals four conditions that must be satisfied in strict order: (1) persistent state forming stable attractors, (2) a causal action loop linking output to input, (3) proprioceptive feedback that makes implicit causal knowledge explicit, and (4) asynchronous awakening - perceptual learning must consolidate before action learning begins. We propose agency gain (A = Err_world - Err_self), the predictive advantage of knowing one's own action, as a metric to track this process. The self-aware predictor consistently outperforms the self-blind predictor across periodic (sinusoidal) and chaotic (Lorenz) environments, and the metric survives ablation of all auxiliary components. Only forward-sampled action selection produces meaningful agency gain; two gradient-based alternatives degenerate.
  Equally significant are 12 falsified hypotheses mapping where development stalls: predictive coding alone does not produce self-represent

</details>


### [14] [Staged Factorial Screening for Budget-Constrained Micro-Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05186)
*Felipe Chavarro Polania*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了预算受限的微预训练场景下，通过分阶段分数阶析因工作流在共享加速器上筛选候选方案的有效性。在固定单GPU训练循环上运行613次实验，发现总批次、深度和宽度在短预算下惩罚最大，随预算增加而放松；预声明种子全屏族中D、A、B、C在5和10分钟后保持非零估计，而E不成立；随机搜索可找到强候选但缺乏因子归因；60分钟桥接锚点均值最低但存在混淆。支持使用短设计筛选识别高惩罚方向，通过重复运行确认有前景锚点，并在缩减空间内局部优化的有界方法结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在预算受限的微预训练场景中，面对大量候选方案，需要在共享加速器上进行筛选后再投入更大搜索预算。当前缺乏系统的工作流来有效识别稳定早期效应结构，以避免资源浪费。

Method: 采用分阶段分数阶析因工作流，在固定单GPU训练循环上运行613次实验，覆盖不同时间长度（2、5、10分钟）的预筛选和后续筛选、种子重运行、锚点检查、基线对比（贪心与随机）、60分钟桥接包以及跨主机（Windows A100和Linux L40S）的锚点延续实验，并使用Benjamini-Hochberg校正进行统计推断。

Result: 总批次、深度和宽度在短预算下惩罚最大，随预算增加而放松；预声明种子全屏族中D、A、B、C在5和10分钟后经校正仍保持非零估计，E不成立；随机搜索可达到强候选但重复在低惩罚区域且无因子归因；60分钟桥接锚点均值最低但存在混淆；跨主机12/24小时延续中桥接均值最低而其他排序受主机影响。

Conclusion: 支持有界方法：使用短设计筛选识别高惩罚方向，通过重复运行确认有前景锚点，在缩减空间内局部优化。推荐桥接中心策略至24小时跨两台主机，但不主张硬件不变排序或通用超参数优化优越性。

Abstract: Budget-constrained micro-pretraining often requires triaging many candidate recipes on a shared accelerator before larger search budgets are spent. We study whether a staged fractional-factorial workflow can recover stable early effect structure in this setting. On a fixed autoresearch-derived single-GPU training loop, we run 613 experiments across pilot and follow-up screens at 2, 5, and 10 minutes; full 16-condition seeded reruns at 5 and 10 minutes; targeted seeded anchor checks; same-host greedy and matched-cost random baselines; a 60-minute bridge package; and bounded Windows A100 and Linux L40S anchor continuations through 24 hours. Main penalties from total batch, depth, and width are largest at short budgets and relax as budget increases. Within the predeclared seeded full-screen families, D, A, B, and C retain non-zero estimates at 5 and 10 minutes after within-budget Benjamini-Hochberg correction, while E does not. Random search can reach strong incumbents in this 32-condition space, but repeatedly in the same low-penalty region and without factor attribution. The 60-minute bridge anchor has the lowest mean, although that package does not separate workflow refinement from the larger bridge model's capacity advantage. In bounded 12-hour and 24-hour three-anchor continuations on both hosts, the bridge has the lowest sample mean while the non-bridge ordering stays host-sensitive. We therefore present a bounded methods result: use short designed screens to identify high-penalty directions, confirm promising anchors under repeated runs, and refine locally inside the reduced space. The evidence supports a bridge-centered recommendation through 24 hours on two hosts, not hardware-invariant ranking or general hyperparameter-optimization superiority.

</details>


### [15] [PyCC.id: A package for hypothesis-driven equation discovery with structural identifiability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05191)
*Federico J. Gonzalez*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文介绍了PyCC库，一种基于特征曲线（CCs）结构"骨架"的假设驱动方法，用于从时间序列数据中发现常微分方程（ODEs），通过整合先验知识来缓解逆问题的不适定性，并支持多种方程发现范式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据驱动方程发现是一个逆问题，通常因不适定性导致多个数学模型拟合数据效果相似。现有方法虽能通过引入假设和约束缩小搜索空间，但仍会产生多个候选模型，需依赖领域专家进行后验手动筛选，缺乏系统性和可扩展性。

Method: 提出基于特征曲线（CCs）的结构"骨架"方法：研究者定义与一族ODE相关联的骨架，基于领域知识迭代添加假设和先验来精炼模型。该方法具有模块化特性，可整合神经网络、符号回归、稀疏回归等多种方程发现范式，且部分骨架具有可证明的结构可辨识性。

Result: 开发了Python库PyCC，将上述方法封装为灵活工具，使研究者和工程师能够无缝定义骨架和假设，从时间依赖数据中发现ODE。

Conclusion: PyCC库提供了一种结构化、假设驱动的方程发现框架，通过骨架的可辨识性验证和模块化设计，有效提升了逆问题求解的可解释性与可靠性。

Abstract: Data-driven equation discovery is fundamentally an inverse problem that seeks to infer the governing differential equations of a system directly from time-series measurements. A known issue is the ill-conditioned nature of the inverse problem, which frequently produces multiple mathematical models that fit the data similarly well. One path to address this issue is by incorporating known hypotheses and constraints into the training phase beforehand. While this approach effectively reduces the search space, it still results in multiple candidate models, forcing practitioners to rely on post-hoc manual filtering based on their own domain expertise. A recent approach incorporates structural `skeletons' inspired by characteristic curves (CCs), defining a hypothesis-driven methodology. In this methodology, practitioners define a skeleton, which is associated with a family of ordinary differential equations (ODEs), and then add their hypotheses and priors based on their domain knowledge to refine the obtained model iteratively. An important advantage of this approach is that some skeletons have demonstrable structural identifiability properties, which are useful for checking whether the skeleton is correct or should be discarded. Furthermore, this formalism enables the use of multiple equation discovery paradigms due to its modularity (such as neural networks, symbolic regression, and sparse regression). In this work, we present the Python library PyCC, which condenses these efforts into a flexible tool that allows researchers and engineers to seamlessly define their skeletons and hypotheses to discover ODEs from time-dependent data.

</details>


### [16] [Temporal Preference Concepts and their Functions in a Large Language Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05194)
*Ian Rios-Sialer,Shantanu Darveshi,Shuai Jiang,Avigya Paudel,Anastasiia Pronina,Ipshita Bandyopadhyay,Justin Shenk*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过 mechanistic interpretability 方法，在蒸馏版 Qwen3-4B 中定位了处理时间偏好的因果子图，发现 LLM 的时间折扣比人类更平缓且偏好不稳定，并初步证明 steer vectors 可调控时间偏好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 越来越多地被用于需要权衡短期收益与长期后果的决策场景，但学界对其内部如何处理这类时间权衡知之甚少，缺乏对其时间偏好表征和推理机制的因果理解。

Method: 使用梯度归因和激活 patching 的汇聚证据，在 Qwen3-4B-Instruct-2507 中因果定位与时间偏好相关的子图；通过残差流分析时间维度的几何编码；进行行为分析比较 LLM 与人类的时间折扣率；使用 steer vectors 进行干预实验。

Result: 时间偏好子图位于模型的中上层；残差流在预期层编码了时间维度的几何结构；LLM 的时间折扣比人类平缓数倍，且偏好跨上下文不稳定；steer vectors 可初步改变时间偏好。

Conclusion: 机械可解释性有助于实现对 LLM 时间推理的可靠控制，应避免隐式依赖训练数据，转而采用显式控制机制。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly being deployed to make decisions that require trading off near-term gains against long-term consequences, yet little is known about how they internally represent or resolve these tradeoffs. In this work, we causally localize an underlying subgraph for temporal preference in a distilled LLM (Qwen3-4B-Instruct-2507), identifying mid-to-upper-layer nodes through converging evidence from gradient-based attribution and activation patching. We find that the geometry of time horizon is encoded in the residual stream at the expected localized layers. A behavioral analysis reveals that unintervened LLMs discount the future several times less steeply than humans, yet this preference is unstable across contexts, motivating explicit control rather than implicit reliance on training. Finally, we find suggestive evidence that steering vectors can shift temporal preference. Our work demonstrates how mechanistic interpretability can bring us closer to reliable control over how LLMs plan and reason

</details>


### [17] [Double Preconditioning (DoPr): Optimization for Test-Time Performance, not Validation Loss](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06418)
*Thomas T. Zhang,Alok Shah,Yifei Zhang,Vincent Zhang,Nikolai Matni,Max Simchowitz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出双预条件（DoPr）优化范式，结合梯度预条件和激活预条件，专门解决深度学习中测试时间反馈（TTF）导致的误差累积问题。实验表明DoPr在多种TTF场景下提升下游性能，但验证损失未必改善。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在自回归语言建模、流式生成模型、机器人策略学习等应用中，模型训练使用一步预测损失，部署时却需要沿自身预测滚动展开，导致训练/验证损失与下游指标（如任务成功率、生成质量）之间存在严重失配（即测试时间反馈TTF），且随任务长度增长而加剧。现有工作多从数据、架构、目标函数入手，本文提出从优化角度缓解这一误差累积问题。

Method: 提出双预条件（DoPr）优化范式，将梯度预条件（如Adam、Muon中的方法）与激活预条件（如KFAC）相结合，形成一种即插即用的优化干预手段，专门针对TTF场景设计。

Result: 在多种TTF设定下，DoPr显著提升了下游模型性能。值得注意的发现是：这些测试时性能的提升并不总是伴随着验证损失的改善，表明传统验证损失可能无法准确反映TTF场景下的真实模型能力。

Conclusion: 优化可以成为缓解TTF误差累积的有效新设计轴。DoPr作为一种简单有效的优化范式，为TTF场景下的模型训练提供了新思路。同时，验证损失与下游性能的脱钩现象对现有评估体系提出了新的挑战。

Abstract: Many modern applications of deep learning involve training a neural network via a one-step prediction loss (e.g., $L^2$ regression, cross-entropy), but deploy the network by rolling out along its own predictions. Key examples include autoregressive language modeling, flow-based generative modeling, and robot policy learning. It is well-documented that these settings induce a phenomenon we call test-time feedback (TTF): the mismatch between the training/validation loss and downstream metrics of interest, such as task success rate and generation quality, which grows with task length. While data curation, architecture, and objective design have been proposed to combat train-test shift in TTF settings, this paper proposes optimization as a new design axis to mitigate error accumulation. Specifically, we introduce a new optimization paradigm called double-preconditioning (DoPr) uniquely tailored to the challenges of TTF. DoPr combines gradient-wise preconditioning, as in Adam and Muon, with activation-wise preconditioning (AP), such as in KFAC. We show that the addition of AP yields a drop-in intervention for increasing downstream model performance across a range of TTF settings. Interestingly, these gains in test-time performance do not consistently accompany improvements in validation loss, opening new questions about how to properly evaluate models trained with one-step supervised objectives.

</details>


### [18] [State commitment learning: training language models to distinguish computation from memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05201)
*Fei Ding,Yongkang Zhang,Runhao Liu,Yuhao Liao,Zijian Zeng,Huiming Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出状态承诺学习（State Commitment Learning）目标，通过反事实擦除强化学习（CERL）训练模型区分持久状态与临时计算，减少推理对隐藏思考的依赖，在数学、逻辑推理等多任务上保持准确率的同时显著降低依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推理语言模型不区分用于计算的token和构成持久状态的token，所有隐藏思考都会保留在上下文中影响后续预测，导致下游推理可能依赖于失败的尝试、死胡同和不应安全依赖的私人草稿。

Method: 提出状态承诺学习（State Commitment Learning）训练目标，定义反事实准则——持久状态充分性（persistent-state sufficiency），使擦除隐藏思考后答案是否仍可用变得可训练和可测量。提出反事实擦除强化学习（CERL），在相同前缀下评估保留和擦除隐藏思考两条路径，仅当擦除路径正确时给予奖励。同时引入擦除依赖协议（Erasure Dependence Protocol）。

Result: 在数学、长链逻辑、科学问答和多轮工具使用评估中，CERL在不牺牲准确率的情况下显著减少答案对隐藏思考的依赖，一致优于仅正确性强化学习和长答案SFT基线。

Conclusion: 通过显式训练模型区分持久状态和临时计算，可以有效减少推理对隐藏思考的不当依赖，同时保持推理准确性。

Abstract: Reasoning language models do not distinguish tokens used for computation from tokens that constitute persistent state: once generated, all hidden thoughts remain in context and influence future predictions. As a result, downstream reasoning may depend on failed attempts, dead ends, and private scratch work that should not be safely relied on later. We recast this phenomenon as a new training objective, state commitment learning: training models to explicitly distinguish information that should be committed as persistent state from temporary computation that can be discarded. We define a counterfactual criterion, persistent-state sufficiency, which makes it trainable and measurable whether an answer remains usable after hidden thoughts are erased. We then propose Counterfactual Erasure RL (CERL), which evaluates, under the same prefix, both a path that keeps hidden thoughts and a path that erases them, and gives reward only when the erasure path remains correct. We also introduce the Erasure Dependence Protocol and show across mathematics, long-chain logic, scientific QA, and multi-turn tool-use evaluation that CERL substantially reduces answer dependence on hidden thoughts without sacrificing accuracy, consistently outperforming correctness-only RL and long-answer SFT baselines.

</details>


### [19] [Adaptive Oscillatory-State Alignment for Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06010)
*Zhangyao Song,Ziqiong Li,Xiangfei Qiu,Chao Zha,Yinfei Xu,Tao Guo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出AOSNET框架，将周期预测从固定模板匹配转为自适应振荡状态对齐，通过希尔伯特变换提取解析信号描述符，利用描述符条件门控自适应对齐局部状态，在八个基准上达到SOTA或竞争性精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有周期预测方法依赖预定义周期、全局频谱成分或固定可学习模板，无法处理真实时间序列中常见的振幅调制、相位漂移和局部频率变化等非平稳振荡行为，导致固定模板与潜在时间状态根本性失配。

Method: AOSNET框架：1）从观测序列和可学习全局振荡先验中提取希尔伯特解析信号描述符（瞬时振幅、相位、频率）；2）通过描述符条件门控自适应对齐局部状态，选择性保留可靠观测并软性修正失配区域；3）全局先验作为柔性振荡参考而非刚性重复模板。

Result: 在八个基准数据集上达到SOTA或高度竞争性精度，推理速度快。受控合成实验表明，随着非平稳性增强（振幅调制、相位漂移、局部频率变化），振荡状态对齐的优势持续增加。

Conclusion: 从固定模板匹配到自适应振荡状态对齐的范式转变能更有效地建模真实世界中的非平稳周期性时间序列，框架兼具高精度和快速推理能力。

Abstract: Long-term time series forecasting benefits from inductive biases that expose recurring temporal structure. Existing periodic forecasting methods typically model recurrence through predefined periods, global spectral components, or fixed learnable templates. However, real-world temporal dynamics are rarely rigidly periodic: oscillatory behavior often evolves through amplitude modulation, phase drift, and local frequency variation. Under these conditions, fixed-template periodic modeling can become fundamentally mismatched to the underlying temporal states. We propose AOSNET, a Hilbert-guided forecasting framework that reformulates periodic forecasting from fixed template matching to adaptive oscillatory-state alignment. AOSNET extracts analytic-signal descriptors from both the observed sequence and a learnable global oscillatory prior, then adaptively aligns local states through a descriptor-conditioned gate that selectively preserves reliable observations while softly correcting mismatched regions. The learned prior serves not as a rigid repeated template but as a flexible oscillatory reference interpreted through local state dynamics. Experiments on eight benchmarks demonstrate state-of-the-art or highly competitive accuracy with fast inference speed. Controlled synthetic studies isolating amplitude modulation, phase drift, and local frequency variation confirm that the advantage of oscillatory-state alignment consistently increases as non-stationarity intensifies.

</details>


### [20] [Gradient Descent with Large Step Size Restores Symmetry in Deep Linear Networks with Multi-Pathway](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05219)
*Hee-Sung Kim,Sungyoon Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究深度线性网络中的多路径专业化现象，发现：大步长离散梯度下降（GD）与梯度流（GF）预测的"赢家通吃"结果不同。单路径解是尖锐极小值，而多路径分布可降低尖锐度。训练早期出现深度驱动的对称性破缺，但随后在稳定性边缘（Edge of Stability）的振荡会覆盖这一趋势，导致信号重新在多路径间平衡分布，最终大学习率GD倾向于共享表示而非单一路径主导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示离散梯度下降（尤其是大步长设置下）与梯度流在深度线性网络多路径专业化方面的根本差异，解释为何实际训练中网络倾向于共享表示而非单一路径主导。

Method: 理论分析与证明：1）证明单路径解是尖锐极小值，多路径分布可降低尖锐度；2）分析训练早期深度驱动的对称性破缺；3）研究稳定性边缘（Edge of Stability）的振荡如何驱动信号重新平衡分布。

Result: 大步长离散GD产生与GF不同的动力学：早期有深度驱动的对称性破缺，但后期Edge of Stability的振荡会覆盖这一趋势，促使信号在多路径间重新分配，最终形成共享表示。

Conclusion: 深度影响路径竞争机制，大步长GD更倾向于共享表示而非持久性的单一路径主导，这与梯度流预测的"赢家通吃"结果不同。

Abstract: Recent analyses of multi-pathway Deep Linear Networks use Gradient Flow to predict a "winner-takes-all" specialization in which path symmetry breaks and each feature concentrates in a single pathway. In this work, we show that discrete Gradient Descent (GD) with a large step size tells a different story. We prove that single-path solutions are sharp minima, whereas distributing signals across pathways reduces sharpness by a factor that decreases with both the number of pathways and depth. Consequently, while early training reproduces the depth-driven symmetry breaking predicted by GF, oscillations at the Edge of Stability subsequently override this tendency and drive the network into a re-balancing phase, where signals redistribute across pathways. Together, these results clarify how depth shapes pathway competition and explain why large-step GD favors shared representations rather than persistent single-pathway dominance.

</details>


### [21] [Differentiable Efficient Operator Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05232)
*Xiaohuan Pei,Jiyuan Zhang,Yuanfan Guo,Weiguo Feng,Tao Huang,Cho-Jui Hsieh,Chang Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出可微分的Efficient Operator Search框架，将多种手动设计的token缩减算子统一到一个共享算子空间中，并联合搜索缩减位置、保留数量和算子行为，在模态基准测试中实现竞争性的精度-效率权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有高效多模态基础模型依赖手动设计的token缩减算子（剪枝、合并、池化、自适应重加权），这些算子看似不同但缺乏统一视角，且人工设计难以覆盖最优组合。

Method: 提出可微分的Efficient Operator Search框架，统一参数化层激活、保留预算和算子行为三个维度，在单边预算和成本约束下优化任务性能，支持联合搜索缩减位置、保留数量和算子行为。

Result: 该框架能恢复代表性手工设计基线作为特例，并发现超越孤立人工设计的混合算子。在多模态基准测试中，搜索出的算子在精度-效率权衡上表现优异，尤其在激进视觉token缩减场景下。

Conclusion: 高效多模态推理可以从手动算子设计转向可微分算子搜索范式。

Abstract: Efficient multimodal foundation models often rely on manually designed token-reduction operators, such as pruning, merging, pooling, and adaptive reweighting. Although these operators appear different, we show that they can be interpreted as distinct regimes of a shared operator space. Based on this view, we introduce Efficient Operator Search, a differentiable framework that jointly searches where to reduce tokens, how many tokens to retain, and how reduced token information should be processed. The proposed search space parameterizes layer activation, retention budget, and operator behavior, while the search policy optimizes task performance under one-sided budget and cost constraints. This formulation recovers representative hand-designed baselines as special cases and further discovers hybrid operators beyond isolated manual designs. Experiments on multimodal benchmarks show that the searched operators achieve competitive accuracy-efficiency trade-offs, especially under aggressive visual-token reduction. These results suggest that efficient multimodal inference can be reframed from manual operator design to differentiable operator search.

</details>


### [22] [DiffSlack: Learning under Nonlinear Inequality Constraints via Learnable Slack Variables](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05247)
*Ziqian Wang,Chenxi Fang,Zhen Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DiffSlack是一种可微投影层，通过可学习的松弛变量将非线性不等式约束转化为等式约束，并利用阻尼高斯-牛顿投影将网络输出映射到可行域，实现端到端的硬约束满足。在车辆路径规划（含200个非线性不等式约束）任务中，DiffSlack相比基线方法取得了更高的规划成功率和更强的几何约束满足度，并在CARLA和实车实验中验证了轨迹的可执行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在神经网络中强制执行非线性不等式约束具有挑战性，尤其是当输出受大量耦合约束限制时。现有的硬约束方法要么对约束集施加结构限制，要么在大规模非线性问题上引入大量计算开销。

Method: DiffSlack将不等式重写为带可学习松弛变量的等式，松弛变量作为网络增强输出的一部分被预测，为阻尼高斯-牛顿投影提供数据驱动的热启动。投影层将原始预测映射到增广可行流形上，同时保持端到端可微性。两阶段课程学习进一步稳定训练并改善约束满足。

Result: 在包含200个非线性不等式约束（碰撞避免、曲率限制、航点间距）的车辆路径规划任务中，DiffSlack相比现有学习基线方法实现了更高的规划成功率和更强的几何约束满足度，且推理预算相当。消融实验表明硬投影层降低了对监督质量的敏感性。CARLA闭环跟踪和实车实验验证了生成轨迹的可执行性。

Conclusion: DiffSlack为在神经网络中嵌入硬不等式约束提供了一种实用且可扩展的方法，适用于工程应用。

Abstract: Enforcing nonlinear inequality constraints in neural networks remains challenging, especially when the output is subject to many coupled constraints. Existing hard constraint methods often impose structural restrictions on the constraint set or introduce substantial computational overhead for large-scale nonlinear problems. Here, we propose DiffSlack, a differentiable projection layer for nonlinear inequality-constrained neural prediction. DiffSlack reformulates inequalities as equalities with learnable slack variables, which are predicted as part of the augmented network output and provide a data-driven warm start for damped Gauss-Newton projection. The projection layer maps raw predictions onto the augmented feasible manifold while preserving end-to-end differentiability. A two-stage curriculum further stabilizes training and improves constraint satisfaction. We evaluate DiffSlack on vehicle path planning with 200 nonlinear inequality constraints from collision avoidance, curvature limits, and waypoint spacing. Compared with existing learning-based baselines, DiffSlack achieves a higher planning success rate and stronger geometric constraint satisfaction under a comparable inference budget. Ablation studies further show that the hard projection layer reduces sensitivity to supervision quality. Closed-loop tracking in CARLA and real-world vehicle experiments confirms the executability of the generated trajectories. These results demonstrate that DiffSlack provides a practical and scalable approach to embedding hard inequality constraints into neural networks for engineering applications.

</details>


### [23] [Alpha-RTL: Test-Time Training for RTL Hardware Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05253)
*Peilong Zhou,Zhirong Chen,Cangyuan Li,Haoyu Gao,Kaiyan Chang,Ziming Qu,Ying Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出首个针对RTL设计的测试时训练框架TTT-RTL，通过将LLM策略与EDA流程闭环结合，在测试时利用可执行的EDA反馈（语法检查、仿真、综合PPA）进行策略自适应优化，在RTLLM v2.0和工业级C910 FPU单元上分别实现65.1%和59.4%的PPA/ADP降低，显著优于冻结策略的基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LLM生成RTL硬件设计方法中，要么在部署前训练通用RTL生成器，要么在测试时使用冻结策略进行搜索，无法根据具体RTL问题的EDA反馈进行自适应优化，限制了生成设计的物理优化程度。

Method: 提出TTT-RTL框架：1) 采样候选实现并通过语法检查和仿真验证；2) 使用综合推导的PPA乘积对有效设计评分；3) 通过PUCT索引的设计状态池复用高奖励变体；4) 使用熵策略梯度目标更新策略；5) 引入自适应KL预算控制器，根据参考KL、有效样本量和奖励饱和信号调整熵约束以稳定策略更新。

Result: 在RTLLM v2.0（Nangate 45nm）上，几何平均PPA乘积降低65.1%，远超最强冻结策略基线（26.1%）；在工业级XuanTie C910 FPU前导零预测单元（Sky130）上实现59.4%的ADP降低。消融实验验证了策略自适应、状态复用和KL预算控制三个组件的有效性。

Conclusion: 测试时训练结合可执行的EDA反馈能够推动LLM-based RTL生成从功能正确性迈向物理优化，为硬件设计自动化提供了新的范式。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have shown increasing promise in generating
  functionally correct register-transfer-level (RTL) hardware designs.
  Recent systems improve further through EDA-integrated reinforcement
  learning with syntax, simulation, and PPA rewards, but train a general
  RTL generator before deployment while test-time approaches search with
  a frozen policy. We instead perform reinforcement learning at test time,
  allowing the LLM policy to adapt to executable EDA feedback for the
  specific RTL problem at hand. We propose TTT-RTL, to our knowledge the
  first per-design test-time training framework that closes the loop
  between an LLM policy and an EDA pipeline for RTL optimization. TTT-RTL
  samples candidate implementations, verifies them through syntax checking
  and simulation, scores valid designs using synthesis-derived PPA product,
  reuses high-reward variants through a PUCT-indexed design-state pool,
  and updates the policy with an entropic policy-gradient objective. To
  stabilize policy updates under sparse or plateaued rewards, we introduce
  an adaptive KL-budget controller that adjusts the entropy constraint
  using reference KL, effective sample size, and reward saturation signals.
  On RTLLM v2.0 under Nangate 45nm, TTT-RTL reduces the geometric-mean
  PPA product by 65.1% over the reference, outperforming the strongest
  published frozen-policy agent baseline at 26.1%. On an industrial
  XuanTie C910 FPU leading-zero-anticipation unit under Sky130, TTT-RTL
  achieves a 59.4% ADP reduction, and ablations confirm that policy
  adaptation, state reuse, and KL-budget control each contribute. These
  results suggest that test-time training with executable EDA feedback can
  move LLM-based RTL generation beyond functional correctness toward
  physically optimized hardware.

</details>


### [24] [Flash-WAM: Modality-Aware Distillation for World Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05254)
*Arman Akbari,Ci Zhang,Arash Akbari,Lin Zhao,Yixiao Chen,Weiwei Chen,Xuan Zhang,Geng Yuan,Yanzhi Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Flash-WAM是一种模态感知的步骤蒸馏框架，通过为视频和动作流分别设计不同的噪声匹配参数化方法，将世界-动作模型的推理压缩到单步，实现23倍加速（8.1秒→348毫秒），在RoboTwin 2.0上保持85.5%的任务成功率，而朴素一致性蒸馏仅24%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界-动作模型（WAMs）需要数十步去噪推理，代价过高无法支持实时控制；现有的步骤蒸馏方法在联合视频-动作设定中失效，因为视频和动作流使用不同SNR偏移的噪声调度，到达训练时边际噪声分布显著不对称，单模态蒸馏方法无法处理这种不对称性。

Method: 提出Flash-WAM，一种基于一致性蒸馏的模态感知步骤蒸馏框架。通过结构分析一致性函数族在边界条件下的可实现梯度缩放，为每个模态选择匹配其噪声 regime 的一致性函数：动作流低噪声 regime 使用线性梯度缩放参数化，视频流高噪声 regime 使用方差保持参数化。

Result: 在LingBot-VA上实例化后，Flash-WAM将推理压缩到每个模态单步。在RoboTwin 2.0上，每块延迟从8.1秒降至348毫秒（23倍加速），实现实时推理。模拟基准：RoboTwin 2.0成功率85.5%，LIBERO 95.7%。真实世界：Unitree G1人形机器人平均成功率60%，而朴素一致性蒸馏在相同步骤预算下仅24%。

Conclusion: Flash-WAM通过模态感知的步骤蒸馏有效解决了多模态噪声分布不对称问题，在实现大幅加速的同时保持任务成功率，使世界-动作模型具备实时控制能力。

Abstract: World-action models (WAMs) jointly generate future video and robot actions through iterative diffusion, achieving strong performance on manipulation benchmarks but requiring tens of denoising steps, a cost that precludes real-time control. Step distillation has emerged as the natural remedy, but off-the-shelf methods break down in the joint video-action setting because video and action streams use different SNR-shifted noise schedules and reach training with substantially different marginal noise distributions, an asymmetry that single-modality distillation methods cannot accommodate. We introduce \textbf{Flash-WAM}, a modality-aware step-distillation framework inspired by consistency distillation that selects the consistency function for each modality to match its noise regime: a linear-gradient-scaling parametrization for the action stream's low-noise regime, paired with a variance-preserving parametrization for the video stream's high-noise regime, grounded in a structural analysis of the consistency-function family that characterizes the achievable gradient scaling under the consistency boundary condition. Instantiated on LingBot-VA, Flash-WAM compresses inference to a single step in each modality. On RoboTwin 2.0, this reduces per-chunk latency from $8.1$ seconds to $348$ ms on NVIDIA L40S, a $23{\times}$ speedup that enables real-time inference. Flash-WAM preserves task success on simulation benchmarks ($85.5\%$ RoboTwin 2.0, $95.7\%$ LIBERO) and substantially recovers real-world performance ($60\%$ average on a Unitree G1 humanoid robot), while naive consistency distillation drops to $24\%$ at the same step budget.

</details>


### [25] [Scaling Laws for Behavioral Foundation Models over User Event Sequences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05257)
*Rickard Brüel Gabrielsson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了行为基础模型中两阶段架构（特征嵌入器+解码器Transformer）的缩放规律，发现小嵌入器（~2%参数）在计算最优性上更优，且训练目标与评估指标的不一致会随计算预算变化，评估指标本身应被视为缩放规律的一部分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基础模型在推荐、支付、欺诈等行为序列任务中广泛应用，但缺乏类似语言模型缩放规律的计算校准指导，无法确定最优的参数分配、数据分配和训练策略。

Method: 在真实交互数据上进行了约600次实验（训练FLOPs范围10^15-10^19），联合变化四个维度：两阶段参数分配比例、关键批大小、模型/数据分配、冻结嵌入器后的负采样数量。

Result: 小嵌入器（约2%参数）在所有预算下计算最优；低计算预算时数据量相对文本更重，随计算增长D/N趋近Chinchilla启发式；训练目标与排序指标的不一致随计算规模变化；大预算下更多负样本更优，10^19 FLOPs时瓶颈转为候选轴内存。

Conclusion: 行为基础模型中评估指标本身应被视为缩放规律的一部分，改变评估指标可能改变计算最优的配置方案。

Abstract: Foundation models are increasingly trained on sequences of user actions in recommendation, payments, fraud, and commerce, but these models still lack the kind of compute calibration that scaling laws provide for language models. We study a common two-part behavioral-model architecture: a feature-based event embedder maps each multi-modal item to a vector, and a decoder-only transformer predicts the next event from the resulting sequence. Across roughly 600 runs on real interaction data, spanning $10^{15}$-$10^{19}$ training FLOPs, we jointly vary four deployment-relevant axes: the two-part parameter split, critical batch size, model/data allocation, and the number of sampled negatives used after freezing the embedder. A small embedder ($s^{\star}\!\approx\!2\%$ of parameters) is compute-optimal at every budget we test because embedder parameters are both more expensive per step and exposed to far more repeated items than contextualizer parameters. Compute-optimal training is data-heavy relative to text at low compute, but its $D/N$ ratio moves toward the Chinchilla heuristic as compute increases. The sampled training objective and deployed ranking metrics disagree in ways that themselves scale: critical batch size, optimal negative count after freezing, and the agreement between loss and ranking quality all shift with compute and with the chosen evaluation metric. For negative sampling, larger budgets increasingly prefer more negatives; by $10^{19}$ FLOPs the active constraint is candidate-axis memory rather than FLOPs. In behavioral foundation models, the evaluation metric is therefore part of the scaling law: changing it can change the compute-optimal recipe.

</details>


### [26] [Policy-Conditioned Counterfactual Credit for Verifiable Reinforcement Learning of Long-Horizon Language Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05263)
*Renwei Meng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CVT-RL算法，通过反事实贡献估计、干预有效性门控和可验证约束，在长程语言智能体任务中将成功率提升至78.9%，证据F1提升至82.8，黑客行为从7.2%降至3.9%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于可验证奖励的强化学习方法中，长程语言智能体仍存在学习无证据支撑的推理链、信念漂移以及仅满足终端检查的捷径行为等问题。现有的过程奖励大多基于相关性而非因果性，无法估计步骤在指定干预下是否真正贡献于最终的验证成功。

Method: 提出CVT-RL算法，包含三个核心组件：1）密集可验证奖励；2）干预有效性门控机制；3）基于策略条件的反事实贡献（PCCC）估计器。采用删除、语义替换、证据替换和工具输出扰动四种受控干预，使用冻结参考策略采样延续序列，并引入选择调整的双重鲁棒估计器增强优势函数。信念控制仅使用前缀可观测标签，通过增广拉格朗日方法约束无支撑声明、跳过验证、工具篡改和危险调用。

Result: 在长上下文QA、ALFWorld、ScienceWorld和web/tool任务上，CVT-RL将平均任务成功率从非因果RL的71.8%和信息匹配的反事实过程基线的75.4%提升至78.9%；证据F1从信息匹配基线的78.9提升至82.8；黑客行为从7.2%降至3.9%。独立人工审计估计CVT-RL的黑客行为为4.6%（基线8.1%），自适应检测规避攻击仅使黑客率升至7.1%。分层bootstrap和混合效应检验在Holm校正后p<0.01。

Conclusion: 精心设计的反事实信用分配，配合有效性门控、诊断机制和可验证约束，为更可靠的长程语言智能体强化学习提供了一条可复现的路径。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards improves reasoning and tool use, yet long-horizon language agents still learn unsupported evidence chains, belief drift, and shortcut actions that satisfy terminal checks. Existing process rewards are mostly correlational: they reward retrieval-, reflection-, or verification-like steps without estimating whether the step contributes to final verified success under a specified intervention. We propose CVT-RL, a constrained policy-gradient algorithm with dense verifiable rewards, intervention-validity gating, and a policy-conditioned counterfactual contribution (PCCC) estimator. Deletion, semantic substitution, evidence substitution, and tool-output perturbation define separate controlled interventions; continuations are sampled from a frozen reference policy, and a selection-adjusted doubly robust estimator augments the advantage. Belief control uses only prefix-observable labels, while an augmented Lagrangian constrains unsupported claims, skipped verification, tool tampering, and unsafe calls. On long-context QA, ALFWorld, ScienceWorld, and web/tool tasks, CVT-RL improves average task success from 71.8% for compute-matched non-causal RL and 75.4% for an information-matched counterfactual-process baseline to 78.9%, improves evidence F1 from 78.9 to 82.8 over the information-matched baseline, and reduces measured hacking from 7.2% to 3.9%. Independent human audit estimates 4.6% hacking for CVT-RL versus 8.1% for the information-matched baseline, and adaptive detector-evasion attacks raise hacking only to 7.1%. Stratified bootstrap and mixed-effects tests give p<0.01 after Holm correction for all primary metrics. Carefully scoped counterfactual credit, paired with validity gating, diagnostics, and verifiable constraints, provides a reproducible route toward more reliable long-horizon RL for language agents.

</details>


### [27] [REGEN: Reference-Guided Synthetic Multivariate Time Series Generation for Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05264)
*Moulik Gupta,Dhruv Kumar,Murari Mandal,Saurabh Deshpande*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ReGeN是一种参考引导的时间序列生成框架，将观测序列分解为周期骨架、随机残差和跨变量依赖三个可解释组件，通过可控采样生成多样化且保留领域结构的合成数据，在低数据场景下能有效替代真实数据，甚至在某些领域超越真实数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多变量时间序列预测需要大规模多样化的训练数据，但现实领域往往只能提供少量观测序列。现有生成方法要么产生领域无关的样本（如CauKer、TimePFN），要么将参考数据视为黑盒监督（如TimeGAN），缺乏对周期性结构、局部变异和跨变量动态的显式控制。

Method: ReGeN将观测序列视为结构支架而非模仿对象，将其分解为三个可解释组件：(1) 相位对齐的周期骨干，捕获领域主导形态；(2) 基于深度核高斯过程的逐变量随机残差；(3) 通过结构因果模型注入的时滞感知跨变量依赖。通过可控温度采样这些组件来拓宽分布覆盖范围，同时保留领域接地结构。

Result: ReGeN生成的数据在最小化预测性能下降的前提下持续替代真实同源数据；在强周期性领域（如交通）甚至优于真实数据本身。基于ReGeN语料库预训练的基模型在性能上优于使用基于先验和数据驱动合成替代方案预训练的模型。

Conclusion: 在低数据场景下，如何结构化地利用参考数据与拥有多少数据同样重要。

Abstract: Training robust multivariate time series forecasting models requires large, diverse corpora, yet many real-world domains provide only a handful of observed sequences. Existing generators fail to resolve this mismatch: prior-based approaches (e.g., CauKer, TimePFN) produce domain-agnostic samples, while data-driven methods (e.g., TimeGAN) treat references as black-box supervision, forfeiting explicit control over periodic structure, local variability, and cross-variable dynamics.
  We propose ReGeN, a reference-guided generative pipeline that treats observed sequences not as examples to imitate, but as structural scaffolds for controllable synthesis. ReGeN decomposes each reference into three interpretable components: a phase-aligned periodic backbone capturing dominant domain morphology; per-variable stochastic residuals modeled with a deep-kernel Gaussian process; and lag-aware cross-variable dependencies injected through a structural causal model with fitted coupling coefficients. Sampling these components at controllable temperature broadens distributional coverage while preserving domain-grounded structure.
  We show that ReGeN-generated data consistently substitutes for real sibling data with minimal forecasting degradation, and in strongly periodic domains such as traffic, can outperform the real source itself. We further show that a foundation model pretrained on ReGeN corpora outperforms those pretrained on prior-based and data-driven synthetic alternatives. This suggests that in low-data regimes, how reference data is structurally exploited can matter as much as how much data is available.

</details>


### [28] [Data-efficient flood depth prediction through domain-aware coreset selection and tabular foundation models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05265)
*Lipai Huang,Adithi Srinath,Manas Singh,Junwei Ma,Ali Mostafavi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种领域感知的核心集构建流程，通过条件化表格基础模型，仅需0.7%的训练数据即可实现跨流域的洪水深度预测，R²达到监督参考的98.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有监督替代模型虽然精度高，但每个流域需要数百万训练样本且无法外推到不同网格结构，亟需一种数据高效、可跨流域迁移的洪水预测方法。

Method: 构建领域感知的核心集（coreset）构建流程：按重现期和受影响最严重流域对风暴分层，然后通过目标感知的空间选择器采样六边形单元；在推理时条件化表格基础模型。

Result: 仅用0.7%的每流域训练数据，在休斯顿地区9个流域上平均R²达0.663（监督参考R²=0.673为98.5%）；无需任务特定重训练即可迁移到未见流域；在远分布外实时风暴上超越监督参考。

Conclusion: 领域感知的核心集构建使表格基础模型能够实现数据高效、跨流域可迁移的洪水预测，无需每个流域单独训练。

Abstract: Near-real-time flood depth prediction demands surrogate models that are accurate, fast, and transferable across watersheds. Supervised surrogates can match physics-based simulators in accuracy but need millions of training rows per watershed and cannot extrapolate beyond their original mesh. We propose a domain-aware coreset construction pipeline that conditions a tabular foundation model at inference time. The pipeline stratifies storms by return period and most-affected watershed, then samples hexagons with a target-aware spatial selector. With 0.7% of the per-watershed training pool, the model attains a mean $R^2$ of 0.663 across nine Houston-area watersheds, within 98.5% of the supervised reference ($R^2$ = 0.673). It transfers to held-out watersheds without task-specific retraining, staying ahead of a coreset-trained supervised baseline. On real storms it exceeds the supervised reference on a far out-of-distribution case and trails it on a mostly in-distribution one. Domain-aware coreset construction lets tabular foundation models deliver data-efficient, watershed-transferable flood predictions without per-watershed training.

</details>


### [29] [Sharp Low-Degree Thresholds for Planted-vs-Planted Testing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05266)
*Anda Skeja,Daniel Gutiérrez Espinoza,Fiona Skerman,Alexander S. Wein*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次建立了 planted-vs-planted 设置下低次多项式检验的尖锐阈值，证明了 planted submatrix 和 planted dense subgraph 模型中计数社区的匹配低次上下界，发现检验阈值与已知的低次恢复阈值在精确常数上一致，而弱检验则呈现平滑转变而非尖锐阈值，并开发了基于潜变量展开的框架来识别和修剪非信号贡献。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在 planted-vs-planted 设置中，目标是以渐近消失的错误率判断两个结构化 planted 机制中哪一个生成了观测数据。此前缺乏对该设置下低次多项式检验的尖锐阈值的理论刻画。

Method: 开发了一个 planted-vs-planted 检验框架，构建于源自低次恢复的潜变量展开之上，并采用新方法来识别和修剪非信号贡献，从而推导出匹配的低次上下界。

Result: 在 planted submatrix 和 planted dense subgraph 模型中，低次多项式检验的阈值与已知的低次恢复阈值在精确常数上一致；而弱检验则呈现平滑转变而非尖锐阈值。

Conclusion: 本文首次建立了 planted-vs-planted 设置下低次多项式检验的尖锐阈值，揭示了检验与恢复任务之间的内在联系，并为区分两个 planted 结构的假设检验问题提供了精确的理论刻画。

Abstract: We establish the first sharp thresholds for low-degree polynomial tests in planted-vs-planted settings, where the goal is to determine with vanishing error which of two structured planted mechanisms generated the observed data. We prove matching low-degree upper and lower bounds for counting communities in the planted submatrix and planted dense subgraph models. The resulting testing threshold coincides, down to the sharp constant, with the known low-degree recovery threshold. In contrast, the task of weak testing, where the goal is to outperform random guessing, does not have a sharp threshold but rather a smooth transition, which we identify. To prove our results, we develop a framework for planted-vs-planted testing that builds on a latent-variable expansion originating in low-degree recovery and employs new methods to identify and prune non-signal contributions.

</details>


### [30] [Learning Manifold and Itô Dynamics with Branched Neural Rough Differential Equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05272)
*Luke Thompson,Dai Shi,Lequan Lin,Junbin Gao,Andi Han*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出分支神经粗糙微分方程（B-NRDEs），通过Hopf代数框架将NRDE的log-ODE步重写为流形上的几何数值积分，匹配驱动代数与相应微积分（Grossman-Larson树用于Itô动力学、Munthe-Kaas-Wright平面树用于流形上有序协变导数、shuffle代数用于Stratonovich情形），并引入分支签名核目标以在训练中暴露二次变分项，从而在非欧几里得- Stratonovich场景下实现高效且精确的随机与流形值动力学建模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准神经粗糙微分方程（NRDE）依赖于shuffle代数（Stratonovich微积分的代数对应），因此无法处理Itô动力学所需的二次变分项，也无法处理流形上Itô流所需的有序协变导数，限制了其在更广泛随机和流形值动力学场景中的应用。

Method: 提出B-NRDEs框架：1) 将NRDE的log-ODE步重写为流形上的几何数值积分，根据驱动微积分类型匹配相应的Hopf代数结构（Grossman-Larson树、Munthe-Kaas-Wright平面树、shuffle代数）；2) 引入分支签名核目标，使二次变分项在训练中可见，实现Itô一致的法律匹配。

Result: 在粗糙Bergomi波动率、仿真到真实SO(3)动力学预测、SPD协方差动力学三个任务上，B-NRDEs相比标准NRDEs展现出更优的性能，能够有效处理Itô动力学和流形约束。

Conclusion: B-NRDEs提供了一个统一的、有效的框架，将神经粗糙微分方程扩展到欧几里得-Stratonovich设置之外，适用于更广泛的随机和流形值动力学系统。

Abstract: Neural rough differential equations (NRDEs) stay accurate under irregular sampling while taking far fewer integration steps than standard neural differential equations, summarising a finely sampled driver by its log-signature and advancing the hidden state over coarse intervals using the log-ODE method. This efficiency rests on the shuffle algebra, the algebraic counterpart of Stratonovich calculus. This reliance means NRDEs cannot expose the quadratic-variation terms Itô dynamics require, nor the ordered covariant derivatives that govern Itô flows on connection-equipped manifolds. Ameliorating this, we introduce Branched Neural Rough Differential Equations (B-NRDEs), a Hopf-algebraic framework that recasts the NRDE log-ODE step as geometric numerical integration on the state-space manifold, matching the driving algebra to the governing calculus: Grossman--Larson rooted trees for Euclidean Itô dynamics, Munthe-Kaas--Wright planar rooted trees for ordered covariant derivatives on manifolds, and the shuffle algebra in the classical Stratonovich case. This yields intrinsic coarse-step dynamics that exactly preserve manifold constraints. Finally, we introduce a branched signature-kernel objective to enable Itô-consistent law matching by making quadratic-variation terms visible during training. On rough Bergomi volatility, sim-to-real $\mathrm{SO}(3)$ dynamics forecasting, and SPD covariance dynamics, B-NRDEs offer a unified, effective approach to stochastic and manifold-valued dynamics beyond the Euclidean--Stratonovich setting.

</details>


### [31] [Anomaly Detection for Electro-Hydrostatic Actuators using LSTM Autoencoder](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05274)
*Nehal Afifi,Abdelmonem Elhendawi,Felix Leitenberger,Nadine Piat,Sven Matthiesen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于LSTM自编码器的离线异常检测框架，用于电静液作动器（EHA）单变量传感器信号（温度和压力），在故障注入测试中达到99.0%的平均准确率、最高100%的精确率、90.2%-99.6%的召回率和93.1%-99.8%的F1分数，显著优于传统统计和机器学习方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EHA系统在航空航天和工业领域广泛应用，其传感器数据量大、采样频率高，传统统计方法和经典机器学习方法（如Z-score、IQR、MAD、孤立森林、高斯混合模型、k-means等）无法有效捕捉EHA信号中的时序依赖关系，导致检测精度低、误报率高；同时，针对EHA系统的数据驱动异常检测方法缺乏系统性评估。

Method: 采用基于重构的LSTM自编码器进行离线异常检测，通过验证集的重构误差分布进行校准和评估；在受控实验台上收集EHA温度和压力数据，注入多种故障场景，使用准确率、精确率、召回率和F1分数评估性能，并进行不同工况下的敏感性分析。

Result: LSTM自编码器在所有传感器上平均准确率99.0%，精确率最高达100%，召回率在90.2%-99.6%之间，F1分数在93.1%-99.8%之间，显示出高检测灵敏度和极低的误报率。

Conclusion: 数据驱动的离线异常检测在EHA系统中是可行的，未来工作将聚焦于将本框架适配到在线（实时）环境中。

Abstract: Electro-Hydrostatic Actuators (EHAs) are widely used in aerospace and industrial systems, where timely detection of sensor anomalies is essential to ensure safe and reliable operation. However, the large volume and high sampling frequency of EHA sensor data pose challenges for accurate and efficient anomaly detection. Conventional statistical and classical machine-learning methods such as Z-score, Interquartile Range (IQR), Median Absolute Deviation (MAD), Isolation Forest, Gaussian Mixture, and k-means often fail to capture the temporal dependencies inherent in EHA signals, resulting in limited detection accuracy and elevated false-alarm rates. Furthermore, systematic evaluations of data-driven anomaly detection approaches for EHA systems remain scarce, particularly under varying operational conditions. This study presents an offline anomaly-detection framework for univariate EHA sensor signals, focusing on temperature and pressure data collected from a controlled test bench. The method employs a reconstruction-based Long Short-Term Memory (LSTM) autoencoder, calibrated and evaluated using validation-set reconstruction-error distributions. Performance is assessed across multiple fault-injection scenarios using accuracy, precision, recall, and F1-score, complemented by sensitivity analyses under varying operating conditions. The LSTM autoencoder achieved an average accuracy of 99.0\%, precision up to 100\%, recall between 90.2\% and 99.6\%, and F1-scores from 93.1\% to 99.8\%, demonstrating high detection sensitivity and a very low false-alarm rate across all evaluated sensors. These results highlight the feasibility of data-driven offline anomaly detection for EHAs. Future work will focus on adapting the developed framework for an online (real-time) environment.

</details>


### [32] [Agentic Monte Carlo: Simulating Reinforcement Learning for Black-Box Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05296)
*Dae Yon Hwang,Raunaq Suri,Valentin Villecroze,Anthony L. Caterini,Jesse C. Cresswell,Noël Vouitsis,Brendan Leigh Ross*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Agentic Monte Carlo (AMC)方法，通过将强化学习与贝叶斯推断等价，利用序贯蒙特卡洛从最优策略的后验分布中采样，实现对黑盒LLM智能体的策略优化，无需修改模型参数。在AgentGym基准测试中，AMC显著优于提示基线，并在测试时计算扩展后超越GRPO。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体分为两类：可进行参数级强化学习优化的开源智能体，以及仅能通过API访问的黑盒智能体。黑盒智能体虽常基于更强大的闭源LLM，但API限制使其无法应用参数级RL方法。为此，需要一种无需修改模型参数即可实现RL风格优化的方法。

Method: 利用强化学习与贝叶斯推断之间的已知等价性，将最优策略视为轨迹的后验分布，以固定黑盒LLM智能体为先验。采用序贯蒙特卡洛方法，通过学习一个值函数来引导智能体行为，从而从该后验分布中采样，不改变底层黑盒模型。

Result: 在AgentGym基准测试的三个不同环境中验证，AMC显著优于提示基线，并在测试时计算扩展后，性能超越Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO)。

Conclusion: AMC证明了对黑盒LLM智能体进行原则性的RL风格优化的可行性，为仅能通过API访问的闭源模型提供了有效的策略优化途径。

Abstract: LLM agents operate in two distinct regimes: open-weight agents amenable to reinforcement learning (RL) and black-box agents whose behaviour must be controlled purely at test time. Although black-box agents are often backed by state-of-the-art proprietary LLMs, API-only access precludes parameter-level optimization, rendering most RL methods inapplicable. To address this limitation, we turn to a known equivalence between RL and Bayesian inference. We propose Agentic Monte Carlo (AMC) to directly sample from the optimal policy of a black-box agent rather than training it through RL. The optimal policy is a posterior over trajectories whose prior we define as the fixed black-box LLM agent. We employ Sequential Monte Carlo to sample from this posterior by learning a value function to steer the agent while leaving the underlying black-box model unchanged. We validate AMC on three diverse environments from the AgentGym benchmark, demonstrating significant improvements over prompting baselines and even outperforming Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) as we scale the test-time compute of our method. AMC demonstrates the feasibility of performing principled RL-style optimization of black-box LLM agents. Code is available at https://github.com/layer6ai-labs/Agentic-Monte-Carlo

</details>


### [33] [Statistically Reliable LLM-Based Ranking Evaluation via Prediction-Powered Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05308)
*Abhishek Divekar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PRECISE 扩展了 Prediction-Powered Inference (PPI) 框架，通过结合少量人工标注和大量 LLM 评判来生成排名评估指标的无偏估计，并在 Precision@K 等层次化指标上实现计算可行，在 ESCI 基准和实际生产系统中均验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上，使用 LLM 作为评判器评估排名系统存在偏差问题，而完全依赖人工标注成本高昂。需要一种既能利用 LLM 大规模评判能力、又能保证估计无偏且统计高效的方法，特别是针对 Precision@K 这类层次化指标（标注在文档级别，指标在查询级别）。

Method: 基于 Prediction-Powered Inference (PPI) 框架，将少量人工标注与大量 LLM 评判结合，产生无偏的偏差校正估计。针对 Precision@K 等层次化指标，将输出空间计算复杂度从 O(2^|C|) 降低到 O(2^K)，其中 C 是候选文档集，K 是截断阈值。

Result: 在 ESCI 基准上，用 30 个人工标注加上 Claude 3 Sonnet 评判，使 Precision@4 的标准误差从 4.45 降至 3.50（相对降低 21%）。在生产系统中，仅用 100 个人工标签和 2 小时专家标注，正确识别出三个系统变体中的最佳方案，A/B 测试证实该排名带来日均销售额 +407 bps 的提升。

Conclusion: PRECISE 框架能够有效结合人工标注与 LLM 评判，在保证无偏估计的同时显著降低标准误差，并且在实际生产环境中具有实用价值，能够以极低的标注成本准确识别最优系统。

Abstract: With PRECISE, we extended Prediction-Powered Inference to produce bias-corrected estimates of ranking evaluation metrics by combining a small human-labeled set with a large LLM-judged set. PPI is provably unbiased regardless of the LLM judge's error profile. We make it applicable to hierarchical metrics like Precision@K, where annotations are per-document but the metric is per-query, by reducing the output-space computation from O(2^|C|) to O(2^K). On the ESCI benchmark, augmenting 30 human annotations with Claude 3 Sonnet judgments reduces the standard error of Precision@4 estimates from 4.45 to 3.50 (a 21% relative reduction). In a production system, our framework correctly identified the best of three system variants from 100 human labels and 2 hours of domain-expert annotation; A/B testing confirmed this ranking with +407 bps in daily sales.

</details>


### [34] [Multimarginal flow matching with optimal transport potentials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05327)
*Raghav Kansal,David Crair,Nghia Nguyen,Scott Pope,Bradley Parry*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出OTP-FM算法，通过结合流匹配与动态最优传输中的势函数项，利用中间观测边际分布约束端点之间的传输流，实现高效、无模拟的多边际流匹配，在单细胞RNA测序、海洋学和气象数据集上取得最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有流匹配方法主要关注两个端点分布之间的传输，但在许多科学领域中存在可采样的中间观测边际分布（如时间序列数据），这些中间分布有助于约束端点间的传输流。该问题尚未被充分探索。

Method: 利用流匹配与动态最优传输之间的联系，通过在动态最优传输作用量中引入势函数项来软约束流经过中间边际分布。将条件流匹配学习目标扩展以纳入这些势函数，推导出高效、无模拟的多边际流匹配算法（OTP-FM）。

Result: 在单细胞RNA测序、海洋学和气象学三个不同领域的多样化数据集上，OTP-FM达到了最先进的性能和训练效率。

Conclusion: OTP-FM通过势函数约束中间边际分布的方法，为多边际流匹配提供了灵活高效的解决方案，在科学领域的时间序列分布建模中具有广泛应用前景。

Abstract: Flow matching (FM) has emerged as a powerful framework for learning dynamic transport maps between two empirical distributions. However, less explored is the setting with intermediate observed marginals that can help constrain the flows between the endpoints. This "multimarginal" regime is central to modeling temporal evolution in dynamical systems in many scientific domains that can sample sequential distributions. We tackle this problem with a novel approach that leverages the connection between FM and dynamic optimal transport (OT), softly steering the flow towards the intermediate marginals through potential terms in the dynamic OT action. By extending the conditional FM learning target to incorporate these potentials, we derive an efficient, simulation-free algorithm for multimarginal FM that offers considerable flexibility in the spatiotemporal dynamics of the learned flows. We demonstrate state-of-the-art performance and training efficiency of OT-potential FM (OTP-FM) on diverse single-cell RNA sequencing, oceanographic, and meteorological datasets. Our code is available at https://github.com/Bexorg-Inc/OTP-FM.

</details>


### [35] [A prism hierarchy of learning regimes in large linear autoencoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05335)
*Eugene Golikov,Yaroslav Gusev,Dmitry Yarotsky*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统性地识别了大型权重捆绑线性自编码器在梯度流下的五种极端学习机制（大数据、小数据、平均场、窄潜在和自由），这些机制对应于三棱柱的2-面，并推导了前四种机制的显式损失演化表达式，与实验结果高度吻合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习模型的理论研究通常只考虑个别极限机制，缺乏对不同极端学习机制的系统性刻画。本文旨在为特定类型模型（大型权重捆绑线性自编码器）提供一幅完整的不同极端学习机制的图像。

Method: 利用形式化损失展开层次结构，建立极端机制与三棱柱面的自然对应关系。针对前四种机制，推导了训练损失和群体损失在梯度流下的显式演化表达式。

Result: 成功识别了五种极端机制（大数据、小数据、平均场、窄潜在、自由），并对前四种机制获得了与实验高度吻合的理论损失演化表达式。

Conclusion: 该工作为理解权重捆绑线性自编码器的学习动力学提供了系统性的理论框架，揭示了不同极端机制间的相互关系，且理论预测与实验一致。

Abstract: Theoretical studies of machine learning models commonly consider different limiting regimes in which the learning dynamics of gradient descent becomes theoretically tractable. It is, however, desirable to have a systematically obtained picture of all qualitatively different extreme learning regimes for a particular type of models. In this paper we propose such a picture for large weight-tied linear autoencoders characterized by input and latent dimensions, initialization magnitude, and training set size. This model is nonlinear in the weights and its gradient flow does not have a general theoretical solution. We show that at the level of the formal loss-expansion hierarchy, its extreme regimes are naturally associated with faces of a triangular prism. In particular, there are five basic extreme regimes associated with the 2-faces of the prism: (1) large-data, (2) small-data, (3) mean-field, (4) narrow-latent, and (5) free. For regimes (1,2,3,4), we derive explicit expressions for both train and population limiting loss evolutions under gradient flow, obtaining very good agreement with experimental results.

</details>


### [36] [PJ-RoPE: A Fourier-Jet-Affine Position Space for Relative Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05345)
*Yaobo Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PJ-RoPE 统一了 RoPE、Jordan-RoPE 和 ALiBi 的相对位置编码方法，提出了一个可学习的傅里叶-喷流-仿射位置空间框架，并通过不同任务验证了不同区域的选择特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的相对位置编码方法（RoPE、Jordan-RoPE、ALiBi）各有其数学结构和适用场景，但缺乏统一的视角来理解它们之间的内在联系以及不同任务对位置编码空间的选择偏好。

Method: 将 RoPE 的傅里叶相位、Jordan-RoPE 的有限喷流和 ALiBi 的仿射近因性统一到一个可学习的相对位置空间中；引入标量 PJ-偏置核与精确 PJ-旋转特征变换的分离机制；提出自适应扇区诊断工具；使用 LC/快度坐标稳定高阶喷流。

Result: 控制探针验证了扇区包含性和选择性；小语言模型运行显示出仿射/近因边界；音乐令牌流中 LC/仿射变体保持强性能并携带可测量的高阶修正；LC 诊断显示尺度稳定性增益伴随相位分辨率损失。

Conclusion: PJ-RoPE 提供了一个统一的框架来解释和选择不同任务所需的相对位置编码结构，并通过实验证明了该框架的有效性和可解释性。

Abstract: We unify RoPE's Fourier phase, Jordan-RoPE's finite jets, and ALiBi's affine recency into a single learnable relative-position space, and study which regions of this space are selected by different tasks. PJ-RoPE is a Fourier-Jet-Affine formulation for relative attention, with an optional Poincare-type reading as the affine completion of a homogeneous Fourier-jet positional representation. Algebraically, the same primitives form a finite constant-coefficient difference module: simple roots of the lag-shift operator give Fourier/RoPE characters, repeated nonzero roots give Jordan/Fourier jets, and the repeated unit root gives ALiBi-like affine recency.
  The framework separates scalar PJ-bias kernels from exact PJ-rotary feature transforms, introduces adaptive sector diagnostics, and uses LC/rapidity coordinates to stabilize high-order jets. Controlled probes verify sector containment and selection; small language runs expose an affine/recency boundary; music-token streams provide the clearest case where LC/affine variants remain strong while carrying measurable high-order corrections; and LC diagnostics show a scale-stability gain coupled to phase-resolution loss.

</details>


### [37] [Mamba-Assisted Non-Markovian Closure for Reduced-Order Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05371)
*Zhi-Feng Wei,Saad Qadeer,Panos Stinis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Mamba-Assisted Closure (MAC)框架，利用Mamba序列模型对降阶模型中的非马尔可夫闭包项进行建模，通过双表示（卷积训练+递归推理）实现高效长轨迹训练和恒定步长推理成本，在Burgers方程和Lorenz '96系统上优于GRU和Wilks方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维动力系统的降阶建模常受限于非马尔可夫闭包项（未解析变量对已解析变量的影响），传统马尔可夫近似精度不足，需要更有效的闭包建模方法。

Method: 借鉴Mori-Zwanzig形式论将闭包项表示为已解析轨迹的泛函，将其转化为序列建模问题；采用Mamba状态空间模型，训练时使用卷积形式进行序列到序列学习，推理时切换为递归形式实现自回归 rollout，与降阶控制方程通过数值积分器耦合。

Result: 在粘性Burgers方程和混沌双尺度Lorenz '96系统上，MAC模型在预测精度和长时间rollout稳定性上显著优于马尔可夫降阶模型、GRU序列模型和Wilks方法。

Conclusion: MAC框架利用Mamba的双表示特性，有效解决了降阶模型中非马尔可夫闭包项的建模难题，兼具高效训练和低推理成本，为复杂动力系统降阶建模提供了新范式。
