We show that fixing the initial flow distribution in a minimum-flow GFlowNet reduces its objective to a Kantorovich OT problem with graph-induced shortest path costs. At the optimum, the learned GFlowNet policy therefore encodes an optimal transport plan from the source distribution to the target distribution: we show that sampling trajectories from the minimum-flow GFlowNet recovers the corresponding optimal coupling. Our formulation enables applying the GFlowNet learning framework to OT problems on large graphs via edge flows and neural parameterization. Experiments confirm agreement with exact OT solvers and demonstrate that GFlowNets can learn high-quality transport plans.

</details>


### [112] [PAC-Bayesian Adversarially Robust Generalization for Message Passing Graph Neural Networks: A Sensitivity Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06293)
*Ziling Liang,Xinping Yi,Qingsong Wen,Shi Jin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文扩展了基于敏感性的PAC-Bayesian框架到消息传递图神经网络，通过利用输出雅可比矩阵的低秩性质构造各向异性高斯后验，推导出更紧的对抗鲁棒泛化界，将主导维度因子从隐藏层宽度缩减为类别数K。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对抗鲁棒泛化分析依赖于各向同性高斯后验和全参数空间权重扰动控制，无法捕捉异构参数敏感性且受隐藏层宽度依赖的复杂度项影响，导致泛化界不够紧。

Method: 1) 推导不同参数块相对于网络输出的雅可比矩阵；2) 利用K类图分类中雅可比矩阵秩至多为K的性质，构造雅可比对齐的敏感性矩阵；3) 使用优化协方差的各向异性高斯后验紧致上界KL散度；4) 将谱范数依赖从隐藏层宽度缩减为类别数K。

Result: 得到了比现有分析更紧的MPGNN对抗鲁棒泛化保证，主导维度因子从隐藏宽度相关的项缩减为类别数K。

Conclusion: 所提分析框架可为MPGNN的设计提供指导，以增强对抗鲁棒性。

Abstract: Whilst the vulnerability of graph neural networks (GNNs) to adversarial attacks poses a critical threat to graph representation learning, the understanding of the robust generalization behavior remains a fundamental challenge in the adversarial setting. Recently, PAC-Bayesian margin-based generalization analysis substantially advances this line of research by providing a flexible and data-dependent analytical framework. However, existing robust analyses often rely on isotropic Gaussian posteriors and control weight perturbations in the full parameter space, which limits the ability to capture heterogeneous parameter sensitivity yet hinges on hidden-width-dependent complexity terms, resulting in not-tight-enough generalization bounds. In this paper, we extend a recently proposed sensitivity-aware PAC-Bayesian framework from deep neural networks to message passing GNNs (MPGNNs) and derive a tighter robust generalization bound in the adversarial setting. Specifically, we first quantify how sensitive the perturbations across different parameter blocks are to the network outputs by deriving the output Jacobians with respect to the weight parameters. Exploiting the fact that these Jacobian matrices have rank at most $K$ in $K$-class graph classification, we then construct Jacobian-aligned sensitivity matrices and use anisotropic Gaussian posteriors with optimized covariances to upper bound the KL divergence in a tight way. Notably, by refining the spectral-norm dependence on the learned weights and reducing the leading dimension factor from hidden-width-dependent terms to the number of classes $K$, our analysis yields much tighter robust generalization guarantees for MPGNNs, thereby guiding their designs to enhance adversarial robustness.

</details>


### [113] [Reactive Flux Matching: Mechanism Discovery and Adaptive Sampling of Rare Events](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06295)
*Rishal Aggarwal,David Ryan Koes,Nicholas M. Boffi,Eric Vanden-Eijnden*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Flux Matching框架，从反应轨迹数据中学习电流速度u(z)和标量势h(z)，分别用于刻画反应路径和作为反应坐标，该方法无需动力学或平稳分布先验知识，在非马尔可夫投影下仍保持良好定义，并可用于改进增强采样。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 路径采样方法能生成连接亚稳态的反应轨迹系综，但从这些数据中提取机制性见解仍然困难，现有方法（如基于committor的方法）在投影到非马尔可夫集体变量时存在局限性。

Method: Flux Matching框架通过最小化反应路径系综上的二次泛函（类比生成模型中的流匹配损失），学习两个互补对象：电流速度u(z)（流线刻画主导反应路径）和标量势h(z)（通过对反应电流进行加权亥姆霍兹-霍奇分解得到，作为数据驱动的反应坐标）。该方法无需底层动力学或平稳分布知识。

Result: 在分子系统上验证了该方法能够生成电流速度轨迹并计算速率常数，且u和h在投影到非马尔可夫集体变量后仍保持良好定义，其水平集可为增强采样方法提供自适应界面。

Conclusion: Flux Matching为从反应轨迹数据中提取机制信息提供了一种新框架，相比committor-based方法具有更好的投影鲁棒性，并能与增强采样方法结合使用。

Abstract: Path sampling methods generate ensembles of reactive trajectories connecting metastable states, but extracting mechanistic insight from these data remains nontrivial. We introduce Flux Matching, a framework that learns two complementary objects directly from reactive trajectory data: a current velocity $u(z)$, whose streamlines trace the dominant reaction pathways, and a scalar potential $h(z)$, obtained from a weighted Helmholtz-Hodge decomposition of the reactive current, that serves as a data-driven reaction coordinate. Both minimize quadratic functionals over the reactive path ensemble, analogous to the flow matching loss in generative modeling, and require no knowledge of the underlying dynamics or stationary distribution. Unlike committor-based methods, $u$ and $h$ remain well-defined under projection onto non-Markovian collective variables, and their level sets in turn provide adaptive interfaces for improved sampling with enhanced sampling methods. Flux Matching is validated through the generation of current velocity trajectories and rate constant calculations on molecular systems.

</details>


### [114] [Plug-and-Play Guidance for Discrete Diffusion Models via Gradient-Informed Logit Correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06303)
*Hongkun Dou,Zike Chen,Fengji Li,Hongjue Li,Yue Deng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出GILC，一个即插即用的框架，通过将预训练去噪网络作为变分代理来高效估计引导信号，并引入无雅可比机制直接修正干净预测logits，实现离散扩散模型的可控生成，在DNA、蛋白质序列和分子生成任务上达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离散扩散模型的可控生成面临计算开销大或需要重新训练的问题，且高维离散空间中梯度不稳定。

Method: 提出GILC框架：(1)将预训练去噪网络作为变分代理估计引导信号；(2)引入无雅可比机制直接修正干净预测logits，避免梯度不稳定；(3)兼容可微和不可微奖励函数。

Result: 在DNA、蛋白质序列和分子生成任务上达到SOTA性能，且无需额外训练，常优于微调方法。

Conclusion: GILC即插即用，无需重新训练即可高效实现离散扩散模型的可控生成，有效解决了梯度不稳定问题，并在多个生物序列和分子生成任务上表现优异。

Abstract: Controllable generation with discrete diffusion models is often hindered by high computational overhead or the need for retraining. In this paper, we present \underline{\textbf{G}}radient-\underline{\textbf{I}}nformed \underline{\textbf{L}}ogit \underline{\textbf{C}}orrection (\textbf{GILC}), a plug-and-play framework that efficiently estimates guidance signals by repurposing the pretrained denoising network as a variational proxy. To circumvent the gradient instability inherent in high-dimensional discrete spaces, we introduce a Jacobian-free mechanism that directly corrects the clean prediction logits, facilitating stable and effective guidance. Our method accommodates both differentiable and non-differentiable reward functions. Extensive experiments across DNA, protein sequence, and molecular generation tasks demonstrate that GILC achieves state-of-the-art performance without additional training, frequently outperforming fine-tuning approaches.

</details>


### [115] [Learning What to Forget: Improving LLM Unlearning via Learned Token-Level Importance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06320)
*Gizem Yüce,Giorgos Nikolaou,Nicolas Flammarion*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ATWU框架，通过保留目标与遗忘目标的冲突程度自动学习每个token对遗忘的重要性权重，无需外部标注或辅助模型，在TOFU和RWKU基准上实现了最优的遗忘-保留权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归语言模型遗忘时，遗忘样本中的不同token对遗忘的贡献不同，现有方法要么忽略这种异质性，要么依赖辅助模型、启发式规则或外部标注来估计每个token的遗忘相关性，缺乏轻量且无监督的解决方案。

Method: 将token权重与模型参数联合优化作为目标函数，在自然分离条件下该目标可恢复oracle级别的遗忘特定token支持。具体提出ATWU框架，利用隐藏状态上的简单线性评分器在学习过程中同时更新token权重和模型参数，无需外部token级监督。

Result: 在TOFU和RWKU基准上，ATWU实现了最优的遗忘-保留权衡，优于样本级方法、基于概率的token加权启发式方法和基于辅助模型的方法。学习到的分数与真实的遗忘特定跨度对齐更好。

Conclusion: 保留冲突为识别语言模型应遗忘的内容提供了有效准则，能够以极小的计算开销直接从模型表示中无监督学习token级遗忘特异性。

Abstract: Machine unlearning aims to remove targeted knowledge from a trained model while preserving its general capabilities. For autoregressive language models, not all tokens in a forget sample are equally relevant to forgetting. Existing approaches either ignore this heterogeneity or rely on auxiliary models, heuristics, or external annotations to estimate each token's relevance for forgetting. We instead characterize it through the interaction with the retain objective: a token is forget-specific to the extent that minimizing the forget loss on that token does not conflict with retain optimality. We formalize this perspective as a joint optimization problem over the model parameters and the token weights and show that, under a natural separation condition, the resulting objective recovers the oracle forget-specific token support. Motivated by this formulation, we introduce Alternating Token-Weighted Unlearning (ATWU), a lightweight framework that jointly learns token forget-specificity and model parameters during unlearning using a simple linear scorer over the hidden states, without external token level supervision. Across TOFU and RWKU, ATWU achieves state of the art forget-retain trade-offs, outperforming sample-level methods, probability-based token weighting heuristics, and auxiliary-model-based approaches. Moreover, the learned scores align substantially better with ground truth forget-specific spans, indicating that ATWU identifies semantically meaningful token level forgetting signals. Overall, our results suggest that retain conflict provides an effective criterion for identifying what language models should forget, enabling unsupervised learning of token level forget-specificity directly from model representations with minimal computational overhead.

</details>


### [116] [PAMF: Prior-Aware Multimodal Fusion for Incomplete Time Series Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06328)
*Ziwen Kan,Wugeng Zheng,Tianlong Chen,Song Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出PAMF框架，通过先验感知流匹配和权重共享，显式处理多模态时间序列中两种缺失模式（模态内缺失和模态级缺失），将插补与下游预测任务耦合，在多个医疗基准上取得最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法存在三个问题：(1) 通过掩码或缺失嵌入隐式表示缺失数据，无法学习实例特定的缺失信息；(2) 大多数方法仅针对一种缺失模式设计；(3) 插补过程与下游任务隔离，无法利用下游任务引导生成更具信息性的表示。

Method: (1) 使用类型特定的先验初始化流匹配源状态，区分两种缺失类型；(2) 通过架构匹配的编码器与权重共享，连接插补和分类任务，将任务相关表示传递到插补过程中。

Result: 在多个多模态医疗时间序列基准上，相比现有基线方法，PAMF在多种缺失设置下取得了最强的整体下游性能。

Conclusion: 显式建模不同缺失模式的结构先验，并将插补与下游任务耦合，能有效提升多模态时间序列在不完整观测下的预测性能。

Abstract: In healthcare, multimodal time series tasks often operate on incomplete observations in practice, for example when ECG segments are lost because electrodes detach or an entire respiratory channel is unavailable during overnight monitoring. Such missingness typically appears in two structurally distinct patterns: within-modality missing, where values are absent within an otherwise observed modality, and modality-level missing, where an entire modality is unavailable. Existing methods typically represent unobserved data implicitly through masks or missing embeddings, without learning instance-specific missing information, and most are designed for only one missingness pattern. A natural approach is to explicitly estimate the missing data; however, existing imputation methods treat missingness uniformly despite their different structural priors, and the imputation process is often isolated from downstream tasks, preventing downstream tasks from guiding imputation toward more informative representations. To address these limitations, we present PAMF, a multimodal time-series framework that explicitly handles different missingness patterns while coupling imputation with downstream prediction through prior-aware flow matching and weight sharing. Specifically, the method initializes the flow-matching source state with type-specific priors to distinguish two missing types. It further connects imputation and classification through architecturally matched encoders with weight sharing, transferring task-relevant representations into the imputation process. Experiments on multiple multimodal healthcare time-series benchmarks show that the proposed method achieves the strongest overall downstream performance across diverse datasets and missing settings compared with existing baselines.

</details>


### [117] [Efficient Mean Curvature Computation on High-Dimensional Data Manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06329)
*Alexandre L. M. Levada*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了两种互补的加速方法，将高维数据集中局部平均曲率估计的计算复杂度从O(m^4)降低到O(k^2 m + k m p^2)，实现了50-300倍的加速比且精度损失可忽略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维数据集的局部平均曲率估计是几何感知机器学习算法（如MCBP方法）的关键，但朴素实现的O(m^4)复杂度使其在处理超过几十个特征的数据集时不可行，亟需更高效的计算方法。

Method: 1) 利用协方差矩阵特征向量的正交性和迹算子的循环性，推导出精确代数恒等式，消除矩阵H并将逐点成本降至O(m^2)；2) 针对局部协方差矩阵秩≤k-1≪m的特性，用k×m中心化数据矩阵的截断SVD替代完整特征分解（O(k^2 m)），并基于Haar度量下外积的期望值推导零空间特征向量贡献的解析近似，最终总成本为O(k^2 m + k m p^2)。

Result: 在真实数据集上，相比原始实现获得50-300倍加速比，快速估计器替代原始版本时精度损失可忽略不计。

Conclusion: 所提出的可扩展数据驱动局部曲率估计方法，使曲率成为从经典到现代深度学习管道中广泛机器学习任务的实用几何特征。

Abstract: Estimating local mean curvature at each point of a high-dimensional dataset is a key ingredient of geometry-aware machine learning algorithms, such as the Mean Curvature Boundary Points (MCBP) method. The naive implementation of this computation, based on a local shape operator approximated from k-nearest neighbor patches, involves an explicit construction of a matrix $H$ whose trace form yields an $O(m^4)$ cost per point, rendering the approach intractable for datasets with more than a few dozen features. This paper introduces two complementary contributions that together reduce this cost by several orders of magnitude. The first contribution is an exact algebraic identity. This identity, derived from the orthogonality of the eigenvectors of the covariance matrix and the cyclicity of the trace operator, eliminates $H$ entirely and reduces the per-point cost to $O(m^2)$ after the eigendecomposition. The second contribution addresses the remaining $O(m^3)$ bottleneck of the full eigendecomposition. Since the local covariance matrix has rank at most $k-1 \ll m$, we replace it with a truncated SVD of the $k \times m$ centered data matrix, an $O(k^2 m)$ operation, and derive an analytical approximation for the contribution of the null-space eigenvectors based on the expected value of their outer product under the Haar measure. The resulting estimator has total cost $O(k^2 m + k m p^2)$, where $p = k-1$. Experiments on real-world datasets confirm speedups of 50 to 300 times relative to the original implementation, with negligible loss when the fast estimator is used to replace the original version. By providing a scalable and data-driven estimate of local curvature, the proposed method establishes curvature as a practical geometric feature for a broad range of machine learning tasks, from classical to modern deep learning pipelines.

</details>


### [118] [Subspace-Aware Sparse Autoencoders for Effective Mechanistic Interpretability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06333)
*Seyed Arshan Dalili,Mehrdad Mahdavi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出标准稀疏自编码器（SAE）假设每个潜在特征为一维，与语言模型中特征的多维结构不匹配，导致特征分裂。作者提出子空间感知稀疏自编码器（SASA），用学习到的子空间替代单向量解码器，在理论保证下将样本复杂度从指数级降至多项式级，实验表明SASA在GPT-2和Mistral-7B上减少了特征分裂并提升了可解释性，且仅需约一半的训练token。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准SAE假设每个潜在特征对应单一解码方向（一维），这与大语言模型中特征实际具有多维内在结构的事实不匹配，导致特征被不合理地分裂成多个接近共线的潜变量，产生虚假的多样性并模糊了内在几何结构。

Method: 提出子空间感知稀疏自编码器（SASA）：(1) 用学习到的解码器子空间替代单向量解码器；(2) 通过Top-s分组门控强制块稀疏性；(3) 使用核范数正则化器自适应调整每个组的有效秩。理论分析表明，当块大小r ≥ d_i时，单个组能表示整个特征切片且是全局最优解。

Result: 在GPT-2和Mistral-7B上的实验表明，SASA减少了特征分裂和吸收现象，提高了单义性和可解释性，在匹配或超越标准SAE性能的同时，仅需约一半的token预算。

Conclusion: 标准SAE的一维假设在理论上有缺陷，会导致指数级原子数需求；SASA通过子空间解码和块稀疏机制解决了这一问题，在理论上将样本复杂度从指数级降至多项式级，实证上也展现出更优的可解释性和效率。

Abstract: Sparse Autoencoders (SAEs) are widely used for mechanistic interpretability in large language models, yet their formulation assigns each latent feature a single decoder direction, implicitly assuming features to be one-dimensional. We show that this assumption mismatches with the multi-dimensional structure of model features, provably inducing feature splitting through two distinct mechanisms. Geometrically, reconstructing a feature of intrinsic dimension $d_i \ge 2$ to error $\varepsilon$ with single-direction decoders forces a number of atoms that is exponential in $d_i$. From an end-to-end optimization perspective, this splitting is not merely possible but actively preferred. We prove that there exists a continuous path from the true $d_i$-dimensional basis to a strictly lower risk of the $\ell_1$-regularized SAE objective, whose descent directions drive any trained dictionary into that exponential regime. A single coherent feature is therefore fragmented across many near-collinear latents, producing spurious multiplicity and obscuring the intrinsic geometry. Motivated by this, we introduce Subspace-Aware Sparse Autoencoders (SASA), which replace single-vector decoders with learned decoder subspaces, enforce block sparsity via Top-$s$ group gating, and adapt each group's effective rank with a nuclear-norm regularizer. We then show that once the block size satisfies $r \ge d_i$, a single group not only can represent the entire feature slice but is the global minimizer of the SASA objective. This consolidation yields a sample complexity polynomial in $d_i$ rather than exponential -- a decisive advantage given that every training activation costs an LLM forward pass. Empirically, on GPT-2 and Mistral-7B, SASA reduces feature splitting and absorption, improves monosemanticity and interpretability, and matches or exceeds standard SAEs while training on roughly half the token budget.

</details>


### [119] [Quantifying the Privacy of Counterfactuals by Leveraging Membership Inference Attacks Against Synthetic Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06334)
*Maryam Babaei,Yingke Wang,Hadrien Lautraite,Heber H. Arcolezi,Ulrich Aivodji,Sebastien Gambs*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示反事实解释（counterfactuals）与合成数据具有类比性，证明了针对合成数据的成员推断攻击可以有效应用于反事实数据，并且即使无法访问原始模型，仅凭反事实数据本身也能成功实施隐私攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反事实解释在高风险决策中被广泛用于解释模型，但其生成的数据可能被对手利用进行隐私攻击。现有针对反事实的成员推断攻击通常需要能够查询模型，本文希望探究在更严格（无模型访问）的条件下，反事实数据是否仍会泄露隐私。

Method: 基于反事实与合成数据在"提供真实训练数据的替代品"这一功能上的类比，将已有针对合成数据的成员推断攻击方法迁移至反事实数据场景。设计了无需访问原始模型、仅依赖反事实数据集合的攻击方案。

Result: 证明了针对合成数据的成员推断攻击对多种类型的反事实数据依然有效；且成功实现了仅使用反事实数据集合（无需模型查询）的成员推断攻击。

Conclusion: 模型开发者在发布反事实数据时应更加谨慎，否则可能导致训练数据隐私泄露。

Abstract: Counterfactuals are typically used in high-stakes decision areas to explain a machine learning model by showing how changes to the user profiles result in the desired outcome. However, explaining the model's decisions through counterfactuals can also be exploited by an adversary to conduct privacy attacks against the model or its training data. Drawing on the analogy that counterfactuals provide realistic substitutes for real training data, similar to synthetic data, we demonstrate in this paper how it is possible to successfully perform privacy attacks on counterfactuals by drawing on the attacks developed against synthetic data. More precisely, we investigate the effectiveness of the membership inference attacks designed for synthetic data on various types of counterfactuals. Additionally, while existing membership inference attacks against counterfactuals usually require to be able to query the model, we show how it is possible to perform successful membership inference attacks using only a set of counterfactuals, with no access to the model from which they are generated. Our results demonstrate that model developers should be more cautious when releasing counterfactuals to various users, as it can lead to a privacy breach.

</details>


### [120] [Bridging Domain Expertise and Generalization for Performance Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06335)
*Shuxuan Li,Zhilin Zhao,Quyu Kong,Wei-Shi Zheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出FRAP方法，通过融合基础模型与外部基础模型的互补优势，在分布偏移下进行更可靠的模型性能估计，实验表明该方法显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有性能估计方法在分布偏移下仅依赖单一模型输出，导致模型偏差被放大，与真实性能的相关性减弱。

Method: FRAP利用外部基础模型与基础模型的互补优势：通过温度缩放校准使基础模型的预测分布与基础模型对齐，再基于置信度加权融合两者预测，生成更可靠的参考分布，最后通过衡量基础模型预测与参考分布的一致性来估计性能。

Result: 在多种数据集和架构上的大量实验表明，FRAP在分布偏移下的性能估计方法中表现出一致且显著的改进。

Conclusion: 融合基础模型的鲁棒性与基础模型的领域专长，能够构建更可靠的标签替代指标，从而在分布偏移场景下实现更准确的模型性能估计。

Abstract: Performance estimation under distribution shift aims to predict how a model behaves on an unlabeled test set whose distribution differs from the training data, a scenario that requires reliable indicators that can faithfully reflect model behavior without ground-truth labels. Existing approaches rely solely on the outputs of the given model whose biases are amplified once the distribution shifts, weakening the correlation with the true performance. Motivated by this limitation, we propose Fused Reference Alignment Prediction (FRAP), which leverages the complementary strengths of an external foundation model and the base model to construct a more reliable surrogate of the ground-truth labels. FRAP aligns the prediction distribution of the foundation model with that of the base model by applying temperature-scaled calibration that minimizes their divergence. The aligned predictions are fused through confidence-based weighting into a refined reference distribution that integrates robustness from the foundation model and domain-specific expertise from the base model, and performance estimation is obtained by measuring how closely the base model predictions agree with this reference. Extensive experiments across diverse datasets and architectures show that FRAP provides consistent and substantial improvements over representative performance-estimation methods under distribution shift.

</details>


### [121] [Equivariant Neural Belief Propagation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06344)
*Zehua Cheng,Wei Dai,Jiahao Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出等变神经信念传播（ENBP），一种因子图框架，其消息为SE(3)等变高斯混合模型，能够合成秩2精度矩阵，在分子构象生成和多体机器人推理任务上达到高精度、极快速度和严格等变性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有等变网络只能输出标量和向量，无法表示各向异性不确定性所需的秩2精度张量；单分量消息会坍塌多模态能量景观为无物理意义的平均值。

Method: 构建因子图框架，消息为SE(3)等变高斯混合模型；通过等变外积合成秩2精度矩阵，经可微谱分解处理，使用基于KL散度的贪心混合约简方法（可证明与SE(3)交换）。

Result: GEOM-QM9和GEOM-Drugs上达到98.9%构象覆盖率、0.090Å误差、亚秒延迟（比扩散基线快100倍以上）；多体机器人推理中，标准BP在15+智能体时发散，ENBP收敛且碰撞率接近零，等变性误差达机器精度（~10⁻⁷ vs 10⁻¹）。

Conclusion: ENBP在严格SE(3)等变性下实现了对空间嵌入变量的概率推理，突破了现有等变网络在不确定性建模上的维度限制，在分子构象和多体推理任务中显著优于现有方法。

Abstract: Probabilistic inference over spatially embedded variables requires beliefs that respect $SE(3)$ symmetry, yet existing equivariant networks produce only scalars and vectors -- not the rank-2 precision tensors needed for anisotropic uncertainty, and single-component messages collapse multi-modal energy landscapes to physically meaningless averages. We introduce Equivariant Neural Belief Propagation (ENBP), a factor-graph framework whose messages are equivariant Gaussian mixture models with sufficient statistics that transform exactly under $SE(3)$. Rank-2 precision matrices are synthesised via equivariant outer products, ingested through differentiable spectral decomposition, and kept tractable by a greedy KL-based mixture reduction that provably commutes with $SE(3)$. On GEOM-QM9 and GEOM-Drugs, ENBP achieves 98.9% conformational coverage at 0.090 $\mathring{A}$ error with sub-second latency -- over $100\times$ faster than diffusion baselines at higher accuracy. On multi-body robotic inference, vanilla loopy BP diverges at 15+ agents while ENBP converges with near-zero collision rates and machine-precision equivariance error (${\sim}10^{-7}$ vs.\ $10^{-1}$ for augmented baselines).

</details>


### [122] [Performance Evaluation of GraphCast for Medium-Range Weather Forecasting over Brazil](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06348)
*Wolfgang R. Rowell,Lucas S. Kupssinskü*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究评估了GraphCast与ECMWF IFS HRES在巴西四个气候亚区的预报性能，发现GraphCast在冬季中程预报中表现不佳，但在延伸期具有优势；在夏季湿季能准确捕捉大尺度水汽输送，同时抑制高频对流变率。该研究为巴西建立了基准，并定义了未来AI模型"热带化"的物理边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全球天气预报正随着机器学习天气预报模型（MLWP）的兴起而迅速转变，但这些数据驱动架构在全球尺度表现优异的同时，在南半球区域的基准评估仍然稀缺，其在复杂、强对流环境中的有效性尚未得到充分验证。

Method: 利用可扩展的云原生管道和WeatherBench-X基准框架，以业务IFS分析为地面真相，评估GraphCast与确定性ECMWF IFS HRES在巴西四个不同气候亚区、四个季节窗口中对选定对流层变量（850 hPa温度、850 hPa比湿、500 hPa位势高度）的表现。

Result: GraphCast在冬季中程（第2-7天）对快速传播的斜压系统的Z500预报表现不佳，但在延伸期具有优势；在夏季湿季能准确捕捉大尺度水汽输送，同时内在抑制高频对流变率。

Conclusion: 该研究为巴西建立了基准，定义了指导未来"热带化"工作的具体物理边界，旨在优化这些基础AI模型以提升区域韧性。

Abstract: The paradigm of global weather forecasting is rapidly shifting with the emergence of Machine Learning Weather Prediction models (MLWP). While these data-driven architectures demonstrate remarkable global skill, regional benchmarks in the Global South remain scarce, leaving their efficacy in complex, highly convective environments largely unverified. This study evaluates the performance of GraphCast operational against the deterministic ECMWF IFS HRES as baseline across four distinct Brazilian climatic sub-regions. Utilizing a scalable, cloud-native pipeline and the WeatherBench-X framework for benchmarking weather models, we assess selected tropospheric variables ($T_{850}$, $Q_{850}$, $Z_{500}$) over four selected seasonal windows, employing the operational IFS analysis as the ground truth to calculate the statistical metrics for both models. Results reveal a regime-dependent skill profile. During the austral winter, GraphCast underperforms in the medium range (lead days 2-7) for $Z_{500}$ when resolving fast-propagating baroclinic systems over southern Brazil, but regains an advantage in the extended range, where its inherent smoothing of chaotic small-scale variability becomes beneficial under deterministic skill metrics. Conversely, during the austral summer wet season, GraphCast accurately captures large-scale moisture transport while intrinsically dampening the high-frequency convective variability that degrades deterministic NWP temperature forecasts. These findings establish a baseline for Brazil and define the specific physical boundaries that will guide future ``tropicalization'' efforts, aiming to optimize these foundational AI models for regional resilience.

</details>


### [123] [Maximising the Set-Piece Return: Optimising Football Corner Tactics with Graph Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06353)
*Sean Groom,Michael Groom,Francisco Belo,Axl Rice,Liam Anderson,Victor-Alexandru Darvariu,Shuo Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于图强化学习的架构，通过优化角球进攻中球员位置和速度来最大化首次触球射门概率，在英超3000+角球数据上显著优于传统优化方法，实现了从历史模仿到奖励驱动战术发现的转变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有足球战术分析方法局限于表征历史动作或分析师指定的反事实场景，未能超越历史观察模式去发现新的、可泛化的球员配置与策略。

Method: 将角球战术优化建模为强化学习决策问题，中央策略调整进攻方球员位置和速度以最大化首次触球射门概率；采用图结构数据上的强化学习架构，生成可适配任意起始球员位置的通用策略。

Result: 在3000+英超角球数据上评估，所提方法在匹配推理预算下显著优于基线优化技术。

Conclusion: 图强化学习可将定位球分析从历史评估与模仿转向奖励驱动的战术发现。

Abstract: Machine learning is increasingly employed for the evaluation of football tactics. However, existing approaches focus on characterising historical actions or analyst-specified counterfactual scenarios. In this work, we seek to go beyond the imitation of historically observed patterns towards discovering new generalisable player configurations and strategies. To tackle this, we focus on optimising corner kick routines, and formulate a decision-making problem in which a central policy makes adjustments to attacking player positions and velocities to maximise first contact shot probability. Unlike classic optimisation that solves for isolated setups, we contribute a reinforcement learning architecture operating on graph-structured data that yields a general policy for adjusting arbitrary starting player positions. Evaluated on over 3,000 Premier League corners, our approach strongly outperforms baseline optimisation techniques under matched inference budgets. Our results suggest that graph reinforcement learning can shift set-piece analysis from historical evaluation and imitation towards reward-driven tactical discovery.

</details>


### [124] [End-to-End Subgraph Detection with GraphDETR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06364)
*Dexiong Chen,Till Hendrik Schulz,Karsten Borgwardt*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GraphDETR是一个深度学习框架，将子图检测视为集合预测问题，借鉴DETR目标检测思想，通过图神经网络编码目标图，使用可学习查询向量和Transformer解码器联合预测所有模式出现，支持精确匹配和近似匹配，在分子结构、循环、团等模式检测中表现优异，在ChEMBL数据集上分子功能基团检测AP₁₀₀达91.2。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 子图检测与子图同构密切相关，后者是NP完全问题，传统组合方法只能处理小规模模式或中等规模图，亟需一种可扩展的深度学习方法来高效检测子图。

Method: 将子图检测公式化为集合预测问题，使用GNN编码目标图，采用固定数量的可学习查询向量，通过Transformer解码器在单次前向传播中联合预测所有模式出现，使用二分图匹配进行端到端训练。

Result: 能够检测包含分子结构、循环、团和模糊模式在内的多样模式，模式规模达50个节点，目标图规模达1000个节点；在ChEMBL数据集上的分子功能基团检测中AP₁₀₀达到91.2。

Conclusion: GraphDETR有效解决了子图检测问题，超越了传统组合方法仅限于精确结构匹配的限制，自然扩展到近似匹配，在多种模式检测任务中表现优异。

Abstract: Subgraph detection seeks to identify whether and where instances of query patterns occur within a larger graph. This problem is fundamental across scientific domains and is closely related to subgraph isomorphism, which is NP-complete, limiting combinatorial approaches to small patterns or moderately sized graphs. We introduce GraphDETR, a deep learning framework that formulates subgraph detection as a set prediction problem, analogous to DETR in object detection. GraphDETR encodes the target graph with a graph neural network, and employs a fixed set of learnable query vectors, decoded via a transformer decoder, to predict all pattern occurrences jointly in a single forward pass. This is enabled by training the model end-to-end with bipartite matching. Unlike traditional combinatorial methods that only solve exact structural matching, GraphDETR naturally extends to approximate matching, enabling detection beyond exact pattern correspondence. Empirically, we show that GraphDETR can detect diverse patterns, such as molecular structures, cycles, cliques, and fuzzy patterns of up to 50 nodes, in target graphs with up to 1000 nodes. We further evaluate on molecular functional group detection over the ChEMBL dataset, where GraphDETR predicts the complete set of functional groups per molecule, achieving a strong performance of $\text{AP}_{100} = 91.2$.

</details>


### [125] [Learned Response-Field Inertia Operator for HEC-RAS 2D Water-Surface Elevation Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06385)
*Edward Holmberg,Elias Ioup,Md Meftahul Ferdaus,Mahdi Abdelguerfi,Julian Simeonov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种跨数据集评估学习型原生细胞替代模型的方法，用于HEC-RAS 2D中求解器一致的水面高程预测。引入无强制项、基于增量的学习型替代模型LRFIO，该模型校准惯性响应算子并通过封闭形式原生细胞滚动部署，实验表明当前原生细胞增量是一种强求解器条件预测框架，新增复杂度仅应在经验验证合理时保留。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有替代模型在跨数据集评估时存在栅格重映射误差和信息访问混淆问题，且对替代模型的复杂度（如分段、残差修正、神经化惯性）缺乏系统的经验验证准则。

Method: 提出LRFIO，基于无强制项的增量式学习，从已求解的HEC-RAS轨迹中校准惯性响应算子，通过封闭形式原生细胞滚动部署。评估采用持久性、全局校准惯性和分段响应场惯性组成的基准优先响应层次，将分段、残差修正和神经化惯性作为可学习建模选择，仅当验证证据支持时才保留新增复杂度。

Result: 在四个不同的HEC-RAS 2D基准上，LRFIO对不同领域保留不同响应结构，选择器审计显示最大验证遗憾率为4.30%，部署滚动时间0.003s至0.242s，Beaver Bayou实测对比估计加速比达2.75×10^4（视界归一化）。

Conclusion: 当前原生细胞增量是一种强求解器条件预测框架；响应场、神经或空间新增复杂度应在经验验证合理时才保留，切勿不加区分地添加。

Abstract: This article presents a cross-dataset evaluation of learned native-cell surrogate models for solver-consistent water-surface elevation (WSE) prediction in HEC-RAS 2D. To avoid raster remapping error and information-access confounding, surrogates are evaluated directly on the original nonuniform computational cells under an explicit policy that separates static project inputs, current hydraulic state, project-input forcing, calibration-derived quantities, and future solver-output targets. We introduce the Learned Response-Field Inertia Operator (LRFIO), a no-forcing, increment-based learned surrogate that calibrates an inertial response operator from solved HEC-RAS trajectories and deploys the retained operator through closed-form native-cell rollout. LRFIO evaluates a base-case-first response hierarchy consisting of persistence, global calibrated inertia, and segmented response-field inertia. Segmentation, residual correction, and neuralized inertia are treated as learnable modeling choices, with added complexity retained only when validation evidence justifies its cost. Evaluated across four diverse HEC-RAS 2D benchmarks, LRFIO retains different response structures for different domains, demonstrating adaptive learned complexity. The selector audit shows controlled complexity with a maximum validation regret of 4.30%. During deployment, retained rollout times range from 0.003 s to 0.242 s, and the Beaver Bayou measured-solve comparison gives an estimated 2.75 x 10^4 horizon-normalized speedup over HEC-RAS. These results indicate that the current native-cell increment is a strong solver-conditioned predictive scaffold and that added response-field, neural, or spatial complexity should be retained only when empirically justified.

</details>


### [126] [Proper Scoring Rules for Right-Censored Survival Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06393)
*Jef Jonkers,Glenn Van Wallendael,Luc Duchateau,Sofie Van Hoecke*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对右删失生存数据的通吃评分规则框架：先将预测分布通过删失机制映射，再应用标准评分规则于观测数据分布，从而得到删失版CRPS、Brier分数等，并证明了其适定性和严格适定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准概率评分规则无法直接应用于右删失数据（事件时间仅部分观测），需要一个理论严谨的框架来评估和训练右删失生存模型的概率预测。

Method: 核心思想是将预测分布通过删失机制映射到观测数据分布，再应用底层评分规则。对于固定删失时间得到局部化评分，对于随机或部分观测的删失时间得到边缘化评分。该方法统一了删失似然和IPCW准则，并扩展了CRPS、pinball loss、Brier score和energy score到删失场景。

Result: 边缘化评分在条件独立删失下是适定的，在可识别区域上是严格适定的。实验表明该方法能在多种删失机制下正确排序oracle预测，而依赖预测的加权评分可能出现排序反转。删失版engression相比朴素训练有显著提升。

Conclusion: 该框架为右删失生存数据的概率评估和训练提供了统一的理论基础，避免了现有加权方法的排序反转问题，并且可扩展到多变量生存建模（删失engression）。

Abstract: Proper scoring rules provide a rigorous theoretical basis for the training and evaluation of probabilistic forecasts. However, in the presence of right censoring, the event time is only partially observed, rendering conventional scoring rules inapplicable in their standard form. We propose a framework for proper scoring of right-censored survival outcomes based on a simple idea: first, map the predictive distribution through the censoring mechanism, then apply the underlying proper score on the induced observed-data law. This yields localized scores for fixed censoring times and marginalized scores when the censoring time is random or only partially observed. The resulting construction recovers familiar right-censored likelihood and IPCW-type criteria within a coherent framework, while also yielding right-censored versions of the CRPS, pinball loss, Brier score, and energy score. We show that the marginalized score is proper under conditional independent censoring and strictly proper on the identifiable region. The same principle also leads to censored engression, a sample-based learning objective for multivariate right-censored survival modeling. In experiments, our scores correctly rank the oracle forecast across several censoring regimes, whereas forecast-dependent plug-in weighted scores can exhibit ranking reversals. Censored engression likewise substantially improves over naive training on censored outcomes.

</details>


### [127] [The Post-GCN Decade Revisited: Curvature-Stratified Evaluation of Relational Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06397)
*Shuo Wang,Xiangyu Wang,Quanxin Wang,Bailin Wu,Bokui Wang,Shunyang Huang,Boyan Deng,Haonan Liu,Ruiyi Fang,Zhenxiang Xu,Boyu Wang,Zhao Kang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了关系学习中传统扁平化排行榜评估方法存在系统性偏差，提出基于曲率分层的评估框架，发现模型排名在相同曲率域内高度稳定但跨域显著变化，表明模型性能本质上是几何依赖的而非通用可迁移的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统关系学习评估依赖扁平化排行榜，跨异质数据集平均性能，隐含假设数据具有统一底层结构。该假设引入系统性偏差，掩盖了几何依赖的性能变化，可能导致关于模型泛化能力的误导性结论。

Method: 提出曲率分层评估框架，按数据集曲率划分为正曲率、负曲率和近零曲率三种区域；在14个数据集上评估18个代表性模型（包括GCN、图基础模型和表格学习方法），分析模型排名在不同曲率域内的稳定性及跨域变化。

Result: 模型排名在同一曲率域内高度稳定，但跨曲率域时显著变化，表明性能本质上是几何依赖的；识别出图基础模型在某些曲率域相比几何对齐的GNN存在边际收益递减现象。

Conclusion: 提出几何感知的评估协议，比标准聚合基准更可靠和可解释；释放代码、曲率分层数据集和评估工具以支持可重复评估。

Abstract: Current evaluation practices in relational learning rely heavily on flat leaderboards that average performance across heterogeneous datasets, implicitly assuming a uniform underlying structure. We show that this assumption introduces systematic bias: it obscures geometry-dependent performance variations and can lead to misleading conclusions about model generalization. In this work, we identify intrinsic geometry as a key latent factor governing model effectiveness. We demonstrate that conventional aggregated metrics mask critical performance trade-offs that only become visible when datasets are stratified by their geometric properties. To address this issue, we introduce a curvature-stratified evaluation framework that partitions datasets into positive, negative, and near-zero curvature regimes. Our benchmark evaluates 18 representative models including Graph Convolutional Networks (GCNs), Graph Foundation Models (GFMs), and tabular learning methods across 14 datasets. We find that model rankings are highly stable within each curvature regime but shift significantly across regimes, indicating that performance is fundamentally geometry-dependent rather than universally transferable. Notably, we identify regimes where GFMs offer diminishing returns compared to geometry-aligned GNNs. Based on these findings, we propose a geometry-aware evaluation protocol that yields more reliable and interpretable comparisons than standard aggregated benchmarks. We release all code, curvature-stratified dataset splits, and evaluation tools to support reproducible and rigorous assessment of future relational learning methods. Code and datasets are provided in our project homepage: https://sirbabbage.github.io/CurvBench_HOME/.

</details>


### [128] [Causal Atlases from Entropic Inference: Bayesian Networks beyond Optimal DAGs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06440)
*Hazhir Aliahmadi,Irina Babayan,Greg van Anders*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于熵推理的方法生成因果关系图谱，通过最大熵系综采样来量化因果结构中的固有模糊性，并证明传统优化方法得到的DAG可能包含因果伪影。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统贝叶斯网络构建依赖优化方法，但底层数据可能存在多条因果链，优化方法不适合学习因果关系，需要更忠实于数据不确定性的因果表示框架。

Method: 采用基于熵推理（entropy-based inference）的方法，在2节点和20节点线性结构方程模型的模拟噪声数据上，采样最大熵系综（maximum-entropy ensemble of graphs），生成与数据一致的因果图谱集。

Result: 该方法量化了因果关系的固有结构模糊性，发现优化得到的DAG可能包含在同等准确拓扑中不一致的因果伪影。

Conclusion: 基于熵推理的方法能够生成与数据一致的多个因果图谱，比传统优化方法更忠实于数据中固有的因果不确定性。

Abstract: Data-driven causal relationship identification is pertinent to advancing understanding of complex systems both within and beyond science. Bayesian networks offer a probabilistic method for modelling generic causal relationships via directed acyclic graphs (DAGs). However, typical techniques for constructing Bayesian networks rely on optimization, which can be ill-suited for learning causal relationships because the underlying data may admit multiple chains of causation. More data-faithful representations of causal relationships would provide frameworks for constructing multiple causal maps that are consistent with the variability that is inherent in underlying data. Here, we show that entropy-based inference generates atlases of plausible causal relationships that are consistent with underlying data. On simulated noisy data of 2- and 20-node linear structural equation models, we sample a maximum-entropy ensemble of graphs that allow us to quantify the inherent structural ambiguity in underlying causal relationships. Our method shows that "optimized" DAGs can contain causal artifacts are not consistent across equivalently accurate topologies.

</details>


### [129] [In-Context Multiple Instance Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06458)
*Alexander Möllers,Marvin Sextro,Julius Hense,Gabriel Dernbach,Klaus-Robert Müller*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于Perceiver架构的上下文学习器，通过在合成数据上预训练，能够在少量标注包的情况下解决多示例学习（MIL）任务，无需梯度更新即可在单次前向传播中完成分类，并在12个MIL基准测试中平均表现最优，超过了需要任务特定训练的监督基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MIL算法在低标签率场景下表现不佳：灵活模型容易过拟合，刚性模型难以适应特定任务。作者希望开发一种能在少量标注包下高效泛化的MIL方法。

Method: 采用Perceiver-style架构的上下文学习器，在合成数据上进行预训练。设计了多种合成数据生成器来模拟包结构数据，并混合使用这些生成器以继承各自互补的归纳偏置。推理时只需单次前向传播，无需梯度更新。

Result: 在12个MIL基准数据集上，混合预训练模型平均表现最佳，超越了需要任务特定训练的有监督基线方法。

Conclusion: 基于合成数据预训练的上下文学习器能有效解决低标签率下的MIL问题，混合多种合成数据生成器可捕获互补的归纳偏置，提升泛化能力。

Abstract: Multiple Instance Learning (MIL) addresses problems where supervision is available at the level of bags of instances and has been successfully applied in fields ranging from computational pathology to satellite imagery. Nevertheless, existing algorithms struggle in the low-label regime that characterizes many real-world applications. Flexible models overfit and rigid ones fail to adapt to the task at hand. We show that pretraining an in-context learner with a Perceiver-style architecture on synthetic data yields a model that can solve new tasks from a handful of labeled bags. At inference time, classification happens in a single forward pass and requires no gradient updates. We propose and investigate different synthetic data generators for bag-structured data and find that they capture complementary inductive biases. A model pretrained on a mixture of these generators inherits their per-task strengths and achieves the best average performance across twelve MIL benchmarks, outperforming supervised baselines that require task-specific training.

</details>


### [130] [Event Detection for Parameter-to-KPI Dependency Learning for AI-RAN](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06459)
*Christie Djidjev,Nicholas Kaminski*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对AI-RAN和O-RAN架构中多个AI驱动控制功能相互干扰的问题，提出了一种将连续遥测数据转换为二元事件指示器的显著性检测方法，用于检测控制参数与KPI之间的真实依赖关系，并通过合成闭环流量生成器验证了该方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 下一代无线网络中多个并发的AI驱动控制功能在优化不同网络目标时相互干扰，而仅从原始网络数据难以检测这种交互。现有方法缺乏可靠的、可解释的依赖结构来捕获控制参数与网络性能结果之间的主动影响关系。

Method: 将连续遥测数据转换为二元事件指示器的问题形式化为显著性检测问题；引入合成闭环流量生成器，其中植入了潜在的依赖关系作为真实基准；构建基于机器学习的依赖恢复管线，评估从含噪连续轨迹中恢复潜在依赖结构的能力。

Result: 实验表明，当信号与背景噪声充分分离时，所提出的管线能够可靠地从含噪连续轨迹中恢复潜在依赖结构；同时揭示阈值校准是控制事件检测质量的关键因素。

Conclusion: 该研究为自适应AI-RAN控制系统的可解释依赖学习奠定了基础性步骤，有助于未来管理多个AI控制功能之间的交互。

Abstract: Next-generation wireless networks are expected to rely on multiple concurrent AI-driven control functions that optimize different network objectives simultaneously, particularly in AI-integrated and open radio access network architectures such as AI Radio Access Network (AI-RAN) and Open Radio Access Network (O-RAN). When these functions interact, they can interfere with one another in ways that are difficult to detect from raw network data alone. A key missing piece for managing such interactions is a reliable, interpretable dependency structure that captures which control parameters are actively influencing which network performance outcomes at any given time. This paper focuses on the event-detection step needed to support such dependency learning by converting noisy continuous telemetry into binary indicators of parameter activity and KPI response. The central difficulty is that not every fluctuation in the data reflects a genuine control interaction, so the method must distinguish real parameter-outcome relationships from background variation. Because real AI-RAN traffic traces with known parameter-KPI ground truth are difficult to obtain, we introduce a synthetic closed-loop traffic generator with planted latent dependencies. We use this controlled telemetry to evaluate a machine-learning-based dependency recovery pipeline that formulates the conversion of continuous traces into binary event indicators as a significance-detection problem. Experimental evaluation shows that the proposed pipeline reliably recovers the latent dependency structure from noisy continuous traces when the signal is sufficiently separated from background variation, while highlighting threshold calibration as the key factor controlling event-detection quality. These results constitute a foundational step toward interpretable dependency learning for adaptive AI-RAN control systems.

</details>


### [131] [PC Layer: Polynomial Weight Preconditioning for Improving LLM Pre-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06470)
*Senmiao Wang,Tiantian Fang,Haoran Zhang,Yushun Zhang,Kunxiang Zhao,Alex Schwing,Ruoyu Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种预条件(PC)层，通过多项式预条件子对权重矩阵进行参数化，在LLM训练过程中保持稳定的权重条件，训练后可将预条件权重合并回原始架构，无推理开销。在Llama-1B预训练中优于标准Transformer，理论上证明了均匀约束各层奇异值可确保梯度下降在深度线性网络中的全局几何收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型训练过程中权重矩阵的条件数会恶化，导致优化不稳定，现有方法缺乏对权重矩阵谱结构的直接且可合并的调控手段。

Method: 提出预条件(PC)层，通过低阶多项式预条件子重塑权重矩阵的奇异值谱，训练后可将预条件权重合并回原始架构而不增加推理开销。

Result: 在Llama-1B预训练中，PC层在使用AdamW和Muon优化器时均优于标准Transformer。

Conclusion: 均匀约束各层奇异值可确保梯度下降在深度线性网络中几何收敛到全局最小值，验证了谱控制原则的有效性。

Abstract: We propose a preconditioning (PC) layer, a weight parameterization via polynomial preconditioner that ensures stable weight conditioning throughout LLM training. The PC module reshapes the singular-value spectrum of weight matrices via low-degree polynomial preconditioning. After training, the preconditioned weights can be merged back into the original architecture, incurring no inference overhead. We demonstrate the advantage of the proposed PC layer over standard transformers in Llama-1B pre-training, for both the AdamW and Muon optimizers. Theoretically, we justify this spectrum-control principle by proving that uniformly bounding each layer's singular values ensures geometric convergence of gradient descent to global minima, for certain deep linear networks. Our code is available at https://github.com/Empath-aln/PC-layer.

</details>


### [132] [RREDCoT: Segment-Level Reward Redistribution for Reasoning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06475)
*Mykyta Ielanskyi,Kajetan Schweighofer,Lukas Aichberger,Sepp Hochreiter*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对推理语言模型中GRPO算法存在的延迟奖励问题（高方差），提出RREDCoT方法，利用模型自身近似最优奖励重新分配，无需额外生成，实现链式思考轨迹中的信用分配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GRPO等强化学习微调算法在推理语言模型中存在延迟奖励问题（CoT完成后才分配奖励），导致蒙特卡洛方法方差高；传统的信用分配方法（如MC采样）计算开销大，不适合长上下文的训练时细粒度信用分配。

Method: 提出RREDCoT（Reward REDistribution for Chain of Thoughts），利用模型自身（无需额外生成）来近似最优奖励重新分配，将CoT轨迹分段后重新分配奖励，强调对得出正确解有贡献的关键片段。

Result: 与MC采样及多种归因方法相比，RREDCoT在奖励重新分配方面表现更优；同时分析了CoT分段粒度、状态值估计等关键因素对重新分配效果的影响。

Conclusion: RREDCoT通过模型自带的近似能力实现高效低方差的信用分配，为长上下文推理模型的强化学习训练提供了可行的延迟奖励解决方案。

Abstract: Recent advancements in reasoning language models have been driven by Reinforcement Learning (RL) fine-tuning. Most often, these rely on the Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) algorithm or modifications thereof to steer the models to produce Chain-of-Thought (CoT) traces. The final answer can only be verified, and the reward assigned, after the CoT trace is complete, making it a delayed reward problem. GRPO and its modifications correspond to Monte Carlo methods in standard RL, which are known to suffer from high variance. A possible solution to this problem is the redistribution of rewards through credit assignment, where segments of the CoT trace that are important for arriving at the desirable solution are emphasized by assigning a higher reward. While Monte Carlo sampling can be used to provide an unbiased estimate of intermediate state values, its computational overhead makes it unsuitable for train-time credit assignment in long contexts at high granularity. We introduce RREDCoT (Reward REDistribution for Chain of Thoughts), which utilizes the model itself to approximate the optimal reward redistribution without additional generation. We investigate the advantages of our method compared to MC sampling and several attribution methods. We further analyze several aspects relevant to the construction of the redistribution such as segmentation of CoT traces and state value estimation.

</details>


### [133] [Pretraining Recurrent Networks without Recurrence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06479)
*Akarsh Kumar,Phillip Isola*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出监督记忆训练(SMT)，通过将RNN训练简化为一步记忆转移标签上的监督学习，避免反向传播时间(BPTT)的时序串行和梯度消失/爆炸问题，实现时间并行的RNN训练，在语言建模和像素序列建模任务上优于BPTT。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准BPTT训练RNN存在两个核心问题：一是时序上串行，限制并行化；二是梯度消失/爆炸，难以学习长程依赖关系。需要一种能克服这些局限的新训练方法。

Method: SMT将RNN训练转化为一步记忆转移标签(m_t, x_{t+1}) → m_{t+1}上的监督学习。先训练基于Transformer的编码器，通过预测状态目标获取记忆标签（只保留预测未来所需的过去信息），再让RNN学习这些标签。这解耦了"记住什么"和"如何更新记忆"两个问题。

Result: 在语言建模和像素序列建模等任务上，SMT预训练多种RNN架构的表现优于BPTT；能更好地捕捉长程依赖关系；支持并行训练；任意两个token之间梯度路径长度稳定为O(1)。

Conclusion: SMT使非线性RNN能够并行训练并更好地捕获长程依赖，有望解锁构建时间抽象记忆的大规模模型训练潜力。

Abstract: Training recurrent neural networks (RNNs) requires assigning credit across long sequences of computations. Standard backpropagation through time (BPTT) addresses this problem poorly: it is sequential in time, limiting parallelism, and suffers from vanishing or exploding gradients, making long-range associations difficult to learn. We propose Supervised Memory Training (SMT), a method for training nonlinear RNNs that sidesteps recurrent credit propagation entirely by reducing RNN training to supervised learning on one-step memory transition labels $(m_t, x_{t+1}) \rightarrow m_{t+1}$. SMT acquires these memory labels by training a Transformer-based encoder on a predictive state objective--retaining only information from the past necessary to predict the future. By decoupling what to remember from how to update memory, SMT enables time-parallel RNN training with a stable $O(1)$ length gradient path between any two tokens--without ever unrolling the RNN. We find that SMT outperforms BPTT when pretraining various RNN architectures on tasks like language modeling and pixel sequence modeling. SMT enables nonlinear RNNs to better capture long-range dependencies and train in parallel, potentially unlocking the scaling of models that build temporal abstractions of past experience.

</details>


### [134] [Regret Minimization with Adaptive Opponents in Repeated Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06486)
*Mingyang Liu,Asuman Ozdaglar,Tiancheng Yu,Kaiqing Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出重复策略遗憾（RP-Regret）作为重复博弈中对抗适应性对手的新指标，解决了外部遗憾无法捕捉玩家反事实推理的问题。作者识别了实现亚线性RP-Regret的必要条件，并提出了三种算法（基于优化预言机、凸线性化代理、直接最小化）来应对其非凸性。当所有玩家最小化该遗憾时，可学习子博弈完美均衡，实验表明在Stag-Hunt等博弈中能实现更高效用的合作解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准的外部遗憾（External Regret）在在线学习中无法捕捉重复博弈中自适应对手（能根据历史做出反应）的反事实推理，需要一种新的博弈论指标来衡量玩家在历史响应中的实际效用与事后最优效用之差。

Method: (1) 定义RP-Regret指标，识别亚线性收敛的必要条件（关于比较器策略的变异性及比较器和对手策略的记忆）；(2) 提出三种算法：基于优化预言机、最小化凸线性化替代函数、直接最小化（对手策略缓慢变化时）；(3) 当所有玩家最小化该遗憾时，证明可学习子博弈完美均衡。

Result: 识别了实现亚线性RP-Regret的必要条件；提出了三种应对非凸性的算法；证明了所有玩家最小化RP-Regret时能学习子博弈完美均衡；实验表明该指标在Stag-Hunt博弈中能引导出更合作的解并实现更高效用。

Conclusion: RP-Regret是比外部遗憾更适合重复博弈自适应对手场景的指标，其最小化有助于发现更优均衡（如合作解），尽管存在非凸性挑战，但所提算法提供了可行的优化路径。

Abstract: In this paper, we study regret minimization in repeated games with \emph{adaptive} opponents who can respond based on histories of play. The standard metric of \emph{external regret} in online learning is known to fail to capture such adaptivity. To account for players' counterfactual reasoning, we introduce {\tt Repeated Policy Regret (RP-Regret)}, a game-theoretic metric that measures the difference between the \emph{realized} and the \emph{best-in-hindsight} accumulated utility when all players can \emph{respond} to the history of play. Compared to existing regret notions in this setting, ours is native to repeated game playing, enabling stronger comparators and opponents with fewer constraints, while maintaining the possibility of finding better equilibria when all players minimize it. We first identify necessary conditions for obtaining {\tt RP-Regret} sublinear in time, on the variation of the player's comparator strategies in the regret definition and on the memories of both the comparator and opponents' strategies. We then study additional conditions and provable algorithms to minimize {\tt RP-Regret}, which is by definition \emph{non-convex} in the strategy space. To address this challenge, we propose three algorithms: (i) one based on an optimization oracle, as assumed in some prior work in online non-convex learning; (ii) one that minimizes a convex and \emph{linearized} surrogate of {\tt RP-Regret} at each iteration; (iii) one that directly minimizes {\tt RP-Regret} when opponents change strategies slowly. Furthermore, when all players can run algorithms to minimize the {\tt RP-Regret} (or its linearized variant), certain subgame perfect equilibria of the repeated game can be learned. We also provide experiments showing that minimizing our regret notions can lead to more cooperative solutions with higher utility in games such as Stag-Hunt.

</details>


### [135] [TailLoR: Protecting Principal Components in Parameter-Efficient Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06494)
*Marius Dragoi,Ioana Pintilie,Alexandra Dragomir,Antonio Barbalau,Florin Brad*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TailLoR是一种基于谱分解的参数高效持续学习方法，通过利用预训练权重的奇异基作为固定参考框架，对奇异值矩阵进行低秩更新，并施加软谱惩罚以减少干扰，将细粒度适应引导到长尾谱坐标中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于谱分解的参数高效微调方法在持续学习中取得了进展，但仍存在灾难性干扰问题，需要一种能有效减少干扰同时保持灵活适应能力的方法。

Method: 利用预训练权重的奇异基U和V作为固定参考框架，学习对奇异值矩阵的低秩更新；同时引入软谱惩罚机制，抑制与主导奇异方向对齐的更新，将细粒度适应引导到高度灵活的长尾谱坐标中。

Result: 通过软谱惩罚有效减少了持续学习中的干扰，同时将细粒度适应引导到长尾谱坐标中，实现了高效的参数更新。

Conclusion: TailLoR通过谱分解和软谱惩罚策略，为持续学习提供了一种有效的参数高效微调方法，平衡了干扰减少与适应灵活性。

Abstract: Parameter-efficient finetuning methods based on spectral decomposition have enabled progress in Continual Learning. In this paper we introduce TailLoR, which utilizes the singular bases U and V of the pre-trained weights as a fixed reference frame to learn a low-rank update applied to the singular value matrix. A soft spectral penalty discourages updates aligned with dominant singular directions, reducing interference while routing fine-grained adaptation into the highly flexible, long-tail spectral coordinates.

</details>


<div id='cs.ET'></div>

# cs.ET [[Back]](#toc)

### [136] [RH+: Row-Hit-Optimized Scheduling for PIM-based LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05511)
*Yongchan Jung,Shafayat Mowla Anik,Byeong Kil Lee,Jeeho Ryoo*

Main category: cs.ET

TL;DR: 针对处理-in-memory（PIM）架构上的大语言模型推理，本文发现DRAM行周期时间（nRC）比之前认为的瓶颈参数nCCDAB大10-11倍，导致先前针对nCCDAB的优化在GEMV操作中无效。作者提出RH+调度策略，通过改变步长使32个连续MAC操作保持在同一行内，实现了8-12倍加速、74%以上能耗降低和高达52倍的EDP改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前工作将功率约束时序参数nCCDAB识别为PIM架构下LLM推理的主要性能瓶颈，但本文发现对于自回归解码中占主导的GEMV操作，DRAM行周期时间nRC远大于nCCDAB（10-11倍），导致nCCDAB完全被掩盖，因此nCCDAB-focused的优化策略对这些负载无效。

Method: 通过分析发现根因是继承自主机端的地址交错（host-centric address interleaving）导致每个全组MAC命令都进入不同的DRAM行。提出RH+调度策略，采用简单的步长改变（stride change），使32个连续的MAC操作保持在相同的DRAM行内。

Result: 在四个LLM工作负载上的周期精确仿真表明，RH+实现了8-12倍的加速比，超过74%的能效降低，以及高达52倍的EDP（能量延迟积）改进。

Conclusion: 识别了先前PIM优化工作的局限，并提出了有效的RH+调度策略，显著提升了PIM架构上LLM推理的性能和能效。

Abstract: Large language model inference on processing-in-memory (PIM) architectures promises to break the memory wall by performing multiply-accumulate (MAC) operations directly within HBM3 DRAM banks. Prior work identifies the power constraint timing parameter nCCDAB as the primary performance bottleneck and optimizes scheduling accordingly. We demonstrate that for GEMV operations that dominate autoregressive decoding, the DRAM row cycle time (nRC) is 10 to 11 times larger than nCCDAB. Consequently, nCCDAB is entirely masked, rendering prior nCCDAB-focused optimizations ineffective for these workloads. The root cause is inherited host-centric address interleaving, which forces every all-bank MAC command into a different DRAM row. We propose RH+ scheduling, a simple stride change that keeps 32 consecutive MAC operations within the same row. Cycle-accurate simulation across four LLM workloads shows that RH+ delivers 8-12x speedup, over 74% energy reduction, and up to 52x EDP improvement.

</details>


### [137] [Wave Focusing in Metamaterials: Tactile Displays Beyond the Diffraction Limit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05572)
*Gregory Reardon,Max Linnander,Dustin Goetz,Neeli Tummala,Yon Visell*

Main category: cs.ET

TL;DR: 本文提出通过局域共振超材料板操控机械波色散关系，突破衍射极限，在触觉频率下实现高分辨率虚拟触觉像素的聚焦，并将像素面积减小十倍，支持多点独立触觉反馈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布式触觉显示器面临波衍射限制，无法在触觉频率下形成空间尺度适合多指交互的局部化虚拟触觉像素，亟需一种能突破衍射极限的聚焦技术。

Method: 在柔性板表面集成机械谐振器晶格，形成局域共振超材料板；通过谐振器与板动态模式的耦合改变色散关系，引入慢波分支以实现超衍射极限聚焦；利用数值模拟设计色散关系，并制造实验装置验证。

Result: 实验表明超材料触觉显示器生成的虚拟像素比无谐振器板更局域化，像素面积减小十倍；系统能传递感知上局域化的单点和多点触觉反馈及移动触觉源，同时保持多点独立波形控制。

Conclusion: 该方法可利用少量驱动自由度实现高分辨率触觉显示器，适用于广泛的应用场景。

Abstract: We address the challenge of engineering distributed haptic displays capable of reproducing multiple localized, independently addressable vibrations -- representing virtual tactile pixels -- at arbitrary locations on a surface. Our technique is based on the focusing of mechanical waves in a flexural plate using a sparse set of actuators. At tactile frequencies, wave diffraction prevents the formation of localized virtual tactile pixels at spatial scales relevant for multi-digit touch interactions. We overcome this limitation by augmenting the plate with a lattice of mechanical resonators, forming a locally resonant metamaterial plate. Coupling between the plate's dynamic modes and those of the resonators alters the dispersion relation governing wave transmission, introducing a slow-wave branch that enables focusing beyond the diffraction limit imposed by the unmodified plate. We use numerical simulations to engineer the dispersion relation of the metamaterial system for high-resolution focusing at tactile frequencies. We then fabricate a metamaterial tactile display and experimentally demonstrate virtual pixels that are far more localized than those generated on an otherwise identical plate without resonators, resulting in a tenfold reduction in virtual-pixel area. In behavioral experiments, we show that this system can deliver perceptually localized single- and multi-point tactile feedback and moving tactile sources while maintaining independent control over temporal waveforms at multiple display locations. The methods reported here can enable high-resolution haptic displays for widespread applications using a small number of actuated degrees of freedom.

</details>


<div id='cs.HC'></div>

# cs.HC [[Back]](#toc)

### [138] [AppAgent-Claw: CLI Is All You Need for GUI Automation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05171)
*Zhixue Song,Zhiheng Zhang,Yi Song,Chi Zhang*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: AppAgent-Claw 通过“记录一次，重复多次”的范式将 GUI 工作流转化为可靠、可复用的 OpenClaw 技能，无需运行时 LLM 推理，采用分层定位策略和验证耦合执行模型来处理视觉变化并确保屏幕效果的正确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OpenClaw 平台的技能导向架构虽能通过 CLI 高效调用外部能力，但许多实际任务仍被限制在无稳定 API 的 GUI 中。基于 LLM 的 GUI 代理虽然通用，但依赖重复的实时模型推理，导致速度慢、成本高、不一致，不适合作为 OpenClaw 技能。

Method: 采用“记录一次，重复多次”的范式，捕获丰富的上下文元数据以支持鲁棒执行；使用分层定位策略处理视觉变化；采用验证耦合执行模型确保预期的屏幕效果。

Result: AppAgent-Claw 为将 GUI 绑定的任务集成到 OpenClaw 生态系统中提供了实用、高效、可诊断的解决方案。

Conclusion: AppAgent-Claw 通过消除运行时 LLM 推理，将 GUI 工作流转换为可靠、可复用的技能，有效解决了基于 LLM 的 GUI 代理在速度、成本和一致性方面的缺陷，为 OpenClaw 平台扩展了 GUI 自动化能力。

Abstract: The OpenClaw platform provides a practical foundation for automation through its skill-oriented architecture, organizing external capabilities into lightweight, reusable components that can be invoked efficiently through a command-line interface (CLI). However, a significant bottleneck remains: many real-world tasks are confined to graphical user interfaces (GUIs) with no stable API available. While LLM-based GUI agents offer generality, their reliance on repeated live model inference makes them too slow, costly, and inconsistent to serve as efficient OpenClaw skills. In this paper, we present AppAgent-Claw, a demonstration-driven system that converts GUI workflows into reliable, reusable skills without runtime inference. By following a ``record-once, replay-many'' paradigm, the system captures rich contextual metadata to facilitate robust execution. It employs a layered localization strategy to handle visual shifts and a validation-coupled execution model to ensure intended on-screen effects. AppAgent-Claw provides a practical, efficient, and diagnosable solution for integrating GUI-bound tasks into the OpenClaw ecosystem.

</details>


### [139] [Is This Edit Correct? A Multi-Dimensional Benchmark for Reasoning-Aware Image Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05172)
*Yixuan Ding,Wei Huang,Ruijie Quan,Xiaojuan Qi,Yi Yang*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出了RE-Edit基准，从物理、环境、文化、因果和指代五个推理维度评估图像编辑系统的隐式逻辑约束推理能力。实验表明现有系统即使生成高质量视觉结果，也常无法满足多维隐式推理需求，并提出了轻量级推理引导后编辑基线作为初步缓解方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散模型图像编辑系统虽然能实现高视觉保真度，但大多仅执行表面指令跟随，无法理解用户请求中隐含的上下文约束，导致编辑结果视觉合理但逻辑不一致。

Method: 构建RE-Edit基准，包含1,000个精心策划的样本，覆盖物理、环境、文化、因果和指代五个推理维度；设计维度对齐的评估标准；提出轻量级推理引导后编辑基线作为模型无关的解决方案。

Result: 对十个开源和两个商业图像编辑模型的全面评估显示，即使先进系统在生成高质量视觉结果的同时，也频繁在隐式多维推理上失败。

Conclusion: 当前图像编辑系统在隐式逻辑约束推理方面存在显著不足，引入显式推理机制有助于缓解这类失败。

Abstract: Diffusion-based image editing has achieved strong visual fidelity under natural language instructions, yet most existing systems still operate at the level of surface instruction following, without reasoning about the implicit contextual constraints embedded in real user requests. This often leads to visually plausible but logically inconsistent edits. In this work, we introduce RE-Edit, a benchmark for REasoning-aware image Editing that evaluates image editing systems across five complementary reasoning dimensions: physical, environmental, cultural, causal, and referential. RE-Edit comprises 1,000 carefully curated samples, each designed such that visual plausibility alone is insufficient and correct editing requires satisfying implicit logical constraints. To support fine-grained analysis, we establish dimension-aligned evaluation criteria and conduct a comprehensive study of ten open-source and two commercial image editing models. Our results show that even advanced systems frequently struggle with implicit multi-dimensional reasoning despite producing high-quality visuals. We further present a lightweight reasoning-guided post-edit baseline as an initial exploration, illustrating how inserting explicit reasoning can help mitigate such failures in a model-agnostic manner.

</details>


### [140] [The Virtual Roundtable: Multi-Agent Personas Simulating the Dynamics of Human Brainstorming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05178)
*Tim Dorn,Saara A. Khan,Julie Mumford*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 提出了一种多智能体架构，通过发散-收敛两阶段模拟圆桌头脑风暴，利用多样化AI角色和智能体引导者产生多样化的创新想法，并以AI智能眼镜案例验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统人类头脑风暴面临群体思维、信息茧房和多样性不足等挑战，而AI驱动的产品开发瓶颈正从"如何构建"转向"构建什么"。因此需要一种自动化、多样化的创意生成方法。

Method: 构建多智能体架构，包含：(1)发散思维阶段——多样化AI角色参与圆桌讨论，各自持有私有想法并公开评论；(2)收敛思维阶段——评估并排序最有前景的想法。系统配备智能体引导者把控讨论方向，通过每个角色的提交配额和投票配额促进参与平衡，并持续追踪每个想法的演化谱系。

Result: 通过AI智能眼镜的案例研究，证明该方法能：(i)产生多样化、相关的创意，并提供演化过程的洞察；(ii)角色间的累积观点交流培育了共享语境，逐步深化讨论质量和产出想法。

Conclusion: 多智能体模拟的头脑风暴可以有效克服人类头脑风暴的固有缺陷，在创意多样性和质量方面表现良好，尤其适合AI驱动的产品开发场景。

Abstract: As AI-driven product development accelerates, the bottleneck is shifting from how we build to what we build. Traditional human brainstorming faces challenges including groupthink, echo chambers, and limited diversity. To address this, we present a multi-agentic architecture that simulates roundtable brainstorming through two phases: divergent thinking to generate diverse ideas, and convergent thinking to evaluate and rank the most promising ones. The system employs diverse AI personas that engage in roundtable discussions, guided by an agentic facilitator that steers the discussion toward productive outcomes. Personas maintain private thoughts while commenting publicly, with ideas emerging organically throughout the discussion. Per-persona quotas on idea submissions and votes promote balanced participation while producing natural rankings. Throughout the session, the system tracks each idea's lineage, capturing how concepts originate and cross-pollinate over time. We demonstrate this approach through a case study generating consumer ideas for AI smart glasses, showing (i) it produces diverse, relevant ideas with insights into their evolution; (ii) the cumulative exchange of perspectives across personas cultivates a shared context that progressively deepens the quality of discussion and the ideas produced.

</details>


### [141] [The Role of Instructional Guidance in Generative AI-Assisted Learning: Empirical Evidence from Construction Engineering Education](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05509)
*Xiaoyu Hou,Bo Xiao,Hexu Liu,Shane Mueller*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本研究提出一个基于生成学习理论（GLT）的五步提示框架，通过对照实验比较了幻灯片学习、无提示AI支持和有提示AI支持三种条件对建筑教育学习效果的影响。结果显示，有提示条件在需要解释和推理的开放式任务中显著优于其他两组（提高约2-3分/18分制，p<0.01），而选择题表现无显著差异，无提示组与幻灯片学习组相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI越来越多地用于支持自主学习，但学生与这类系统的互动往往缺乏结构性，限制了深层认知过程的参与。本研究旨在探究如何通过教学引导来塑造学生与AI的互动，以提升学习效果。

Method: 基于生成学习理论（GLT）提出五步提示框架；通过控制实验比较三种条件：幻灯片学习、无提示AI支持和有提示AI支持；采用多选题和开放式任务评估学习表现，使用UEQ问卷测量用户体验。

Result: 有提示条件在开放式任务中显著优于其他两组（提高约2-3分/18分制，p<0.01）；多选题表现无显著差异；无提示组与幻灯片学习组表现相当。

Conclusion: AI支持学习的有效性取决于互动如何被结构化；所提出的框架为将学习科学原则整合到建筑教育中的生成式AI系统提供了基础。

Abstract: Generative artificial intelligence (AI) is increasingly used to support self-directed learning, yet student interaction with such systems often remains unstructured, limiting engagement in deeper cognitive processes. This study examines how instructional guidance shapes student and AI interaction in construction education. A five-step prompting framework grounded in Generative Learning Theory (GLT) is introduced to guide learner interaction during review activities. A controlled experiment compares three learning conditions: slide-based learning, unprompted AI-supported learning, and prompted AI-supported learning. Learning performance is assessed using multiple-choice and open-ended tasks, and user experience is measured using the User Experience Questionnaire (UEQ). Performance differences are concentrated on tasks requiring explanation and reasoning. The prompted condition achieves higher open-ended scores, with an improvement of approximately 2 or 3 points on a scale of 18 (p < 0.01), while no significant differences are observed in multiple-choice performance. The unprompted condition remains comparable to slide-based learning. These findings indicate that the effectiveness of AI-supported learning depends on how interaction is structured. The proposed framework provides a basis for integrating learning science principles into generative AI systems for construction education.

</details>


### [142] [Architecting Strategic Influence: Operationalising the UXR Point of View Framework for Research Function Maturity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05826)
*Rohinin Singh,Renee Barsoum*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文通过案例研究展示了用户研究（UXR）职能从孵化期向成熟期过渡时，系统应用UXR POV框架作为有效的运营支撑结构，提出包含"进攻性"和"防御性"策略的Playbook，帮助UXR领导者克服利益相关者偏见、被动任务分配和洞察碎片化等体制障碍，从而将研究职能从战术执行提升为战略合作伙伴。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: UXR职能在从孵化期向成熟期过渡时面临利益相关者偏见、被动任务分配和洞察碎片化等系统性问题，缺乏系统化的运营框架来指导这一关键转型。

Method: 采用案例研究方法，系统应用UXR POV框架，结合"进攻性"（Offensive）和"防御性"（Defensive）策略，构建了一个可适应的Playbook工具包。

Result: 应用该Playbook驱动的成熟度策略能够显著提升研究职能成为战略合作伙伴的可能性，使其能够提供基于证据的、可操作的见解。

Conclusion: 刻意、主动地应用成长策略，能够帮助UXR职能超越战术执行层面，直接塑造长期业务战略。

Abstract: This case study illustrates that the systematic application of the User Experience Research (UXR) Point of View (POV) framework serves as an effective operational scaffolding for a UXR function undergoing the critical transition from incubation to maturity. By assimilating structured 'Offensive' and 'Defensive' strategies, the presented Playbook equips UXR leaders with an adaptable toolkit to systematically navigate common institutional barriers, such as stakeholder bias, reactive tasking, and insight fragmentation. By pre-emptive and purposeful application of growth strategies, the likelihood of the research function establishing itself as a strategic partner capable of delivering evidence-based, actionable perspectives is significantly enhanced. The analysis demonstrates how this deliberate, Playbook-driven maturity strategy empowers research functions to move beyond tactical execution and directly shape long-term business strategy.

</details>


### [143] [EEGDancer: Dynamic Emotion Latent Space Masked Modeling with Reinforcement Learning for EEG Continuous Emotion Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05855)
*Zhihao Zhou,Weishan Ye,Li Zhang,Gan Huang,Zhen Liang*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 提出EEGDancer框架，结合VQ-VAE、掩码时序建模和强化学习轨迹优化，用于连续脑电图情绪预测，在多个数据集上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有连续EEG情绪预测方法主要依赖逐点回归和高维EEG特征直接建模，难以捕捉长时间依赖和连贯的情绪动态演化。

Method: 设计因果时空VQ-VAE学习结构化情绪原型并构建离散-连续情绪潜空间；基于Transformer的掩码动态建模捕捉长时间依赖；将连续情绪预测建模为序列决策问题，引入SAC框架进行序列级轨迹优化。

Result: 在SEED、SEED-IV和长期自然情感数据集上，EEGDancer一致优于现有机器学习和深度学习方法；消融实验验证了潜空间和RL轨迹优化的有效性。

Conclusion: EEGDancer通过潜空间学习和序列级轨迹优化，有效解决了连续EEG情绪预测中长时依赖和动态演化建模的挑战。

Abstract: Continuous electroencephalography (EEG) emotion prediction aims to model the temporal evolution of human emotional states from EEG signals. Unlike conventional discrete emotion recognition, continuous prediction requires capturing long-range temporal dependencies and coherent emotional dynamics. However, existing methods mainly rely on point-wise regression and directly model noisy high-dimensional EEG features, limiting their ability to characterize continuous emotional evolution.To address these challenges, we propose EEGDancer, a dynamic emotional latent space learning framework for continuous EEG emotion prediction. The framework integrates vector-quantized representation learning, masked temporal modeling, and reinforcement learning-based trajectory optimization into a unified architecture.Specifically, a causal spatiotemporal Vector-Quantization Variational Autoencoder (VQ-VAE) is designed to learn structured emotional prototypes and construct a discrete-continuous emotional latent space from EEG signals. Based on the learned latent representations, a Transformer-based masked dynamic modeling strategy captures long-range emotional dependencies and temporal evolution patterns. Furthermore, continuous emotion prediction is formulated as a sequential decision-making problem, and a Soft Actor-Critic (SAC) framework is introduced to optimize emotional prediction trajectories at the sequence level instead of frame-wise local fitting.Extensive experiments on the SEED, SEED-IV, and Long-Term Naturalistic Emotion datasets demonstrate that EEGDancer consistently outperforms existing machine learning and deep learning methods. Ablation studies further verify the effectiveness of the proposed latent space and reinforcement learning-based trajectory optimization for modeling continuous EEG emotional dynamics.

</details>


### [144] [Empathy on Demand: How Empathic AI Can Scale Emotional Support for Verbal Harassment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05995)
*Anouk Bergner,Philipp Winder,Christian Hildebrand*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文研究了语言骚扰中AI的共情倾听效果，发现大语言模型在共情倾听信号（观点采择、情感验证、行动导向）上表现优于人类非专家和心理健康专业人员，能提升受害者的被倾听感和应对效能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言骚扰日益成为全球性心理压力源，但许多人缺乏及时有效的支持；人们越来越多地转向AI寻求情感支持，因此需要检验AI能否提供类似人类共情的心理益处。

Method: 首先开发了一个包含三种共情倾听语言信号（观点采择、情感验证、行动取向）的心理框架；然后比较了LLM、人类非专家和心理健康专业人员在这些信号上的表现；最后通过行为实验验证这些信号对受害者被倾听感和应对自我效能的影响。

Result: LLM在共情倾听信号上持续优于人类非专家和训练有素的心理健康专业人员；这些信号能促进更趋近导向（而非回避导向）的应对策略，并提升受害者的被倾听感和应对自我效能。

Conclusion: 特定的语言特征能够在人类与高级对话AI之间建立共情连接，增强人们的心理韧性；AI有潜力作为可扩展的情感支持来源，尤其是在人类支持不可用或不足时。

Abstract: Verbal harassment is a growing source of psychological stress for people around the world. It occurs both online and offline and relies on language to demean, threaten, or discredit its targets. Unlike other stressors such as loss or uncertainty, verbal harassment aims at silencing its targets by eroding their sense of being heard and weakening their perceived ability to respond. Many individuals lack access to adequate and timely support, however, when they experience such harassment. People increasingly turn to conversational artificial intelligence (AI) such as ChatGPT or dedicated AI companions for emotional support, raising questions about whether it can facilitate the same psychological benefits as actual human empathy. We focus on online contexts as a prevalent application of verbal harassment. We develop and test a psychological framework identifying three key linguistic signals of empathic listening (perspective-taking, emotional validation, and action orientation), that together restore a sense of feeling heard and enhance coping in the context of verbal harassment. We find that LLMs consistently produce language exhibiting stronger empathic-listening markers than human non-experts and trained mental health professionals, promoting more approach-oriented (vs. avoidance-oriented) coping strategies. A subsequent behavioral study shows that these linguistic signals boost recipients' sense of feeling heard and increase their coping self-efficacy. These findings reveal how specific linguistic features create empathic connections between humans and advanced conversational AI and can enhance people's psychological resilience. Our results highlight the potential for AI to serve as a scalable source of emotional support, especially when human support is unavailable or insufficient.

</details>


### [145] [Deterring Searches for Child Sexual Abuse Material on Google Search and Promoting Help-Seeking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06126)
*Rebecca Umbach,Griffin Hunt,John Buckley,Joel Scanlan,Caoilte Ó Ciardha,Ethel Quayle,Ainslie Heasman,Maximlian von Heyden,Elizabeth Letourneau,Donald Findlater,Tegan Insoll,Richard Wortley,Chad Steel,Abhishek Roy*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: Google搜索在用户查询儿童性虐待材料时部署"Onebox"功能。研究发现，将此前侧重于举报的干预措施调整为强调后果和治疗资源的新版本后，在相同搜索会话中后续CSAM相关查询减少了3.8个百分点，且帮助资源链接的平均点击率为0.73%。这表明部分个体可以被劝阻继续搜索CSAM并转向治疗服务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估Google搜索中针对CSAM查询的"Onebox"干预措施从侧重举报转向强调后果与治疗资源的战略调整效果，探究能否有效减少后续CSAM搜索行为并引导用户获取帮助。

Method: 采用双重差分法分析Google内部搜索日志数据，比较新旧两种干预措施在相同搜索会话中对后续CSAM相关查询的影响，同时统计帮助资源超链接按钮的平均点击率。

Result: 新干预措施使相同搜索会话中后续CSAM相关查询比旧版本减少3.8个百分点；帮助资源链接的平均点击率为0.73%。

Conclusion: 部分主动搜索CSAM的个体可以被有效劝阻并引导至治疗服务，提示以后果和治疗资源为核心的干预策略在减少有害搜索行为方面具有实际效果。

Abstract: Google Search deploys a "Onebox" feature at the top of the results page when users conduct searches for Child Sexual Abuse Material. This study evaluates the impact of a strategic shift in this feature, comparing a revised intervention, focused on repercussions and therapeutic resources, to a previous iteration that focused on reporting. Using a difference-in-differences analysis of internal Google Search logs data, we found the new messaging resulted in a 3.8 percentage point reduction as compared to the status quo in subsequent CSAM-related queries within the same Search session. We found an average click through rate of 0.73% on any of the hyperlinked buttons to help-providing resources. Together, this research presents convergent evidence that a subset of individuals can be deterred from ongoing CSAM-seeking and redirected to therapeutic services.

</details>


### [146] [A MATLAB Toolbox for Standardized Reading Speed Assessment: Implementing and Extending the Perrin Sentence Generator for English Corpora](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06297)
*Daniel P. Spiegel,Romain Bachy*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文针对静态阅读范式在测量阅读速度时的记忆效应和言语运动混杂问题，开发了一个基于MATLAB的开源工具箱。该工具改编自Perrin等人(2014)的句子生成范式，利用语义本体和"原型真理"逻辑自动生成数千个语法简单、真值明确的英语句子，并引入了带通心理语言学过滤和英语语义消歧逻辑，为科学界提供开放的阅读速度测量平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统静态阅读图表在测量阅读速度时存在两个关键缺陷：重复测试导致的记忆效应，以及口头复述引入的言语运动混杂因素，这些障碍掩盖了真实的信息处理速度，亟需一种新的方法论工具来克服这些问题。

Method: 基于MATLAB开发开源工具箱，改编Perrin, Paillé和Baccino (2014)的句子生成范式到英语环境；利用语义本体和"原型真理"逻辑自动生成大量语法简单的句子；引入带通心理语言学过滤和针对英语特有的语义消歧逻辑。

Result: 成功开发出一个能够自动生成数以千计独特、语法简单且真值明确的英语句子的开源软件包，同时实现了最大阅读速度(MRS)测量以及英语语义歧义解决功能。

Conclusion: 该开源工具箱为科学界提供了一个可验证和完善的开放平台，有望推动阅读速度测量方法的标准化和可比性，减少传统范式的混杂变量。

Abstract: In the fields of vision science, cognitive psychology, and psycholinguistics, the accurate measurement of reading speed is frequently hampered by the limitations of static reading charts. Repeated testing often leads to memorization effects, while the requirement for oral recitation introduces speech-motor confounds that obscure true information processing speed. To address these methodological hurdles, this paper introduces an open-source MATLAB toolbox that adapts the sentence generation paradigm originally proposed by Perrin, Paillé, and Baccino (2014) for the English language. This system utilizes a semantic ontology and a "proto-truth" logic to autonomously generate thousands of unique, grammatically simple sentences with unambiguous truth values. Beyond the original scope of Maximum Reading Speed (MRS) measurement, this implementation introduces band-pass psycholinguistic filtering and specific logic to resolve semantic ambiguities unique to English. We present this complete software package as an open platform for the scientific community to validate and refine.

</details>


### [147] [Computational Modeling of Human Adaptation in Urban Infrastructure Management under Extreme Conditions: A Case Study of Subway Flood Scenarios](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06429)
*Jinfeng Lou,Zijie Liang,Pengkun Liu,Yuxin Zhang,Cleotilde Gonzalez,Pingbo Tang*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本研究将实例学习理论（IBLT）整合到土木工程计算中，构建了模拟操作员决策的计算认知架构，并通过地铁洪水导致轨道中断的人机实验，揭示了人类操作员在面对极端事件时存在获取、过度自信、过度校正和重新校准四个非线性适应阶段，其中过度校正阶段的心理偏差会覆盖稳定的记忆驱动决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前城市基础设施管理中的计算支持系统未能考虑人类的非单调适应过程和潜在的心理学偏差（如过度自信和防御性过度校正），导致极端事件下的决策支持不足。

Method: 将实例学习理论（IBLT）整合到土木工程计算中，建立基于记忆检索和效用混合数学机制的计算认知架构，作为有界理性适应的计算基线；通过人机微波世界实验（模拟地铁洪水导致的轨道中断），让调度员在乘客安全和服务效率之间权衡。

Result: 揭示了人类操作员存在复杂的非线性适应循环（获取→过度自信→过度校正→重新校准四个阶段）；计算模型在事后"过度校正"阶段与人类行为出现显著分歧：人类表现出即时的防御性风险高估，而模型维持基于累积经验的稳定轨迹。

Conclusion: 故障后的操作不稳定性往往源于急性心理偏差覆盖了稳定的记忆驱动适应，这一模式在类似高风险环境中预计具有可重复性，并可通过多模态行为和传感器数据验证。

Abstract: Decision-making in urban infrastructure management during extreme events relies heavily on human operators, yet current computational support systems often fail to account for non-monotonic human adaptation and latent psychological biases like overconfidence and defensive overcorrection. This study addresses this gap by integrating Instance-Based Learning Theory (IBLT) into the domain of civil engineering computing. We establish a computational cognitive architecture that simulates operator decision processes through the mathematical mechanisms of memory retrieval and utility blending. This model functions as a computational baseline, representing boundedly rational adaptation driven by experiential priors, thus allowing for the algorithmic isolation of latent psychological biases from the baseline dynamics of memory-based learning. We demonstrated this framework using a human-in-the-loop microworld experiment simulating subway flood-induced track suspensions, where dispatchers must balance passenger safety against service efficiency. Analysis revealed a complex, non-linear human adaptation cycle consisting of four phases: acquisition, overconfidence, overcorrection, and recalibration. Specifically, the computational model exposed a significant divergence during the post-accident "overcorrection" phase: while human operators exhibited immediate, defensive risk overestimation, the model maintained a stable trajectory based on accumulated experience. This strategic divergence confirms that operational instability following failure is often attributable to acute psychological bias overriding stable memory-based adaptation, a pattern theoretically expected to recur across analogous high-stakes environments and validatable through multi-modal behavioral and sensor data from professional operators.

</details>


<div id='cs.CR'></div>

# cs.CR [[Back]](#toc)

### [148] [Domain-Conditioned Safety in Frontier Computer-Using Agents: A 793-Episode Browser Benchmark, a Coding-Domain Cross-Reference, and a Reproducibility Audit of Recent Red-Teaming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05233)
*Nicholas Saban*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文发现，针对计算机使用代理（CUA）的提示注入攻击，现有文献报告的高成功率（42-98%）主要源于RL优化的对抗文本，而非攻击类别本身。作者构建了CUA-HandCrafted基准测试（793个episode、24个多步网页任务、56个攻击模板），在Claude Sonnet 4.6和GPT-5.4上测量到手写模板的多步攻击成功率为0/140（95%置信上限2.60%），证明前沿模型已对该领域具备鲁棒性，但同一权重在编码代理基准（SkillBench）上技能注入成功率高达100%，说明安全加固具有领域条件性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CUA红队论文报告的提示注入攻击成功率（42-98%）集中在已退役模型和每个论文面板中最脆弱的模型上，缺乏对当前前沿模型手写攻击模板有效性的系统性评估。

Method: 构建CUA-HandCrafted基准，包含793个episode、24个多步网页任务、56个攻击模板（8个攻击家族）、4种系统提示配置。在Claude Sonnet 4.6和GPT-5.4上进行系统性测试，并进行提示消融实验（对比系统提示嵌入vs模型权重内置）。

Result: 手写模板多步攻击成功率为0/140（Clopper-Pearson 95%上限2.60%）；提示消融实验显示鲁棒性来自模型权重而非系统提示；同一权重在编码代理基准上技能注入成功率高达100%。

Conclusion: 文献中高ASR主要归因于RL优化的注入文本而非攻击类别本身；前沿模型的安全加固具有领域条件性（特定于浏览器表面）；不发布优化字符串或跨领域外推ASR数字导致结果不可复现。
