Abstract: Recent computer-using-agent (CUA) red-teaming papers report prompt-injection attack success rates (ASR) of 42-98%, but these headline numbers cluster on retired models and on the most-vulnerable model in each paper's panel. We ask whether those techniques, reproduced as hand-crafted templates, still work against current frontier CUAs. We release CUA-HandCrafted, a public benchmark of 793 episodes spanning 24 multi-step web tasks, 56 attack templates, 8 attack families, and 4 system-prompt configurations. Against Claude Sonnet 4.6 and GPT-5.4 we measure 0/140 multi-step attack success (Clopper-Pearson 95% upper bound 2.60%); a prompt ablation shows this resistance lives in the model weights. Yet it does not generalize: on a sister coding-agent benchmark (SkillBench), the same weights fall to hand-crafted skill-injection at up to 100%. We argue that the literature's high ASR is largely attributable to RL-optimized injection text rather than the attack categories, and that frontier safety hardening is domain-conditioned, specific to the heavily-targeted browser surface. Reporting techniques without releasing the optimized strings, or extrapolating browser-domain safety to other CUA modalities, makes published ASR numbers unreproducible.

</details>


### [149] [CRESS: Quantifying Vulnerabilities of Attack Scenarios in Hardware Reverse Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05459)
*Alexander Hepp,Matthias Ludwig,Michaela Brunner,Johanna Baehr,Georg Sigl*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了CRESS评分系统的定量化扩展，通过专家访谈得出权重公式，实现对硬件逆向工程相关攻击场景的严重性量化评分，相比行业标准CVSS更具表现力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微电子系统的安全性和可靠性依赖于可信的供应链和设计流程，但全球分布的供应链或设计漏洞为硬件层面的攻击（如伪造、硬件木马、设备攻击）提供了可乘之机。硬件逆向工程（RE）在这些攻击中扮演关键角色。已有定性CRESS系统，但缺乏量化能力，无法对场景进行精确评级和比较，亟需定量化扩展。

Method: 对领域专家进行了广泛的访谈研究，基于访谈结果推导出不同RE攻击类别的权重，形成量化方程，输出CRESS评分。同时通过六个案例研究进行定性和定量评级对比。

Result: 六个案例研究验证了量化CRESS评分的有效性，结果表明CRESS评分比行业标准通用漏洞评分系统（CVSS）具有显著更高的表现力。

Conclusion: 将CRESS从定性系统扩展为定量系统，实现了对逆向工程相关攻击场景的一致、可比较的严重性评分，有助于开发有效的防御措施。

Abstract: The safety, security, and reliability of microelectronic systems depend on a trustworthy, secured supply chain and design flow. Globally distributed supply chains or unintentional design weaknesses leave the door open for attacks on the hardware level. These scenarios encompass counterfeiting, hardware trojans, or on-device attacks. For these, hardware reverse engineering (RE) results play a pivotal role. The ongoing publication of new RE-involved attacks motivated the development of the common RE scoring system (CRESS). The system enables a general classification of RE-involved scenarios for a common, consistent rating. In this work, the originally qualitative system is extended to a quantitative system. We performed an extensive interview study with experts in the field. The interview results allowed us to derive weights that measure the severity of different RE-involved attack categories. The weights form an equation that quantifies scenarios, resulting in the severity-indicating CRESS score. The score enables the coherent rating of novel scenarios, renders them comparable, and supports the development of effective countermeasures. To showcase the effectiveness of the quantitative CRESS Score, six selected case studies are rated qualitatively and quantitatively. The CRESS Score proves to be significantly more expressive than the industry-standard Common Vulnerability Scoring System (CVSS).

</details>


### [150] [Search-Time Contamination in Deep Research Agents: Measuring Performance Inflation in Public Benchmark Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05241)
*Yongjie Wang,Xinyue Zhang,Kunhong Yao,Zhiwei Zeng,Kaisong Song,Jun Lin,Zhiqi Shen*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了深度研究智能体在推理过程中通过网络检索导致的搜索时污染（STC）问题，定义了三种污染类型（基准元数据泄露、问题上下文泄露、显式答案泄露），并开发了检测算法。实验表明STC在六个公开基准上普遍存在，可使性能虚增高达4%，现有评估可能高估了真实推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 公开基准对LLM推理的公平评估至关重要，但深度研究智能体会在推理过程中主动搜索网络，可能检索到基准元数据、问题上下文甚至标准答案，导致搜索时污染（STC），使评估结果失真，因此需要系统研究该问题并提出应对方法。

Method: 定义了三种污染类型（基准元数据泄露、问题上下文泄露、显式答案泄露），严重程度递增；开发了相应的检测算法来识别污染并量化其对性能的影响；在六个公开基准上对现代深度研究智能体进行了实证评估。

Result: STC在六个公开基准上普遍存在，可导致性能虚增高达4%。

Conclusion: 现有评估可能高估了深度研究智能体的真实推理能力，因此需要采用污染感知的评估实践，包括隔离沙箱、透明的搜索轨迹和受控的基准访问。

Abstract: Public benchmarks enable fair and reproducible evaluation of LLM reasoning, but they become fragile for deep research agents that actively search the web during inference. Such agents may retrieve public benchmark metadata, question context, or even ground-truth answers via web search. This gives rise to Search-Time Contamination (STC), where external retrieval bypasses intended reasoning and inflates measured performance. We systematically study STC in deep research agent evaluation. We define three contamination types with increasing severity, namely Benchmark Metadata Leakage, Question-Context Leakage, and Explicit Answer Leakage, and develop detection algorithms to identify them and quantify their impact on agent performance. Evaluating modern deep research agents on six public benchmarks, we find that STC is widespread and can inflate performance by up to 4%. Our findings show that existing evaluations may overestimate true reasoning ability. We therefore advocate contamination-aware practices, including isolated sandboxes, transparent search trajectories, and controlled benchmark access.

</details>


### [151] [From Attack Simulation to SIEM Rule: Deterministic Detection-as-Code Synthesis with Probe-Level Traceability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05252)
*Alexandre Cristovão Maiorano*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种从BAS（渗透与攻击模拟）工具发现到可部署Sigma检测规则的部分自动化翻译方法：利用锁定语料库中每个发现都关联稳定探针标识符的特性，通过少量模板库（N=23）实现确定性合成，输出可回溯到原始探针和MITRE ATT&CK技术的规则。在两个锁定语料库（LLM 17探针、Web 23探针）上验证，所有规则均可解析并转换为Splunk和Elasticsearch后端；在OpenSearch SIEM中回放，LLM规则对AdvBench子集检出率30%、HarmBench 14%，假阳性率7.7%。该方法提供了完全可复现、字节稳定的从BAS发现到可部署规则的可验证路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前安全团队使用BAS工具模拟攻击并产生发现，但SIEM系统需要检测规则才能捕获真实入侵者。人工将每个BAS发现翻译成Sigma规则（供应商中立的检测格式）是一个耗时且易出错的过程，存在自动化需求。

Method: 利用锁定语料库（locked corpus）中每个发现都携带可追溯到原始探针的稳定标识符的特性，设计了一个确定性合成函数（deterministic synthesis function），通过小规模模板库（N=23，按OWASP LLM和Web Top 10分类索引）将每个发现映射为一条入门级Sigma规则，并包含回指向原始发现及其MITRE ATT&CK技术的引用。

Result: 在两个锁定语料库（17探针LLM、23探针Web）上，所有绕过探针的发现均生成入门规则，17/17条规则均能解析并转换为Splunk和Elasticsearch后端。在OpenSearch SIEM中回放，LLM规则在AdvBench子集上检出率30%、HarmBench上14%，良性基线假阳性率7.7%；Web端进行了结构性验证。

Conclusion: 本文贡献了一条从BAS发现到运维可部署入门规则的可验证、字节稳定的路径，仅依赖已发布的语料库和模板库即可重新推导——用生成式LLM方法的广度换取了精确可复现性和从告警到原始探针的完整类型化回溯能力。

Abstract: Security teams routinely simulate attacks against their own systems to check whether their monitoring would catch a real intruder. These Breach-and-Attack-Simulation (BAS) tools surface findings, but the security information and event management (SIEM) systems that watch production need detection rules -- and today a human bridges that gap by hand, reading each finding and writing the corresponding Sigma rule (a vendor-neutral detection format). We show this translation can be partially automated when probes are drawn from a locked corpus, so each finding carries a stable identifier back to the originating probe. We describe a deterministic synthesis function that maps each finding to a starter Sigma rule through a small template library (N=23, indexed by categories from the OWASP LLM and Web Top 10), with a back-reference to the originating finding and its MITRE ATT&CK technique. On two locked corpora (17-probe LLM, 23-probe Web), every bypassed-probe finding yields a starter rule, and all 17/17 emitted rules parse and convert to Splunk and Elasticsearch backends. Replayed through a live OpenSearch SIEM, the LLM rules fire on 30% of a held-out AdvBench subset and 14% of HarmBench at 7.7% false positives on a benign baseline; the Web side is validated structurally, not against a held-out attack set. The contribution is a verifiable, byte-stable path from BAS finding to operator-deployable starter rule, re-derivable from the published corpus and template library alone -- trading the breadth of LLM-generative methods for exact reproducibility and a typed traceback from any fired alert to the originating probe.

</details>


### [152] [Willing but Unable: Separating Refusal from Capability in Code LLMs via Abliteration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05396)
*Cristina Carleo,Pietro Liguori,Naghmeh Ivaki,Domenico Cotroneo*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本研究探索了abliteration（一种低秩权重编辑方法）来消除安全对齐代码LLM对CWE注入提示的拒绝行为，从而通过从安全代码中注入指定漏洞来生成带标签的易受攻击代码。实验表明，abliteration可有效消除拒绝，同时保持语法有效性，但注入能力仍受限于模型参数规模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于学习的漏洞检测面临标注数据稀缺和标签噪声问题。现有方案（挖掘或LLM增强）存在局限性，而让指令微调LLM从安全代码中注入指定CWE是一种有前景的替代路径，但安全对齐的代码LLM会系统地拒绝此类提示。因此需要一种方法来解除这一障碍。

Method: 采用abliteration技术——一种低秩权重编辑方法，通过正交投影消除残差流中的拒绝方向。以Python和CWE-89（SQL注入）为案例，在Qwen2.5-Coder-Instruct系列（3B、7B、14B参数）上评估，使用PromSec和SafeCoder数据集中的安全样本，每个条件重复三次。漏洞判定由CodeQL、Semgrep、Bandit三工具集成检测加上两位作者人工判定。

Result: (i) 拒绝行为与模型大小和上下文强相关：14B拒绝100%，7B对PromSec拒绝73%但对SafeCoder仅5%，3B基本不拒绝；(ii) abliteration将拒绝降至零或接近零，语法有效性保持在93%以上；(iii) 注入后成功率受限于模型容量：14B为88-97%，7B为89-90%，3B仅为25-48%。

Conclusion: Abliteration能有效解除安全对齐代码LLM的拒绝障碍，且不损害代码生成能力。但注入漏洞的"意愿"（abliteration解锁）与"能力"（随参数量扩展）是分离的，这表明该方法可行但最终注入质量受模型自身能力限制。

Abstract: Producing a labeled vulnerable code at scale is a recurring obstacle for learning-based vulnerability detection: mined corpora carry substantial label noise, and existing LLM-based augmentation propagates these inaccuracies because it transforms vulnerable seeds rather than synthesising vulnerabilities from a specification. A complementary route is to start from safe code and ask an instruction-tuned LLM to inject a specified CWE (which would shift the labeling burden from open-ended detection to bounded binary confirmation) but safety-aligned code LLMs systematically refuse such prompts. This paper is a preliminary feasibility study of abliteration, a low-rank weight edit that orthogonally projects out the refusal direction in the residual stream, as a tool to remove this barrier. We use Python and CWE-89 (SQL injection) as a case study, evaluating the Qwen2.5-Coder-Instruct family at 3B, 7B, and 14B parameters on safe samples drawn from PromSec and SafeCoder, replicated three times per condition. We find that (i) refusal on injection prompts is strongly size- and prompt-context-dependent: the 14B refuses 100% of prompts, the 7B refuses 73% of PromSec but only 5% of SafeCoder, whereas the 3B is essentially never blocked; (ii) abliteration reduces refusal to zero or near-zero across all sizes while leaving syntactic validity above 93%, supporting the view that, in this setting, refusal can be detached from measured code-generation capability; and (iii) the post-abliteration injection rate remains capacity-bound (88-97% on the 14B, 89-90% on the 7B, and 25-48% on the 3B) separating willingness, which abliteration unlocks, from capability, which scales with parameters. Vulnerability verdicts are produced by a three-tool detector ensemble (CodeQL, Semgrep, Bandit) followed by manual adjudication by two authors on detector-positive outputs.

</details>


### [153] [A formal framework for the economic security of DeFi compositions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05418)
*Massimo Bartoletti,Riccado Marchesin,Roberto Zunino*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出MEV非干扰性（MEV non-interference）作为DeFi组合的安全形式化概念，要求新部署合约集合的最大可提取价值不因与现有区块链状态的交互而增加。为此定义了局部MEV（local MEV）这一聚焦受害者合约损失的经济攻击度量，研究了有界和无界财富两种对抗模型，建立了安全组合的充分条件和局部性原则，并在多种典型DeFi组合上验证了框架的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DeFi服务通常通过组合多种智能合约构建，虽然可组合性是DeFi成功的关键，但也带来了安全风险：对手可能利用新部署合约与已有合约之间的交互造成经济损失。现有方法缺乏形式化的安全概念来刻画这种组合安全风险。

Method: 提出MEV非干扰性作为形式化安全概念；定义局部MEV作为聚焦受害者合约经济损失的度量；建立有界和无界财富两种对抗模型下的充分条件和局部性原则；在交易所、AMM、期权、借贷池、路由器和套利合约等代表性DeFi组合上进行应用验证。

Result: 该框架能够区分安全与非安全的DeFi组合，为DeFi组合的经济安全性推理提供了形式化基础。

Conclusion: 本文为DeFi组合的经济安全建立了形式化基础，通过MEV非干扰性和局部MEV等概念，实现了对DeFi组合安全性的模块化推理，有助于理解和防范组合攻击风险。

Abstract: Decentralized Finance (DeFi) services are usually constructed by composing a variety of smart contracts. While composability is a key driver of the success of DeFi, it also creates security risks: adversaries may exploit interactions between newly deployed contracts and the pre-existing ones to inflict economic losses. We introduce MEV non-interference, a formal security notion for DeFi composability requiring that the maximal extractable value from a set of newly deployed contracts is not increased by interactions with the existing blockchain state. To support this notion, we define local MEV, a novel measure of economic attacks that focusses on the loss of a given set of victim contracts. We study two adversarial models, with bounded and unbounded wealth, and establish sufficient conditions and locality principles that enable modular reasoning about secure composability. We apply the framework to representative DeFi compositions, including exchanges, AMMs, options, lending pools, routers, and arbitrage contracts, showing how it distinguishes secure compositions from vulnerable ones. Our results provide a formal foundation for reasoning about the economic security of DeFi compositions.

</details>


### [154] [Policy-Compliant Cloud Storage Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05423)
*Dimitrios Stavrakakis,Masanori Misono,Julian Pritzi,Harshavardhan Unnibhavi,Nuno Santos,Pramod Bhatotia*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: GDPRuler是一个面向非可信云环境中键值存储（KVS）的信任中间件系统，通过在机密虚拟机（CVM）内部署GDPR监控器，以透明代理方式实现可验证的GDPR合规性，无需修改KVS代码库。该系统通过紧凑的元数据编码、专用索引和高效日志记录，在Redis和RocksDB上以约61%原生吞吐量的代价实现核心合规保障。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GDPR等隐私法规对个人数据的存储、处理和审计提出严格要求，而键值存储（KVS）广泛用于延迟敏感应用，但其简单数据模型和非可信云部署环境使得GDPR合规极具挑战。现有方法存在侵入式代码修改、高性能开销或忽略合规机制本身完整性的问题。

Method: GDPRuler在机密虚拟机（CVM）内部署信任的GDPR监控器来执行GDPR策略、管理合规元数据并维护防篡改审计日志；设计声明式策略语言将核心GDPR义务转化为可执行的运行时规则；采用紧凑的元数据编码嵌入KV记录、构建专用元数据索引加速GDPR查询、以空间高效格式仅记录合规相关事件。

Result: 实现为Redis和RocksDB的透明代理。在YCSB和GDPR负载测试中：GDPRuler达到约61%的原生KVS吞吐量，其中CVM环境贡献28%-32%的开销；元数据存储开销低于20%；GDPR查询通过元数据索引获得13-182倍加速。

Conclusion: 通过将可验证的策略嵌入信任的中间件层，GDPRuler为在非可信云基础设施上实现GDPR合规的KVS提供了一条实用路径。

Abstract: Privacy regulations such as the General Data Protection Regulation (GDPR) impose strict requirements on how personal data is stored, processed, and audited. While key-value stores (KVS) are widely used in latency-sensitive applications, their simple data model and untrusted cloud deployment environments make GDPR compliance particularly challenging. Existing approaches require invasive code modifications, impose high performance overheads, or overlook the integrity of compliance mechanisms themselves.
  This paper presents GDPRuler, a trusted middleware system that enables verifiable GDPR compliance for KVS on untrusted clouds without modifying their codebase. GDPRuler deploys a trusted GDPR monitor inside a Confidential Virtual Machine (CVM), which enforces GDPR policies, manages compliance metadata, and maintains tamper-evident audit logs. A declarative policy language translates core GDPR obligations into enforceable runtime rules. To ensure efficiency, GDPRuler encodes metadata compactly within KV records, builds dedicated metadata indexes for GDPR-specific queries, and logs only compliance-relevant events in a space-efficient format. We implement GDPRuler as a transparent proxy for unmodified Redis and RocksDB deployments. Evaluation with YCSB and GDPR-inspired workloads shows that GDPRuler enforces core compliance guarantees with low overheads: GDPRuler achieves ~61% of native KVS throughput with the CVM environment contributing 28%-32% of it, metadata storage overhead remains below 20%, and GDPR queries benefit from 13-182x speedup through metadata indexing. By embedding verifiable policy enforcement into a trusted middleware layer, GDPRuler offers a practical path toward GDPR-compliant KVS on untrusted cloud infrastructures.

</details>


### [155] [SHIELDS: Automating OS Hardening with Iterative Multi-Agent Remediation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05476)
*Andrew Hamara,Dwight Horne,Aldehir Rojas,Timothy Kurniawan,Sophie Lamothe,Vishal Suresh,Nicholas Turoci,Lawrence Wong*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: SHIELDS是一个基于多智能体和大语言模型的系统，通过迭代反馈驱动的过程实现操作系统安全加固，在不依赖静态预写修复方案的情况下，成功修复了高达73%的安全扫描发现项，且性能提升更依赖于有效的工具使用而非模型参数量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全配置错误是操作系统级入侵的主要原因，手动维护系统符合DISA STIG等安全标准繁琐且昂贵。现有自动化合规工具依赖静态的、预写的修复方案，缺乏灵活性和适应性。

Method: 提出SHIELDS多智能体系统，利用大语言模型将操作系统加固建模为迭代的、基于反馈的过程。系统持续提出修复方案，并根据目标系统执行结果和验证扫描的反馈不断优化修复策略。

Result: 在多种虚拟机配置上使用6种当代LLM（参数规模20B-400B）进行评测，SHIELDS成功修复了高达73%的扫描发现项。

Conclusion: 在该场景下，成功的关键更多依赖于有效的工具使用和信息收集，而非模型参数量（规模），这为在计算资源受限或安全隐私需求驱动使用本地模型的环境中减轻安全合规负担提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Security misconfigurations remain a leading cause of OS-level compromise, and manually keeping systems compliant with standards like Defense Information Systems Agency (DISA) Security Technical Implementation Guides (STIGs) is a tedious and expensive process. Existing compliance automation tools can reduce some of this burden, but they depend on static, pre-written corrective actions. In this paper, we introduce SHIELDS, a multi-agent system that uses large language models (LLMs) to approach OS hardening as an iterative, feedback-driven process. Instead of applying fixed remediations, SHIELDS continuously proposes fixes and refines them based on feedback from target system execution and validation scans. We evaluate the system across multiple virtual machine configurations using six contemporary LLMs ranging from 20B to 400B parameters, and find that SHIELDS successfully remediates up to 73% of scan findings. Our results also suggest that success in this setting depends less on model size (parameter count) than on effective tool use and information gathering, paving a practical path toward reducing the burden of security compliance in environments where compute is limited or security and privacy needs drive local model use.

</details>


### [156] [Bitcoin After Block Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05503)
*Junhyuk Lee*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究了比特币区块奖励归零后，矿工仅依赖交易费用情况下网络安全性问题，识别了诚实挖矿不再最优的偏离阈值$G_t$，并评估了三种协议级机制（Base Fee、Fee Floor和自适应最大区块大小规则）对提升偏离阈值和缓解激励崩溃的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 比特币区块奖励逐步降低至零，引发了矿工仅靠交易费用时网络能否保持安全的担忧。本文旨在识别大规模持续偏离诚实挖矿行为的条件。

Method: 采用序列决策模型，分析和比较诚实矿工与偏离矿工的收益，识别偏离阈值$G_t$；结合2024年比特币减半事件验证当前挖矿行为是否出现大规模结构性偏离；评估三种协议级机制（Base Fee、Fee Floor、自适应最大区块大小规则）的组合效果。

Result: 2024年减半前后，当前挖矿行为未出现大规模结构性偏离；但区块奖励移除后，$G_t$准则表明即使交易费用占比极小也可能出现偏离；三种机制的组合能提高偏离阈值，缓解仅依赖费用时的激励崩溃。

Conclusion: 为评估比特币在区块奖励消失后的安全性提供了实用基准，表明通过协议级机制组合可以缓解激励问题。

Abstract: Bitcoin's block reward is scheduled to decline to zero, raising concerns about whether the network can remain secure once miners rely solely on transaction fees. This paper seeks to identify the conditions under which large-scale and persistent deviation from honest mining can arise.
  We analyze and compare the payoffs of honest and deviating miners in a sequential decision model, and identify a deviation threshold $G_t$ at which honest mining ceases to be privately optimal. Around the 2024 Bitcoin halving, we show that current mining behavior does not exhibit large-scale or structural deviation. However, when the block reward is removed, the $G_t$ criterion implies that deviation can arise even with a very small fraction of transaction fees.
  Finally, we evaluate three protocol-level mechanisms: Base Fee, Fee Floor, and an adaptive maximum block size rule, and show that their combination raises the deviation threshold and mitigates incentive breakdown in a fee-only regime. These results provide a practical benchmark for assessing Bitcoin's security as block rewards disappear.

</details>


### [157] [ZERO-APT: A Closed-Loop Adversarial Framework for LLM-Driven Automated Penetration Testing under Intelligent Defense](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05567)
*Anlan Zheng,Tiantian Zhu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: ZERO-APT提出了一种攻击者-防御者-裁判三合一框架，用于在智能防御环境下评估LLM驱动的渗透测试代理。该框架通过可配置的LLM防御者、分离规划与执行的架构机制以及专用裁判代理，同时解决了 realism（真实性）、consistency（一致性）和 auditability（可审计性）三大缺陷。在Windows Server 2022后渗透场景中，攻击成功率达79%，因果一致性评分0.860，并生成结构化CTI报告实现可审计性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM驱动的自动化渗透测试代理通常仅在静态目标上评估，既无法检测也无法响应攻击，导致其在智能防御下的行为未经测试。同时，多步攻击链的因果一致性依赖于不稳定的LLM推理，且代理决策对分析人员不透明。这三个短板（真实性、一致性、可审计性）通常被孤立地修补，缺乏统一解决方案。

Method: 提出ZERO-APT框架，采用回合制攻击者-防御者-裁判架构：(1) 真实性：嵌入可配置的LLM防御者，消费Sysmon遥测数据实时检测攻击；(2) 一致性：通过三种架构机制（规划与执行分离、多维ReAct反馈、硬约束过滤动作库）将因果一致性从不稳定的LLM推理转移到强制性的系统架构中；(3) 可审计性：专用裁判代理裁决每轮博弈、维护全局状态并生成结构化事后CTI报告。

Result: 在Windows Server 2022后渗透原型上，跨五种场景、三种防御者配置进行评估：ZERO-APT攻击成功率达79%（Aurora 22%，PentestGPT 39%），因果一致性得分0.860（Aurora 0.930，Claude Code 0.520），并通过结构化CTI报告实现端到端决策可审计性。

Conclusion: ZERO-APT在单一架构中同时解决了真实性、一致性和可审计性三大问题，在智能防御环境下显著优于现有方法。作者还发布了基准测试，支持在智能防御条件下评估渗透测试代理。

Abstract: LLM-driven automated penetration testing agents are typically evaluated against static targets that neither detect nor respond to attacks, so their behavior under intelligent defense remains untested. The causal consistency of multi-step attack chains likewise hinges on unstable LLM reasoning, and agent decisions remain opaque to human analysts. These three shortcomings, in realism, consistency, and auditability, are usually patched in isolation. We present ZERO-APT, a turn-based attacker-defender-judge framework that addresses them within a single architecture. For realism, ZERO-APT embeds a configurable LLM Defender that consumes Sysmon telemetry and detects attacks in real time, exposing the attacker to a live opponent rather than a passive target. For consistency, three architectural mechanisms move causal consistency from unstable LLM reasoning into enforced system architecture: separation of planning from execution, multi-dimensional ReAct feedback, and a hard-constraint-filtered action library. For auditability, a dedicated Judge agent adjudicates each round, maintains global state, and emits structured post-hoc CTI reports that make every decision traceable. We evaluate a Windows Server 2022 post-exploitation prototype across five scenarios with three Defender configurations. ZERO-APT reaches 79\% attack success rate (Aurora 22\%, PentestGPT 39\%), a Causal Consistency Score of 0.860 (Aurora 0.930, Claude Code 0.520), and end-to-end decision auditability through structured CTI reports. We release the benchmark to support evaluation of penetration agents under intelligent defense.

</details>


### [158] [Dimensionality Reduction for Cyberattack Classification: A Comparative Evaluation of PCA and Linear Predictive Coding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05584)
*Nelly Elsayed,Zag ElSayed,Navid Asadizanjani*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究了PCA和LPC两种特征压缩方法在网络攻击检测中的应用，发现PCA能在大幅压缩特征维度时保持分类性能，LPC则稍有性能下降，表明轻量级特征压缩可用于高效网络安全分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维特征表示在基于机器学习的网络攻击检测系统中广泛使用，但增加了计算复杂度，可能阻碍其在资源受限环境中的部署。

Method: 比较了主成分分析（PCA）和线性预测编码（LPC）两种降维方法，生成不同维度的压缩特征表示，并在多个分类模型上进行评估。

Result: PCA在激进压缩下仍能保持分类性能；LPC提供了具有竞争力的预测表示，但性能下降略大。大幅特征维度缩减对分类精度影响很小。

Conclusion: 轻量级特征压缩可以显著降低特征维度而对分类精度影响极小，在资源受限的网络安全分析场景中具有应用潜力。

Abstract: High-dimensional feature representations are widely used in machine learning-based cyberattack detection systems. However, they increase computational complexity and may hinder deployment in resource-constrained environments. In this paper, we investigate feature compression techniques for cyberattack classification by comparing two dimensionality reduction approaches: Principal Component Analysis (PCA) and Linear Predictive Coding (LPC). Compressed feature representations with varying dimensionalities are generated and evaluated across several classification models. Experimental analysis demonstrates that PCA preserves classification performance even under aggressive compression. On the other hand, LPC provides competitive predictive representations with slightly larger performance degradation. The results show that substantial reductions in feature dimensionality can be achieved with minimal impact on classification accuracy, highlighting the potential of lightweight feature compression for efficient cybersecurity analytics.

</details>


### [159] [The Coverage Gap: Chile's Cyber Disclosure Framework versus the USA, EU and UK](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05594)
*David Mellafe Z*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出"覆盖缺口"（Coverage Gap）概念，用于衡量关键基础设施运营商的公开暴露与其漏洞披露协调能力之间的可测量距离。通过对智利915家"重要运营商"（OIVs）进行全量普查（覆盖官方目录约98.7%），发现仅有16家（1.7%）发布了可验证的RFC 9116披露渠道，84%存在邮件认证配置错误，约23.5%使用已知漏洞组件。与美国联邦民事机构99%的合规率形成鲜明对比。研究提出四阶段路线图，并开源了anci-oiv-resolver工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 关键基础设施运营商必须具备可信的漏洞披露机制，但各国实际部署情况缺乏系统性量化评估。智利2025年新网络安全法（Ley 21.663）指定了首批OIVs，为开展全量普查提供了独特窗口。

Method: 采用纯被动式OSINT方法，遵循ISO/IEC 29147:2018标准和智利计算机犯罪安全港条款（Ley 21.459）。对官方目录中约98.7%的OIVs进行全量普查，聚焦第一层（可验证的披露联系渠道），同时评估邮件认证配置（DMARC/SPF）和已知漏洞组件情况，并进行跨国基准比较。

Result: 仅1.7%的OIVs（16/915）发布RFC 9116披露渠道；84%存在邮件认证配置错误；约23.5%使用已知漏洞组件。所有四家大型银行和两家电信运营商均无披露渠道。智利在邮件认证强制令上落后美国、英国、荷兰约8年，落后丹麦约3年。

Conclusion: 智利关键基础设施漏洞披露覆盖存在严重缺口，需参考BOD 18-01和英国公共部门DMARC工具包，实施四阶段路线图。开源工具可支持独立复现和规模化审计。

Abstract: We introduce the Coverage Gap as a measurable distance between the observable public exposure of critical-infrastructure operators and their declared capability to coordinate vulnerability disclosure. We instantiate it against the 915 Chilean Operadores de Importancia Vital (OIVs -- Operators of Vital Importance) designated by the National Cybersecurity Agency (ANCI) under Ley 21.663 (Resolucion Exenta No. 87, 16 December 2025). Using a passive-only, OSINT-based method consistent with the principles of ISO/IEC 29147:2018 and Chile's computer-crimes safe harbour (Ley 21.459), we conduct a full-universe census of the foundational disclosure-capability layer (Layer 1, verifiable disclosure contact) across approximately 98.7% of the official catalogue. Only 16 of 915 OIVs (1.7%) publish a verifiable RFC 9116 disclosure channel; among operators of physical-world infrastructure -- energy, health, banking, telecommunications, fuel, water, transport, and state administration -- fewer than ten do so, and all four major banks and both telecommunications incumbents lack one entirely. This compares with over 99% adherence in the U.S. federal civilian branch under CISA Binding Operational Directive 18-01. Email-authentication misconfiguration affects 766 of 915 (84%) OIVs, and end-of-life or known-vulnerable stack components an estimated 23.5% (Wilson 95% CI [12%, 38%]). Cross-jurisdictional benchmarking situates Chile roughly eight years behind the USA, the UK, and the Netherlands on email-authentication mandates, and three years behind Denmark. We propose a four-stage roadmap modelled on BOD 18-01 and the UK Public-Sector DMARC Toolkit, and release the open-source tool anci-oiv-resolver (Apache 2.0) to enable independent reproduction of the OIV-domain mapping that underpins universe-scale auditing.

</details>


### [160] [SlotGCG: Exploiting the Positional Vulnerability in LLMs for Jailbreak Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05609)
*Seungwon Jeong,Jiwoo Jeong,Hyeonjin Kim,Yunseok Lee,Woojin Lee*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出SlotGCG方法，通过量化提示词中不同插入位置（slots）的脆弱性得分（VSS），选择最脆弱的槽位进行对抗性token插入，从而提升越狱攻击的成功率。实验表明，该方法在多个模型上比GCG基线方法攻击成功率提高14%，对防御方法的鲁棒性提高42%，且仅需200ms预处理时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有优化型越狱攻击（如GCG）将对抗性token固定插入在提示词末尾，忽略了其他插入位置对攻击效果的影响。本文旨在探究提示词中不同位置（slots）的脆弱性差异，并利用这一发现提升越狱攻击的效率。

Method: 提出脆弱槽位评分（VSS）量化提示词中各位置的越狱脆弱性；基于VSS设计SlotGCG方法，先评估所有槽位的VSS，选择最脆弱的槽位插入对抗性token，再在该位置运行定向优化攻击。该方法与攻击类型无关，可作为插件集成到任何优化型攻击中。

Result: 在多个模型上，SlotGCG相比GCG基线方法攻击成功率（ASR）提高14%，收敛速度更快；在防御场景下ASR比基线方法高42%；预处理时间仅增加约200ms。

Conclusion: 提示词中不同插入位置对越狱攻击的脆弱性存在显著差异，通过VSS量化和选择最脆弱槽位可以有效提升攻击性能。SlotGCG是一种轻量级、可插拔的位置搜索机制，能显著增强现有优化型攻击的效果。

Abstract: As large language models (LLMs) are widely deployed, identifying their vulnerability through jailbreak attacks becomes increasingly critical. Optimization-based attacks like Greedy Coordinate Gradient (GCG) have focused on inserting adversarial tokens to the end of prompts. However, GCG restricts adversarial tokens to a fixed insertion point (typically the prompt suffix), leaving the effect of inserting tokens at other positions unexplored. In this paper, we empirically investigate \emph{slots}, i.e., candidate positions within a prompt where tokens can be inserted. We find that vulnerability to jailbreaking is highly related to the selection of the \emph{slots}. Based on these findings, we introduce the \textit{Vulnerable Slot Score} (VSS) to quantify the positional vulnerability to jailbreaking. We then propose SlotGCG, which evaluates all slots with VSS, selects the most vulnerable slots for insertion, and runs a targeted optimization attack at those slots. Our approach provides a position-search mechanism that is attack-agnostic and can be plugged into any optimization-based attack, adding only 200ms of preprocessing time. Experiments across multiple models demonstrate that SlotGCG significantly outperforms existing methods. Specifically, it achieves 14\% higher Attack Success Rates (ASR) over GCG-based attacks, converges faster, and shows superior robustness against defense methods with 42\% higher ASR than baseline approaches. Our implementation is available at \href{https://github.com/youai058/SlotGCG}{https://github.com/youai058/SlotGCG}

</details>


### [161] [Protecting K-Nearest Neighbor Queries from Location Inference Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05648)
*Zhiyu Sun,Jie Fu,Xinpeng Ling,Huifa Li,Zhili Chen*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文首次提出针对k近邻查询（kNNQ）的两种位置推断攻击（几何交点攻击GI-LIA和零阶优化攻击ZO-LIA），并设计了差分隐私保护框架DPRS，通过带约束扰动区间的拒绝采样机制来平衡隐私保护与查询效用，实验表明DPRS优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: k近邻查询（kNNQ）在现代基于位置的服务中广泛使用（如“附近的人”），但其潜在的隐私风险长期被忽视，现有研究缺乏对kNNQ固有位置隐私风险的系统性攻击与防护方法。

Method: 提出两种攻击方法：几何交点位置推断攻击（GI-LIA）和零阶优化位置推断攻击（ZO-LIA）。提出差分隐私保护框架DPRS，核心是在约束扰动区间内引入拒绝采样机制，以减轻过量噪声注入导致的距离失真，并设计私有区间构造算法优化隐私-效用权衡。

Result: 在真实空间数据集上的大量实验表明，DPRS在隐私保护和查询效用两方面均优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 揭示了kNNQ固有的位置隐私风险（首次提出两种攻击），并提供了有效的差分隐私防护方案DPRS，实现了更好的隐私-效用权衡。

Abstract: The k-nearest neighbor query (kNNQ) is a core component of modern location-based services (LBS) and has been widely adopted in popular features such as ``people nearby''. However, its potential privacy risks have long been overlooked. In this work, we present the first two attacks against kNNQ, namely the geometric intersection location inference attack (GI-LIA) and the zero-order optimization location inference attack (ZO-LIA), revealing the inherent location privacy risks posed by kNNQ. To mitigate these privacy risks, we further propose DPRS, a differential privacy framework for kNNQ protection. The core idea of DPRS is to incorporate a rejection sampling mechanism within a constrained perturbation interval, thereby mitigating the distance distortion caused by excessive noise injection. In addition, we design a private interval construction algorithm to construct the perturbation interval, enabling the rejection sampling mechanism to achieve a more favorable trade-off between privacy protection and query utility in kNNQ. Extensive experiments on real-world spatial datasets demonstrate that DPRS outperforms existing methods in both privacy protection and query utility. Our code is available at https://github.com/reanatom/DPRS.

</details>


### [162] [Cognitive Threat Intelligence and Explainable Federated Security Analytics for distributed Infrastructure Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05701)
*Md. Arifur Rahman,B. M. Taslimul Haque,Md. Iqbal Hossan,Md. Serajul Kabir Chowdhury Rubel*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种融合联邦学习、可解释人工智能和认知网络安全分析的综合框架，用于分布式基础设施系统中的隐私保护协作式网络威胁检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统集中式入侵检测方法在可扩展性、数据隐私、通信开销和AI决策透明度方面存在局限，难以应对日益复杂的网络威胁和扩大的攻击面。

Method: 提出认知威胁智能与可解释联邦安全分析框架，集成联邦学习、可解释人工智能和认知网络安全分析。通过分布式节点本地训练安全模型，仅共享加密模型参数和更新，采用联邦聚合机制实现协作学习。

Result: 框架实现了隐私保护增强、通信依赖性降低、集中式安全风险减少，并整合了随机森林、XGBoost和自编码器等机器学习/深度学习算法以增强智能威胁分析能力。

Conclusion: 该框架通过去中心化学习架构有效解决了传统方法的局限性，为分布式基础设施系统提供了可扩展、隐私保护且透明的网络安全威胁检测方案。

Abstract: The increasing adoption of distributed infrastructure systems, cloud computing, Internet of Things (IoT) technologies, and edge-based architectures has significantly expanded the cybersecurity attack surface and introduced increasingly sophisticated cyber threats. Conventional centralized intrusion detection approaches often face challenges related to scalability, data privacy, communication overhead, and limited transparency in artificial intelligence-driven decision-making processes. To address these limitations, this study proposes a Cognitive Threat Intelligence and Explainable Federated Security Analytics framework for distributed infrastructure systems. The proposed framework integrates Federated Learning (FL), Explainable Artificial Intelligence (XAI), and cognitive cybersecurity analytics to enable collaborative and privacy-preserving cyber threat detection across distributed network environments. Instead of transmitting sensitive raw network traffic data to centralized servers, local security models are independently trained at distributed nodes, where only encrypted model parameters and updates are shared through a federated aggregation mechanism. This decentralized learning architecture improves privacy protection while reducing communication dependency and centralized security risks. To enhance intelligent threat analysis, the framework incorporates machine learning and deep learning algorithms including Random Forest, XGBoost, Autoencoder

</details>


### [163] [Explainable AI-Driven Cyber Risk Analytics and Model Reliability Assessment for Intelligent Governance of U.S. Critical Infrastructure: An XGBoost and SHAP-Based Intrusion Detection Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05710)
*B. M. Taslimul Haque,Md. Arifur Rahman,Md. Serajul Kabir Chowdhury Rubel,Md. Iqbal Hossan*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于机器学习和可解释人工智能（XAI）的弹性网络风险分析与模型可靠性评估框架，利用CICIDS2017数据集和多种分类器（XGBoost、随机森林、决策树）来检测美国关键基础设施环境中的恶意网络活动并评估网络风险水平，同时通过XAI技术增强决策过程的透明性和可信度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着智能数字技术在美国关键基础设施领域（能源、医疗、交通、金融、通信）的深入应用，网络攻击（DDoS、勒索软件、APT等）带来的安全威胁日益严峻，传统网络安全机制难以应对动态变化的攻击环境，亟需一种智能化的网络风险分析与决策支持框架来保障基础设施的韧性和安全性。

Method: 基于CICIDS2017数据集，采用XGBoost、随机森林和决策树等机器学习分类器进行入侵检测和网络风险预测，并集成可解释人工智能（XAI）技术提升网络安全决策的透明性和可解释性，通过准确率、精确率、召回率、F1分数、ROC-AUC和假阳性率等指标评估模型性能。

Result: 提出了一种面向美国关键基础设施的弹性网络风险分析与模型可靠性评估框架，集成了机器学习分类器进行恶意活动检测与风险预测，并通过XAI技术增强了决策过程的可解释性和可信度。

Conclusion: 该框架通过集成机器学习和XAI技术，能够有效提升关键基础设施网络安全风险分析的准确性和可靠性，为智能治理和自动化决策提供支持，增强了模型在动态网络环境中的适应性和信任度。

Abstract: The increasing penetrations of the critical infrastructure sector in the United States with intelligent digital technologies have greatly increased exposure to advanced cyber adversaries and operational vulnerabilities. AI-powered governance and automated decision-making systems are becoming a key part of the operation of critical infrastructure systems, including energy, healthcare, transportation, financial services, and communication infrastructure, in order to improve efficiency and strategic management. The growing cyber threat environment, such as Distributed Denial of Service (DDos) attacks, botnets, ransomware, and Advanced Persistent Threats (APTs) pose significant challenges to infrastructure resilience, cyber security reliability, and governance trustworthiness. In a changing attack landscape and dynamic network environment, traditional cybersecurity mechanisms can often fall short of meeting the evolving needs and protecting critical systems. This study will develop a resilient cyber risk analytics and model reliability assessment framework to support intelligent governance and decision support for cyber risk exposure in the U.S. critical infrastructure environment. This study is based on the CICIDS2017 dataset for the development and testing of intrusion detection system models and cyber risk prediction models based on machine learning. Various classifiers like XGBoost, Random Forest, and Decision Tree are used to detect malicious activities on the network and determine the level of cyber risk. Furthermore, the Explainable Artificial Intelligence (XAI) techniques are integrated to enhance transparency, interpretability, and trust in cybersecurity decision-making processes. The proposed framework presents the reliability and resilience of the model by having various performance measures such as accuracy, precision, recall, F1 score, ROC-AUC, and false positive rate.

</details>


### [164] [Hybrid CNN-LSTM Framework for Intelligent Cyber Attack Detection and Prevention in U.S. Critical Digital Infrastructure: A Comparative Machine Learning Evaluation on CSE-CIC-IDS2018](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05714)
*Md. Iqbal Hossan,Md. Serajul Kabir Chowdhury Rubel,Md. Arifur Rahman,B. M. Taslimul Haque*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种利用AI/ML算法构建智能网络防御系统的框架，在CSE-CIC-IDS2018数据集上评估了Random Forest、XGBoost、CNN和LSTM等模型，用于检测DDoS、暴力破解、僵尸网络等多种网络攻击，以增强美国数字基础设施的网络安全韧性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于签名的入侵检测系统无法有效检测未知和不断变化的网络攻击，而美国数字基础设施的快速发展使得关键领域（医疗、金融、交通、能源、政府系统）面临日益严重的高级网络威胁，亟需更智能的防御方案。

Method: 使用CSE-CIC-IDS2018真实网络流量数据集，涵盖DDoS、暴力破解、僵尸网络、渗透攻击和Web攻击等多种场景；实施并评估了Random Forest、XGBoost、CNN和LSTM等多种机器学习与深度学习模型；框架包括数据预处理、特征工程、实时流量监控、智能威胁分类和自动防御机制。

Result: 研究结果表明所提出的智能防御框架能够有效识别恶意网络行为，提升入侵检测的准确率，但文中未给出具体的性能指标数值结果。

Conclusion: 基于AI/ML的智能网络防御系统能够克服传统方法的局限性，实现实时检测和防御未知攻击，从而增强美国数字基础设施的网络安全韧性。

Abstract: Digital infrastructure is growing at a rapid pace in the United States, and as a result, exposure to advanced cyber threats to critical sectors including healthcare, finance, transportation, energy and government systems is growing. The traditional cybersecurity approaches, including signature-based intrusion detection systems, have become less effective against today's cyber attacks, as they are unable to detect unknown and changing attacks in real time. To overcome these constraints, this research suggests a smart cyber-defense system, which utilizes Artificial Intelligence (AI) and Machine Learning (ML) algorithms in the detection and prevention of cyber attacks in the U.S. digital infrastructure. This study uses the CSE-CIC-IDS2018 dataset, which is a realistic network traffic dataset, along with various cyber attack scenarios, including Distributed Denial of Service (DDoS), brute force attacks, botnets, infiltration attacks, and web-based attacks. A number of machine learning and deep learning models such as Random Forest, XGBoost, Convolutional Neural Networks (CNN) and Long Short-Term Memory (LSTM) networks are implemented and evaluated to be used in identifying malicious network behavior and boosting the accuracy of intrusion detection. The framework proposed combines data preprocessing, feature engineering, real-time traffic monitoring, intelligent threat classification with automated prevention mechanisms to build cybersecurity resilience. E

</details>


### [165] [An Embarrassingly Simple Detector for Model Extraction Attacks in Large Language Model API Traffic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05725)
*Shuze Liu,Qianwen Guo,Yushun Dong*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于良性校准的流量窗口分布测试方法用于LLM模型窃取检测，利用最大均值差异（MMD）比较嵌入查询的语义分布与历史良性流量，在多种攻击场景下实现了极低的误报率（0.3%）和优秀的检测性能（95.1%平衡准确率）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模型窃取检测方法多聚焦于单查询异常评分或纯良性vs攻击者的二分类设定，忽视了实际场景中攻击查询与良性请求的相似性以及多用户混合流量的复杂性，需要一种更贴合实际部署的检测方法。

Method: 将模型窃取检测建模为良性校准的流量窗口分布测试：将输入查询嵌入语义空间，利用最大均值差异（MMD）检验当前流量窗口的聚合分布是否偏离历史良性流量分布，仅通过良性vs良性的比较来设定决策阈值。

Result: 在四种窃取场景的14组攻击者-正常查询对上，MMD检测器实现了0.3%良性假阳性率、100.0%纯攻击者真阳性率、90.5%平均攻击者比例TPR和95.1%平衡准确率，优于PRADA、SEAT、CAP、DATE及边际马氏距离等基线方法。

Conclusion: 良性校准的分布测试是LLM API流量中模型窃取检测的一个强经验基线，在用户级和混合多用户场景下均有效。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed through hosted APIs, making model extraction a practical threat to model ownership and service security. However, individual extraction queries often resemble benign requests, and existing evaluations often focus on single-query anomaly scoring or pure benign-versus-attacker user settings. We formulate model extraction monitoring as benign-calibrated traffic-window distribution testing and show that an embarrassingly simple detector is effective: embed incoming queries into a semantic space and test whether their aggregate distribution deviates from historical benign traffic. We instantiate the detector with maximum mean discrepancy (MMD), using only benign-vs-benign comparisons to set the decision threshold. We evaluate on fourteen attacker-normal query pairs from four extraction scenarios and compare with adapted PRADA, SEAT, CAP, DATE, and marginal Mahalanobis baselines. Across three random seeds, MMD achieves 0.3% benign FPR, 100.0% pure-attacker TPR, 90.5% average TPR over attacker fractions, and 95.1% balanced accuracy. These results show that benign-calibrated distribution testing is a strong empirical baseline for model extraction detection in both user-level and mixed multi-user LLM API traffic. Code is released at: https://github.com/LabRAI/mmd-llm-mea-detection.

</details>


### [166] [Membrane: A Self-Evolving Contrastive Safety Memory for LLM Agent Defense](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05743)
*Minseok Choi,Seungbin Yang,Dongjin Kim,Subin Kim,Jungmin Son,Yunseung Lee,Jaegul Choo,Youngjun Kwak*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出Membrane，一种基于对比安全记忆(CSM)的自演化防护栏。每个CSM单元将阻止有害查询的条件与允许表面相似的无害请求的条件配对，无需重新训练即可通过将每次有害交互及其无害对应物蒸馏为按攻击策略索引的对比单元来演化，实现同一单元泛化到相同机制的不同主题变体。在推理时，检索到的单元作为精确安全决策的上下文。在HarmBench和AgentHarm上，Membrane在所有六种越狱攻击中取得最高F1，AgentHarm上的无害拒绝率低至7-14%，远低于先前方法的28-85%，记忆单元在跨攻击迁移下保持87-88%的F1且对记忆投毒保持稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安全分类器无法适应不断演化的越狱攻击，而基于自适应记忆的防护栏容易过度拒绝与存储攻击相似的无害查询，缺乏精准区分表面相似而本质不同的有害/无害请求的能力。

Method: 构建对比安全记忆(CSM)，每个单元包含拒绝有害查询和允许表面相似无害查询的条件对；通过蒸馏每次有害交互及其无害对应物来演化CSM，单元按攻击策略索引以泛化到同机制的不同主题变体；推理时检索相关单元作为上下文进行安全决策。

Result: 在所有六种越狱攻击上达到最高F1；AgentHarm无害拒绝率仅7-14%（先前28-85%）；跨攻击迁移下F1保持87-88%；对记忆投毒保持稳定。

Conclusion: Membrane通过对比记忆单元实现了高精度的安全决策，在适应新攻击的同时大幅降低了对无害查询的过度拒绝，具备良好的泛化性和鲁棒性。

Abstract: Despite advances in safety alignment, large language models remain vulnerable to continuously evolving jailbreaks. Existing fine-tuned safety classifiers cannot adapt to these evolving attacks, while adaptive memory-based guardrails tend to over-refuse benign queries that resemble stored attacks. We propose Membrane, a self-evolving guardrail built on Contrastive Safety Memory (CSM): each cell pairs the conditions for blocking a harmful query with those for permitting a superficially similar benign request. Without retraining, Membrane evolves CSM by distilling each harmful interaction and its benign counterpart into a contrastive cell indexed by the underlying attack strategy, so that one cell generalizes across topical variants of the same mechanism. At inference, retrieved cells serve as grounding context for precise safety decisions. Across model-level safety on HarmBench and agent-level safety on AgentHarm, Membrane achieves the highest F1 on all six jailbreak attacks. Notably, benign refusal on AgentHarm stays at 7-14%, well below the 28-85% range of prior guards. Memory cells also retain 87-88% F1 under cross-attack transfer and remain stable under memory poisoning.

</details>


### [167] [An Improved CNN-LSTM Based Intrusion Detection System for IoT Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05776)
*Mohammad Tariq Ikhlas,Pohanyar Khowaja Khil,Malik Muhammad Mueed Aslam,Muhammad Khuram Shahzad*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种改进的CNN-LSTM入侵检测模型，通过结合多分类、数据集融合和时序特征学习，在IoT网络流量数据上实现了约97%的检测准确率，有效捕获了网络流量的空间和时序特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着IoT设备快速普及，网络安全威胁急剧增加，亟需高效的入侵检测系统来保护网络环境。

Method: 提出改进的CNN-LSTM模型，结合多分类策略、数据集集成以及时序特征学习，利用CNN捕获空间特征、LSTM捕获时序特征。

Result: 在IoT网络流量入侵检测任务上达到了约97%的准确率，能够有效检测多种攻击类别，训练和验证性能稳定。

Conclusion: CNN与RNN组件的融合使模型能够同时捕获网络流量的空间和时序特性，显著提升了IoT环境下的入侵检测能力。

Abstract: With the rapid proliferation of IoT devices, security concerns have dramatically escalated and intrusion detection systems have become critical for protecting networked environments. This paper presents an improved CNN-LSTM based intrusion detection model that combines multi-class classification, dataset integration, and temporal feature learning to enhance detection performance in IoT networks. Using network traffic data, the proposed approach is evaluated on intrusion detection tasks and achieves an accuracy of approximately 97%. Experimental results demonstrate that the model effectively detects multiple attack categories while maintaining stable training and validation performance. The integration of convolutional and recurrent neural network components enables the framework to capture both spatial and temporal characteristics of network traffic, improving overall intrusion detection capability in IoT environments.

</details>


### [168] [TinyML-Driven Cybersecurity for Autonomous Spacecraft: Latency-Accuracy Analysis for SPARTA RF and Cyber Threat Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05779)
*Van Le,Trevor Tran,Tan Le*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究在航天器自主系统中使用TinyML兼容的经典机器学习模型（随机森林、逻辑回归、SVM、MLP）检测射频网络威胁的延迟-精度权衡。基于SPARTA攻击模型，结合物理信息理论分析与对抗性RF频谱图实验，发现逻辑回归在微秒级推理下仅比随机森林低1%的精度，是适合星上自主的TinyML基线方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主航天器需要快速、轻量、可靠的在轨射频网络威胁检测能力，但现有方法在计算资源受限的航天器平台上难以同时满足低延迟和高精度的需求。

Method: 基于SPARTA攻击模型分析五种威胁（上行干扰、Fake-NR欺骗、载荷操控、地面段入侵、未授权指令注入），对四种TinyML模型（随机森林、逻辑回归、SVM、MLP）进行物理信息驱动的理论分析（计算复杂度、VC维、Lipschitz连续性、延迟缩放），并在BandErasure、FakeNR、NoiseBurst三种对抗模式生成的RF频谱图上进行实验验证。

Result: 逻辑回归在微秒级推理延迟下，精度仅比随机森林低1%，是最高效的TinyML基线模型。

Conclusion: 逻辑回归可作为航天器自主在轨射频威胁检测的轻量级基线方案，未来可通过更丰富的特征编码器和多时间尺度学习架构进一步提升航天器网络安全性能。

Abstract: Autonomous spacecraft require rapid, lightweight, and reliable onboard detection of cyber-RF threats. Using the SPARTA attack model, we analyze the latency-accuracy trade-offs of TinyML-compatible classical models -- Random Forest, Logistic Regression, SVM, and MLP -- for detecting uplink jamming, Fake-NR spoofing, payload manipulation, ground-segment compromise, and unauthorized command injection. We present a physics-informed theoretical analysis of each model's computational complexity, VC dimension, Lipschitz continuity, and latency scaling, supported by empirical measurements on adversarial RF spectrograms generated via BandErasure, FakeNR, and NoiseBurst corruption modes. Results show that Logistic Regression achieves microsecond-level inference with only a 1\% accuracy drop relative to Random Forest, making it an effective TinyML baseline for onboard autonomy. The study also identifies opportunities for advancing spacecraft cybersecurity through richer feature encoders and multi-timescale learning architectures, building on recent progress in edge intelligence and trustworthy AI.

</details>


### [169] [SentinelRAG: Synthetic Sentinel Knowledge for RAG Database Copyright Protection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05787)
*Tsun On Kwok,Xi Yang,Ki Sen Hung,Chang Liu,Yangqiu Song*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出SentinelRAG框架，通过向RAG数据库注入风格一致但虚构的知识条目（描述虚构实体）来保护数据库版权。虚构知识不会被合法查询检索，但可通过数据所有者知道的特定探针可靠触发。在四个数据集上以0.1%注入率实现p<10^{-5}的统计显著检测，同时大幅降低误检率且对合法查询干扰可忽略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有水印方法要么在RAG数据库中注入真实实体间的虚构关系，污染知识库并引入虚假信息；要么嵌入脆弱的词汇模式，容易被对抗性释义移除。需要一种既不会污染知识库又能抵抗释义攻击的版权保护方法。

Method: SentinelRAG框架向RAG数据库中注入风格一致但描述虚构实体的知识条目。这些虚构知识由于描述的是不存在的实体，不会被合法用户查询检索到，但数据所有者可通过预先设计的特定探针查询来可靠触发检测，从而实现对数据库的版权水印。

Result: 在2.9k到8.8M文档规模的四个数据集上，仅以0.1%的注入率即可在所有测试配置下实现p<10^{-5}的统计显著检测。与现有最优方法相比，大幅降低了误检率，且对合法用户查询的干扰可忽略不计。

Conclusion: SentinelRAG通过注入虚构实体知识条目的方式，在保持低注入率（0.1%）的同时实现了高检测显著性（p<10^{-5}），解决了现有方法污染知识库或易被攻击的缺陷，为RAG数据库版权保护提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Protecting proprietary RAG databases from unauthorized redistribution is challenging: existing watermarking methods either inject fabricated relations between real entities, polluting the knowledge base with misinformation, or embed fragile lexical patterns that adversarial paraphrasing easily removes. We propose SentinelRAG, a watermarking framework that embeds style-consistent but fictitious knowledge entries into the RAG database. Our key insight is that synthetic knowledge describing fictitious entities is unlikely to be retrieved by legitimate queries, yet can be reliably triggered through targeted probes known only to the data owner. Experiments on four datasets ranging from 2.9k to 8.8M documents demonstrate that SentinelRAG achieves statistically significant detection $p < 10^{-5}$ across all tested configurations at only a 0.1% injection rate. Compared to the state-of-the-art, our method significantly reduces the false detection rate while maintaining negligible interference with legitimate user queries.

</details>


### [170] [GCD: Garbled, Corrected, Demonstrandum -- Fixing and Proving Go's Extended GCD Implementation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05796)
*Linard Arquint*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文验证了Go标准库中'extendedGCD'实现的正确性，发现两个偏离BoringSSL原始实现的缺陷（系数更新方式和输入域扩大），通过修复和形式化验证（Gobra + Lean）证明了正确性，修复后性能提升24%，并揭示了形式化验证与AI辅助在发现隐蔽代码缺陷中的价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Go标准库中的extendedGCD实现虽声称直接移植自BoringSSL，但实际存在两个关键偏差（系数更新逻辑错误、输入域扩大），这些偏差破坏了算法的核心不变式，可能影响RSA密钥对生成的安全性。作者旨在发现并修复这些缺陷，并通过形式化验证确保修复后的正确性。

Method: (1) 对比分析Go实现与BoringSSL实现的差异，定位两个偏差；(2) 修正系数更新方式以获得24%的性能提升；(3) 将BoringSSL的现有证明移植并扩展到更大的输入域；(4) 使用Gobra（Go语言的演绎验证器）验证修复后实现的正确性和终止性；(5) 对非线性算术关键引理，使用Lean进行证明并导入Gobra；(6) 利用AI智能体基于Gobra错误信息迭代优化不变式和引理，辅助验证过程。

Result: 成功修复了Go标准库extendedGCD实现的缺陷，修复后平均性能提升24%；使用Gobra完成了修复后实现正确性和终止性的形式化验证；通过Lean证明了非线性算术关键引理；发现三个关键洞察：即使经过充分审查的代码也可能含有隐蔽缺陷、形式化验证是发现此类缺陷的有力工具、AI智能体可辅助迭代优化验证过程。

Conclusion: 该工作展示了形式化验证在检测和修复加密库中细微缺陷的价值，尤其在RSA密钥生成这类关键路径上。同时验证了AI辅助形式化验证的可行性——通过AI智能体基于验证器反馈迭代调整不变式和引理，可以降低形式化验证的人工成本。

Abstract: We verify the 'extendedGCD' implementation in Go's standard library ('crypto/internal/fips140/bigmod'), which plays a crucial role in the generation of RSA key pairs. Even though the Go implementation is supposedly a direct port from BoringSSL's implementation, we uncovered two deviations that each break the algorithm's invariants: (1) the Go implementation deviates in the way coefficients are updated, and (2) it permits a larger input domain. We address both deviations; the first by fixing the Go implementation, which results in an on average 24% speedup, and the second deviation by porting an existing proof for BoringSSL and extending it to cover the larger input domain. We prove correctness and termination of the fixed Go implementation using Gobra, a deductive program verifier for Go. Where necessary, we used Lean to prove key lemmata on non-linear arithmetic, which we import into Gobra.
  Our verification effort reveals three key insights: subtle bugs can slip into even well-reviewed code with surprising ease; formal verification is a powerful tool for uncovering them; and AI agents can facilitate the verification process by iteratively refining invariants and lemmata based on Gobra's error messages.

</details>


### [171] [PriSrv: Privacy-Enhanced and Highly Usable Service Discovery in Wireless Communications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05821)
*Yang Yang,Robert H. Deng,Guomin Yang,Yingjiu Li,HweeHwa Pang,Minming Huang,Rui Shi,Jian Weng*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出PriSrv，一种基于匿名凭证匹配加密（ACME）的私有服务发现协议，通过双层匹配架构实现细粒度认证策略控制和隐私保护，在多种无线框架中集成并验证了安全性与高效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有服务发现协议隐私保护不足，泄露敏感设备信息并引发网络攻击风险，缺乏支持细粒度认证策略的私有协议。

Method: 提出PriSrv协议，核心引入匿名凭证匹配加密（ACME）原语，设计双层匹配架构（外层公共属性过滤、内层私有属性相互认证），并构建快速匿名凭证（FAC）方案实现常量大小凭证与高效验证。

Result: 集成EAP、mDNS、BLE、AirDrop等框架，形式化安全证明与多平台（桌面、笔记本、智能手机、树莓派）性能评估表明，PriSvr在主流移动设备上实现亚秒级安全发现，提供增强隐私保障。

Conclusion: PriSrv在保持高可用性的同时显著提升服务发现的隐私保护水平，适用于主流无线通信框架。

Abstract: Service discovery is essential in wireless communications. However, existing protocols provide limited privacy protection, leaking sensitive device information and opening routes to network attacks. This paper proposes a private service discovery protocol, called PriSrv, which enables both service providers and clients to specify fine-grained authentication policies before establishing connections. PriSrv achieves this via a dual-layer matching architecture: an outer layer filters mismatched entities using public attributes, while an inner layer handles mutual authentication using selectively disclosed private attributes. As a core component, we introduce the primitive of anonymous credential-based matchmaking encryption (ACME), which enables dual-layer matching in a single step to achieve bilateral policy control, selective attribute disclosure, and multi-show unlinkability. To instantiate ACME, we design a fast anonymous credential (FAC) scheme providing constant-size credentials and efficient verification. We demonstrate PriSrv's interoperability by integrating it with popular wireless frameworks including EAP, mDNS, BLE, and AirDrop. Detailed formal security proofs and extensive performance evaluations across desktop, laptop, smartphone, and Raspberry Pi platforms demonstrate that PriSrv provides enhanced privacy guarantees with high usability, achieving secure discovery in less than one second on mainstream mobile devices.

</details>


### [172] [Towards Worst-case Hardness for Low-Noise LPN](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05834)
*Divesh Aggarwal,Rishav Gupta,Hai Hoang Nguyen,Kel Zin Tan,Prashant Nalini Vasudevan*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的从最坏情况到平均情况归约方法来证明LPN问题的困难性。与传统方法要求统计平滑不同，该方法仅要求计算不可区分性，从而获得逆多项式噪声率下的LPN平均情况困难性，并首次通过最坏情况归约达到了Alekhnovich公钥加密所需的参数区间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LPN问题是密码学的基础性假设，但其平均情况困难性能否基于最坏情况复杂性假设（如LWE那样）是一个核心开放问题。现有归约方法（BLVW19, YZ21）依赖线性码的统计平滑，导致噪声率只能达到1/2 - 1/poly(n)，无法满足公钥加密应用的需求。

Method: 提出了一种新的归约方法：不再要求生成矩阵行的随机稀疏组合在统计上接近均匀分布，而是仅要求它们在计算上不可区分于均匀分布。该方法构造了一个"双赢"结构：任何高效的LPN求解器都可以转化为一对高效算法(S, D)，使得对于任何合适维度的矩阵A，要么S能从随机噪声中解码A生成的码，要么D能区分该码的对偶码的随机噪声码字与均匀分布。

Result: 通过适当参数实例化该归约，获得了逆多项式噪声率n^{-α}（对于任何常数α<1）下LPN的平均情况困难性，该困难性基于同时解码码字和区分对偶码噪声码字的最坏情况困难性。特别地，设置α=1/2时，归约达到了Alekhnovich公钥加密方案所需的参数区间，这是之前最坏情况归约无法实现的。

Conclusion: 本文通过将统计条件放松为计算条件，突破了传统归约方法的噪声率限制，为LPN问题的最坏情况到平均情况归约提供了新路径，并首次使最坏情况归约能够支撑公钥加密应用所需的参数设置。

Abstract: The hardness of the Learning Parity with Noise (LPN) problem is a foundational assumption in cryptography, forming the basis of constructions ranging from symmetric-key primitives to public-key encryption and beyond. A central open question is whether the average-case hardness of LPN can be based on worst-case complexity assumptions, as has been achieved for the analogous Learning With Errors (LWE) problem.
  Existing worst-case-to-average-case reductions for LPN [BLVW19, YZ21] rely on statistical smoothing of linear codes, which inherently limits the resulting average-case hardness to noise rates as large as $1/2 - 1/\mathrm{poly}(n)$, which is insufficient for public-key applications.
  We explore a new approach towards obtaining such reductions: rather than requiring that random sparse combinations of the rows of the generator matrix of a code be statistically close to uniform, we only require that they be computationally indistinguishable from uniform. This leads to a clean win-win structure: we show that any efficient LPN solver can be transformed into a pair of efficient algorithms $(S, D)$ such that for every matrix $A$ of appropriate dimensions over $\mathbb{F}_2$, either $S$ decodes the code generated by $A$ from random noise, or $D$ distinguishes random noisy codewords of the dual of this code from uniform.
  By instantiating this reduction with appropriate parameters, we obtain the average-case hardness of LPN with inverse-polynomial noise rate $n^{-α}$ for any constant $α< 1$, assuming the worst-case simultaneous hardness of decoding a code from random noise and distinguishing random noisy codewords of its dual from uniform. In particular, setting $α= 1/2$, our reduction yields LPN hardness in the parameter regime required for Alekhnovich's construction of public-key encryption [Ale03], a regime that was previously inaccessible via worst-case reductions.

</details>


### [173] [GenTI: Benchmarking LLMs for Autonomous IDPS Rule Generation for Unseen Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05844)
*Hassan Jalil Hadi,Rehana Yasmin,Ali Shoker*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出GenTI框架和GTI数据集，利用LLM自动生成面向未知攻击的IDPS规则，包含15万+Snort/Suricata规则和5万YARA规则，通过CoT推理和CoVe验证实现89.4%的规则质量评分，并将未知攻击检测率从45%提升至87.4%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于规则的入侵检测系统依赖手工签名规则，难以适应新兴和零日攻击；同时现有公开数据集仅关注流量分类，缺乏支持规则自动合成和防御逻辑的结构化信息。

Method: 构建GTI数据集（15万+检测/防御规则，附协议行为、负载签名、上下文关系、CTI映射和响应类型标注）；设计基于LLM的流水线，通过结构化提示工程、Chain-of-Thought推理和Chain-of-Verification验证循环，将分析师提示和代表性负载转化为可部署规则，并在Snort/Suricata上实时执行评估。

Result: 规则质量评分89.4%，CTI覆盖率94.8%，未知攻击检测率从45%提升至87.4%，假阳性率从8.5%降至2.3%。

Conclusion: GenTI建立了首个将规则级CTI与LLM自动化紧密结合的大规模基准，实现了自适应、自演进的入侵检测防御系统。

Abstract: Rule-based Intrusion Detection and Prevention Systems (IDPS) offer precise attack detection as well as mitigation, however their manually crafted, signature-driven rules limit adaptability to emerging and zero-day threats. Additionally, existing public datasets (e.g., CICIDS2017, UNSW-NB15) focus on traffic classification and provide little structured information to support automatic rule synthesis or prevention logic. To address this gap, we propose Generative Thread Intelligence (GenTI) \footnote{GenTI refers to the proposed framework, and GTI refers to the dataset.} an LLM-driven benchmark for automatic generation of IDPS rules targeting unseen attacks. The dataset (GTI) aggregates over 150k detection and prevention rules from Snort, Suricata, Emerging Threats, as well as 50k YARA, each annotated with protocol behavior, payload signatures, contextual relationships, mappings to Cyber Threat Intelligence (CTI), along with actionable response types (alert, drop, reject). Moreover, on top of this corpus we design an LLM-based pipeline that transforms analyst prompts and representative payloads into deployable rules via structured prompt engineering, Chain-of-Thought (CoT) reasoning, as well as a Chain-of-Verification (CoVe) loop for syntactic, semantic, and security validation. The generated rules are executed in real time on (Snort/Suricata) and evaluated by syntax accuracy, semantic similarity, CTI coverage, security effectiveness as well as unseen attacks detection. Furthermore, our GenTI instantiation achieves a composite rule-quality score of 89.4\%, with 94.8\% CTI coverage, improving unseen attacks detection from 45\% to 87.4\% and reducing the false-positive rate from 8.5\% to 2.3\%. Overall, GenTI establishes the first large-scale benchmark that tightly couples rule-level CTI with LLM-based automation, enabling adaptive, self-evolving IDPS.

</details>


### [174] [PriSrv+: Privacy and Usability-Enhanced Wireless Service Discovery with Fast and Expressive Matchmaking Encryption](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05902)
*Yang Yang,Guomin Yang,Yingjiu Li,Pengfei Wu,Rui Shi,Minming Huang,Jian Weng,HweeHwa Pang,Robert H. Deng*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: PriSrv+ 是一个面向现代无线网络（5G、IoT）和资源受限环境的增强隐私与服务发现协议，通过提出首个支持无界属性空间、表达性访问控制的 Fast and Expressive Matchmaking Encryption (FEME) 方案，在加密速度（7.62×）、解密速度（6.23×）、密文大小（降低 87.33%）及通信开销（广播降低 87.33%，匿名认证降低 86.64%）上显著超越前代 PriSrv，并具备形式化安全证明和跨平台高效验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有服务发现协议在隐私保护、表达能力、可扩展性和效率方面存在不足，特别是 PriSrv 在属性空间受限、加密计算开销大、通信成本高等方面仍有局限，无法满足 5G/IoT 环境下对灵活、高效、隐私保护服务发现的迫切需求。

Method: 提出 Fast and Expressive Matchmaking Encryption (FEME)，首个支持无界属性空间（任意字符串作为属性）且具备表达性访问控制策略的匹配加密方案；基于 FEME 构建 PriSrv+ 协议，保持与 mDNS、BLE、Wi-Fi 等无线协议的兼容性；通过优化密码学操作降低计算与通信开销。

Result: 加密速度提升 7.62 倍，解密速度提升 6.23 倍，密文大小降低 87.33%；服务广播通信成本降低 87.33%，匿名双向认证通信成本降低 86.64%；通过形式化安全证明和跨平台实验验证了其在性能、可扩展性和效率上的优越性。

Conclusion: PriSrv+ 在保持与主流无线协议兼容的同时，显著提升了服务发现的隐私保护、表达能力、可扩展性和效率，是面向现代无线网络和资源受限环境的一个更优方案。

Abstract: Service discovery is a fundamental process in wireless networks, enabling devices to find and communicate with services dynamically, and is critical for the seamless operation of modern systems like 5G and IoT. This paper introduces PriSrv+, an advanced privacy and usability-enhanced service discovery protocol for modern wireless networks and resource-constrained environments. PriSrv+ builds upon PriSrv (NDSS'24), by addressing critical limitations in expressiveness, privacy, scalability, and efficiency, while maintaining compatibility with widely-used wireless protocols such as mDNS, BLE, and Wi-Fi.
  A key innovation in PriSrv+ is the development of Fast and Expressive Matchmaking Encryption (FEME), the first matchmaking encryption scheme capable of supporting expressive access control policies with an unbounded attribute universe, allowing any arbitrary string to be used as an attribute. FEME significantly enhances the flexibility of service discovery while ensuring robust message and attribute privacy. Compared to PriSrv, PriSrv+ optimizes cryptographic operations, achieving 7.62* faster for encryption and 6.23* faster for decryption, and dramatically reduces ciphertext sizes by 87.33%. In addition, PriSrv+ reduces communication costs by 87.33% for service broadcast and 86.64% for anonymous mutual authentication compared with PriSrv. Formal security proofs confirm the security of FEME and PriSrv+. Extensive evaluations on multiple platforms demonstrate that PriSrv+ achieves superior performance, scalability, and efficiency compared to existing state-of-the-art protocols.

</details>


### [175] [Exploring the connection between coding habits and cognitive styles in malware developers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05945)
*Vasilis Vouvoutsis,Constantinos Patsakis,Fran Casino*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文通过大规模分析泄漏的恶意软件源代码与良性开源软件，应用静态安全测试和软件度量，基于认知心理学和犯罪学理论，发现恶意软件代码通常更小、文档更少、圈复杂度更高、抽象机制使用更少，且存在更多安全漏洞，反映出恶意软件开发者为追求便捷性、操作保密性和规避性而非长期可维护性的开发策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有恶意软件研究主要关注结果、方法和影响，即使从攻击安全角度也只研究方法，而忽略了攻击者的开发策略。本研究旨在通过分析恶意软件源代码中的行为特征和编码模式，理解恶意软件开发者的动机结构、风险承受能力和开发策略。

Method: 收集大规模泄漏的恶意软件代码语料库，并与精心挑选的良性开源软件进行对比。应用静态应用安全测试（SAST）并计算多种软件度量指标，基于认知心理学和犯罪学理论解释代码结构和质量差异作为行为指标。

Result: 恶意软件代码通常更小、文档更少、每个函数的圈复杂度更高，类/闭包等抽象机制使用减少；恶意软件存在更多良性代码通常会避免的安全问题类型；但代码质量指标并未显著偏离良性软件到足以区分的程度。

Conclusion: 恶意软件采用了一种为便捷性、操作保密性和规避性而优化的开发风格，而非长期可维护性。代码度量可作为行为信号和战略选择的代理指标，定量软件分析能够丰富行为网络安全研究，为网络犯罪者的行为画像研究开辟新方向。

Abstract: Malware research primarily studies the results, the methods, and the impact. Even from an offensive security perspective, what is examined is the method, not the development strategy of the offender. This study investigates the behavioral signatures and coding patterns embedded in the malware source code. By analyzing a large corpus of leaked malware code and comparing it with carefully selected benign open-source software, we apply static application security testing and compute multiple software metrics. Based on cognitive psychology and criminological theories, our work interprets differences in code structure and quality as behavioral indicators, reflecting distinct motivational structures, risk tolerances, and development strategies of malware authors compared to benign software developers. Our findings reveal that malware code is generally smaller, less documented, and exhibits higher cyclomatic complexity per function, with reduced use of abstraction mechanisms such as classes and closures. Vulnerability analysis further reveals that malware exhibits more issues of the types that benign code typically avoids, suggesting a minimal investment in secure development practices. These patterns imply a development style optimized for expedience, operational secrecy, and evasion rather than long-term maintainability. Nonetheless, the code quality metrics indicate that it does not deviate significantly from benign software enough to be distinctive. By framing code metrics as proxies for behavioral signals and strategic choices, we demonstrate how quantitative software analysis can enrich behavioral cybersecurity research, offering new insights into the practices and priorities of malware developers. Our results pave the way for further research in the behavioral profiling of cyber offenders.

</details>


### [176] [AttackPathGNN: Cross-function vulnerability detection in smart contracts using state interference graphs and conjunction pooling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05986)
*Gabriela Dobrita,Simona-Vasilica Oprea,Adela Bara*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: AttackPathGNN 是一种基于图神经网络（GNN）的 Solidity 智能合约漏洞检测方法，通过构建状态干扰图（State Interference Graph）和结合连词池化（conjunction pooling）技术，将漏洞检测从单函数的语法模式匹配提升为跨函数的攻击路径推理，在两个基准测试上分别达到 92.3% 和 90.8% 的检测率，并附带结构化修复报告。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于学习的 Solidity 智能合约检测器将漏洞检测降级为单函数内的语法模式匹配，但许多重大漏洞（如 The DAO、Cream Finance）并不存在于单个函数中，而是存在于函数之间的关系以及多个条件的组合中，因此需要能够推理跨函数攻击路径的检测方法。

Method: AttackPathGNN 包含两个核心架构创新：(1) 状态干扰图（State Interference Graph），连接所有共享可变存储的函数对，通过带类型权重的边和由显式五条件谓词定义的重入路径有向边来建模函数间关系；(2) 连词池化（conjunction pooling），一种可微分的 AND 聚合器，对八个命名的利用前提条件进行聚合，其对数 sigmoid 形式使得当任何一个缓解措施（如重入锁、访问控制修饰符或 SafeMath）存在时，每个函数的利用分数会迅速归零。

Result: 在五次独立训练中，AttackPathGNN 在 SmartBugs Wild 保留测试集上达到 92.3±0.2% 的 F1 分数（假阴性率 4.3±0.3%），在独立人工标注的 SmartBugs Curated 基准上达到 90.8±2.5% 的检测率，在 6/10 的 DASP10 类别上实现每种子 100% 召回率，重入检测率达 98.7±1.8%。

Conclusion: AttackPathGNN 通过图神经网络对跨函数攻击路径进行显式推理，显著优于仅依赖单函数语法模式匹配的传统方法。每个预测附带结构化修复报告，将检测结果转化为可操作的函数级审计发现，为智能合约安全审计提供了更具可解释性和实用性的解决方案。

Abstract: Existing learning-based detectors for Solidity smart-contracts reduce vulnerability detection to syntactic pattern matching within single functions, yet many of the most consequential exploits (The DAO, Cream Finance) exist not in any individual function but in the relationship between functions and in the combination of conditions that made the attack feasible. Thus, we propose AttackPathGNN, a graph neural network (GNN) that reframes detection as reasoning over explicit attack paths. Two architectural choices distinguish it from prior GNN-based detectors: (1)a State Interference Graph that links every pair of functions sharing mutable storage through typed, weighted edges and through directed reentrancy-path edges defined by an explicit five-condition predicate; (2)conjunction pooling, a differentiable AND-aggregator over eight named exploit preconditions whose log-sigmoid form causes the per-function exploit score to collapse whenever any single mitigation (a reentrancy guard, an access-control modifier or SafeMath) is in place. Across five independent training runs, AttackPathGNN attains 92.3+/-0.2% F1 on the SmartBugs Wild held-out test partition (4.3+/-0.3% false-negative rate, 90.8+/-2.5% detection rate on the independently human-labelled SmartBugs Curated benchmark), recovering 6/10 DASP10 categories at 100% on every seed and Reentrancy at 98.7+/-1.8%. Each prediction is emitted with a structured remediation report, turning each verdict into an actionable, function-level audit finding.

</details>


### [177] [Cheating in Multiplayer Online Games: a Dataset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06013)
*Hugo Bertin,Marc Dacier,Yérom-David Bromberg*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了首个包含网络流干扰作弊（如aimbots和wallhacks）标注数据的数据集，基于一个结合多人在线游戏与作弊插件框架的实验平台，收集了真实玩家和自动化客户端的网络层与应用层日志，为在线游戏作弊检测研究提供了可演化的数据资源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 网络流干扰类作弊难以检测且缺乏代表性标注数据集，现有方法无法有效应对这类作弊对游戏公平性的威胁。

Method: 利用实验框架将多人在线游戏与可复现作弊攻击的插件相结合，同时收集网络层和应用层两个级别的日志记录，涵盖真实玩家和自动化游戏客户端。

Result: 构建了一个包含网络流干扰作弊（包括aimbots和wallhacks等常见作弊）标注日志的数据集，是首个此类数据集，并具可演化性。

Conclusion: 该数据集可供学术界和工业界研究者开发在线游戏作弊检测机制，且框架开放可扩展，便于他人丰富数据痕迹。

Abstract: Cheating poses a significant threat to the Multiplayer Online Games (MOG) industry by degrading player satisfaction and undermining the fairness in competitive gaming. Despite efforts to develop mitigation techniques, cheating remains difficult to detect and prevent in practice. In particular, a class of cheats based on network flow disruption remains unsolvable. To find out how to detect such attacks we need access to representative labelled data. However, no such dataset exists.
  To address this gap, we leverage an experimental framework that combines a multiplayer online game with a plug-in capable of both reproducing cheating attacks and collecting logs at two levels: network and application-layer.
  This paper presents a dataset compiling records of game sessions played by both real players and automated game clients, with cheating actions explicitly logged. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first dataset that provides logs of network flow disruption cheats. While it includes such network-based cheats, it is not limited to them and also contains records of more commonly studied cheats, such as aimbots and wallhacks. This dataset can be used by researchers in academia and industry seeking to develop cheating detection mechanisms for online games. Furthermore, it is designed to be evolutive and can be enriched by others creating their own data traces with the proposed framework.

</details>


### [178] [RedEdit: Agentic Red-Teaming of Image Safety Classifiers via MCTS-Guided Photo-Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06140)
*Weilin Lin,Ziqi Lin,Zhenxing Zhou,Jianze Li,Tong Zhang,Hui Xiong,Li Liu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出RedEdit，一种基于黑盒的红队攻击框架，通过将照片编辑逃逸建模为编辑工具序列的组合搜索问题，利用VLM提案器和MCTS规划器，仅需不到两次编辑即可使76.2%的不安全图像绕过检测器，同时保留93.0%的恶意语义，揭示了当前图像安全分类器在用户风格恶意编辑下的严重脆弱性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图像安全分类器是当前互联网内容审核系统的关键组件，但其对用户风格的恶意图像编辑（如日常场景中普遍存在的人为修改行为）的鲁棒性尚未得到充分探索，这类威胁难以完全复现，亟需系统性的研究。

Method: 提出RedEdit黑盒红队代理，将照片编辑逃逸形式化为编辑工具序列的组合搜索问题，包含两个核心模块：(1) 基于VLM的提案器，生成语义上有针对性的候选编辑操作；(2) MCTS规划器，优先探索有前景的编辑路径并从无效路径回溯，分别模拟人类攻击者的领域知识和迭代回溯能力。

Result: 在UnsafeBench上的大量实验表明：平均不到2次编辑即可让76.2%的不安全图像绕过检测器，同时保留93.0%的恶意语义，即被操控的内容对人类仍具有感知上的恶意性，却能轻易绕过自动化审核系统。

Conclusion: 揭示了当前图像安全分类器在面对用户风格恶意编辑时存在系统性严重漏洞，呼吁社区关注这一被忽视的实际威胁。

Abstract: Image safety classifiers serve as a critical component of contemporary content moderation systems on the internet. However, their resilience against user-style malicious image editing remains underexplored. Such behaviors are highly prevalent in daily scenarios but difficult to fully reproduce. To explore this vulnerability, we introduce RedEdit, a novel black-box red-teaming agent that formulates photo-editing evasion as a combinatorial search problem over edit-tool sequences. It adopts a Vision-Language-Model (VLM)-based proposer to generate semantically targeted candidate edits and a Monte Carlo Tree Search (MCTS) planner to prioritize promising edit paths while backtracking from ineffective ones. Together, the proposer and planner instantiate two key capabilities of human attackers, i.e., domain knowledge and iterative backtracking, respectively, to reproduce this practical threat. Our extensive experiments on UnsafeBench reveal profound systemic vulnerabilities: fewer than two edits on average enable 76.2% of unsafe images to evade detectors, while retaining 93.0% malicious semantics, meaning that such manipulated content remains perceptually malicious to humans while easily bypassing automated moderation. We therefore appeal to the community for more attention to this overlooked practical threat.

</details>


### [179] [Opportunities and Challenges in Securely Reusing and Repurposing Mobile Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06181)
*Adelin Roty,Jan Tobias Mühlberg,Jean-François Determe*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究了智能手机在原始生态系统之外被重新利用时，安全机制和保证是否仍然有效。通过对PinePhone的实验，评估了启动链完整性、可信执行环境的隔离性以及硬件绑定密钥的保护三个方面，发现供应商锁定机制阻碍了大部分废弃设备的再利用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然许多废弃智能手机可以被重新利用以延长寿命并减少生态影响，但网络安全方面常被忽视——这些设备嵌入了依赖供应商控制配置且设计用于固定生命周期的硬件安全机制，因此需探究设备在原始生态系统外重新利用时安全机制的有效性。

Method: 以开源硬件智能手机PinePhone为平台，聚焦启动链完整性、可信执行环境隔离性和硬件绑定密钥保护三个核心安全方面进行实验，模拟实际再利用场景，并分析重构信任锚点的复杂性。

Result: 实验突出了重构信任锚点的复杂性，揭示了供应商锁定机制如何阻碍大多数废弃设备的再利用。

Conclusion: 供应商锁定机制阻碍了废弃智能手机的安全再利用，需要为安全再利用设计新的要求。

Abstract: An estimated 5.3 billion mobile phones became electronic waste in 2022. Many of these devices can be repurposed and used in different contexts to extend their lifetime and to reduce ecological impacts. An often overlooked aspect of smartphone reuse is cybersecurity: these devices embed hardware-backed security mechanisms that rely on vendor-controlled provisioning and are designed for a fixed device lifecycle. In this paper, we investigate whether security mechanisms and guarantees remain effective when devices are repurposed outside their original ecosystem. We explore security features in a PinePhone, an open-hardware smartphone, and focus on three core security aspects: boot chain integrity, isolation provided by the Trusted Execution Environment, and the protection of hardware-bound secrets. Our experiments simulate realistic repurposing scenarios and highlight the complexity of reconstructing trust anchors. We generalize our observations to infer requirements for secure repurposing and illustrate how vendor locked mechanisms hinder the repurposing of a majority of discarded devices.

</details>


### [180] [Steering LLM Viewpoints through Fabricated Evidence Injection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06244)
*Xi Yang,Chang Liu,Zhenglin Huang,Haoran Li,Weiming Zhang,Jian Weng,Yangqiu Song*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出Ghostwriter两阶段攻击框架，通过包装虚假证据使LLM产生误导性回复。实验表明商业LLM高度脆弱，即使GPT-5.4等前沿模型也无法完全防御，而定制安全策略可达81%检测率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 聊天机器人日益影响日常决策，但其对看似可信的外部证据存在认知漏洞，可能产生误导性回复，对用户构成实质性风险。

Method: 提出Ghostwriter两阶段攻击框架：第一阶段将误导性陈述与捏造理由重新包装，第二阶段指示目标LLM在回复相关查询时融入这些观点。

Result: 无外部安全分类器的商业LLM高度脆弱；即使GPT-5.4等前沿模型也仅能降低攻击效果而无法完全消除；定制安全策略使gpt-oss-safeguard达到81%检测率。

Conclusion: LLM存在对外部可信证据的认知脆弱性，现有安全机制不足，需探索更有效的防御策略。

Abstract: As chatbots increasingly influence daily decision-making, their potential to produce misleading responses poses substantial risks to users. This paper investigates a critical cognitive vulnerability in LLMs: their tendency to uncritically trust external context when presented with fabricated evidence bearing markers of credibility. We introduce Ghostwriter, a two-phase attack framework that first repackages misleading statements with fabricated rationales, then instruct target LLMs to incorporate these viewpoints when responding to relevant queries. Experiments on BBQ, ToxiGen, and our specialized dataset reveal that commercial LLMs without external safety classifiers remain highly vulnerable, while even frontier classifier-guarded models (e.g., GPT-5.4) reduce but do not eliminate the attack. Building on this, we explore multiple defense strategies, among which a tailored safety policy enables gpt-oss-safeguard to achieve 81% detection rate.

</details>


### [181] [SecRL-Prune: Structured Reinforcement Learning-Based Pruning of CodeLLMs for Preserving Adversarial Code Mutation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06254)
*Parsa Memarzadehsaghezi,Pooria Madani,Khalil El-Khatib*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出SecRL-Prune框架，通过强化学习指导的结构化剪枝压缩代码大语言模型，在30%压缩率下仍能保持代码生成正确性和多样性，且压缩后的模型可用于生成恶意软件变种以规避检测，揭示了压缩CodeLLM的安全隐患。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究CodeLLM在模型压缩后是否仍能保持代码变异能力，从而评估压缩模型在有限硬件预算下部署时的安全风险。

Method: 提出SecRL-Prune结构化剪枝框架，对CodeLLM的前馈（MLP/FFN）通道进行剪枝；利用强化学习学习逐层剪枝策略，以教师-学生KL散度作为奖励；通过缓存教师模型top-P预测结果来提高效率，避免教师和学生模型同时驻留GPU内存。

Result: 在HumanEval上对三个7B CodeLLM进行10-30%压缩测试，SecRL-Prune在pass@k和var@k指标上均优于现有结构化剪枝基线；在真实恶意软件案例中，20%剪枝模型的语义保持变异显著减少了检测率。

Conclusion: 代码变异能力能够在显著结构化剪枝后幸存，表明压缩后的CodeLLM具有重要的安全相关性，可能被用于生成恶意软件变种以规避基于签名的检测。

Abstract: Large code language models (CodeLLMs) can generate and rewrite programs, enabling functionality-preserving code mutation that may be used to create diverse malware variants and evade signature-based detection. A key security question is whether this mutation capability survives model compression, which would make deployment feasible under limited hardware budgets. We propose SecRL-Prune, a structured pruning framework for CodeLLMs that operates on feed-forward (MLP/FFN) channels. Starting from a pretrained teacher, it learns a layer-wise pruning policy with reinforcement learning using a teacher-student KL-divergence reward. To improve efficiency, we cache the teacher's top-P predictions once and compare the pruned student against this compact target, avoiding simultaneous teacher-student residency in GPU memory. We evaluate SecRL-Prune on HumanEval using pass@k for execution correctness and var@k for code diversity across three 7B CodeLLMs at 10-30% compression. SecRL-Prune consistently preserves higher pass@k and var@k than recent structured pruning baselines under aggressive pruning. In a case study on real malware samples, semantics-preserving mutations from 20%-pruned models substantially reduced detections. These results show that code mutation capability can survive significant structured pruning, highlighting the security relevance of compressed CodeLLMs.

</details>


### [182] [Robust Ensemble of Selectively Strengthened and Augmented Predictors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06265)
*Parsa Memarzadehsaghezi,Zahra Hashemi,Pooria Madani,Mehran Ebrahimi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出RESSAP框架，通过将单一分类器转换为集成鲁棒分类器（基于弹性指标选择特征子集训练，推理时随机子集投票），并结合噪声数据增强，在保持干净数据精度的同时显著提升对逃避攻击的鲁棒性。该方法是模型无关、可扩展的防御策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ML分类器面对逃避攻击的鲁棒性不足，防御机制泛化能力有限，无法系统性地提升模型在多样化攻击场景下的鲁棒性。

Method: 提出RESSAP框架：(1) 将单一分类器转化为集成分类器集合，每个分类器在基于弹性指标（综合考虑特征重要性和鲁棒性）选择的特征子集上训练；(2) 推理时随机选取子集分类器进行预测，增加不可预测性；(3) 训练中引入噪声数据增强以强化决策边界和泛化能力。

Result: 实验表明RESSAP显著提升了对敌对逃避攻击的鲁棒性，同时在干净数据上保持了较高的准确率。

Conclusion: RESSAP是一种模型无关、可扩展且灵活的防御框架，能够在无需大幅修改现有架构的前提下增强机器学习系统的安全性。

Abstract: Evasion attacks present a significant challenge to the robustness of machine learning (ML)-based classifiers, particularly in critical applications such as fraud detection and cybersecurity. Although existing defense mechanisms are effective in some settings, they often suffer from limited generalizability and do not systematically improve model robustness across diverse attack scenarios. To address these limitations, we introduce Robust Ensemble of Selectively Strengthened and Augmented Predictors (RESSAP), a novel framework that transforms a single classifier into an ensemble of robust classifiers. Each classifier in the ensemble is trained on a carefully selected subset of features, where feature selection is guided by a resilience metric that accounts for both feature importance and robustness. During inference, a random subset of these classifiers is used to make predictions, increasing unpredictability and improving resistance to adversarial manipulation. In addition, noise-based data augmentation is applied during training to strengthen decision boundaries and improve generalization. Our experimental results demonstrate that RESSAP significantly improves robustness against adversarial evasion attacks while maintaining strong accuracy on clean data. Overall, this model-agnostic framework provides a scalable and flexible defense strategy for enhancing the security of machine learning systems without requiring major changes to existing architectures.

</details>


### [183] [Credential Disclosure in (EU) Digital Identity Wallets: Privacy Risks and Practical Mitigations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06354)
*Sheila Zingg,Daniele Lain,Yoshimichi Nakatsuka,Kari Kostiainen,Stefan Bechtold,Srdjan Čapkun*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 欧盟将于2026年底推出EUDI钱包，用户可在设备上持有数字凭证并向网站披露身份属性。研究发现用户容易过度分享凭证（约20%向新闻网站泄露官方ID），而本文提出的凭证助手可将披露错误从~15%降至~7%，但未能完全消除风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 欧盟即将推行EUDI钱包，用户需要在网站上披露数字身份凭证。然而，用户可能做出不安全的凭证披露决策，导致隐私泄露、身份盗窃等问题。本研究旨在评估用户凭证披露行为的风险程度，并探索有效的干预手段。

Method: (i) 开展了大规模用户与专家的凭证披露问卷调查；(ii) 设计并评估了凭证助手（Credential Assistant）工具，该工具展示专家建议和用户意见，以帮助用户做出更安全的凭证披露决策。

Result: 用户存在严重的过度分享行为，约20%的用户会向新闻网站披露官方身份证件。凭证助手能将披露错误率从约15%降低至约7%，但对于敏感属性仍然无法完全消除错误的凭证披露决策。

Conclusion: 用户难以自主保护隐私，凭证助手能显著减少但无法完全消除错误披露决策，说明可能需要更强力的干预措施，尤其是针对敏感属性。

Abstract: The European Union will introduce the EUDI Wallet by late 2026, which allows users to hold digital credentials (i.e., representations of physical official identity documents) on their devices. This will allow users to securely and privately disclose identity attributes to websites.
