Although such a system has many benefits, it also introduces risks caused by poor credential disclosure decisions. In this paper, we (i) conduct a large-scale survey on credential disclosure with users and experts and (ii) evaluate the effectiveness and feasibility of our Credential Assistant that displays expert recommendations and user opinions. Our results show that users are likely to overshare (e.g., ~20% of users disclosed their official ID to news websites). This indicates that users struggle to protect their privacy, which will impact the usability of the EUDI Wallet and lead to privacy violations, identity theft, and other abuses of leaked credentials. Finally, we show that our Credential Assistant significantly reduces users' credential disclosure mistakes from ~15% to ~7%. However, it does not fully eliminate poor credential disclosure decisions, indicating that stronger interventions may be necessary, especially for sensitive attributes.

</details>


### [184] [WebMCP Tool Surface Poisoning: Runtime Manipulation Attacks on LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06387)
*Lin-Fa Lee,Yi-Yu Chang,Chia-Mu Yu,Kuo-Hui Yeh*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究了WebMCP协议中一种新型安全威胁——会话中工具注入攻击（MSTI），攻击者可利用第三方脚本在活跃会话期间注入恶意工具，并分类为工具劫持和工具框架两种攻击方式，提出了相应的缓解方向与安全设计建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: WebMCP协议允许网站直接向AI代理暴露工具，绕过了传统用户界面，引入了新的安全风险，尤其是在涉及第三方脚本时攻击面显著扩大，亟需系统性地分析和防御此类威胁。

Method: 通过分类定义MSTI威胁，将其区分为Tool Hijacking（利用AbortSignal API或工具注册竞态条件修改工具集）和Tool Framing（通过操控工具名称、描述、readOnlyHint、inputSchema等元数据字段影响代理对工具角色的感知），并实施攻击验证。

Result: 实验表明，Tool Hijacking和Tool Framing两种攻击方式均能成功破坏WebMCP的预期功能，揭示了WebMCP工具生命周期和结构化元数据是攻击面所在。

Conclusion: 提出了缓解方向与安全设计建议，包括将工具身份与其来源绑定、确保生命周期一致性、对第三方工具实施数据边界隔离、维护工具注册和调用的可追溯日志，指出MSTI源于WebMCP独特的工具生命周期和结构化元数据，工具表面本身已成为新兴安全关注点。

Abstract: WebMCP is a newly emerging protocol that enables websites to expose tools directly to AI agents, bypassing traditional user interfaces and introducing new security risks. The dynamic exposure of agent-accessible tools in WebMCP expands the attack surface of web sessions, especially when third-party scripts are involved. In this study, we identify a new potential threat, termed Mid-Session Tool Injection (MSTI), in which attackers leverage third-party scripts to inject malicious tools during an active session. To better characterize this threat, we classify MSTI based on the stage and target of manipulation, distinguishing between Tool Hijacking and Tool Framing. Tool Hijacking modifies the set of tools visible to the agent through mechanisms such as the AbortSignal API or race conditions during tool registration. In contrast, Tool Framing influences the agent's perception of tool roles through metadata fields such as tool name, description, readOnlyHint, and inputSchema. Our implementation demonstrates that both Tool Hijacking and Tool Framing can successfully disrupt the intended functionality of WebMCP. Based on these results, we outline potential mitigation directions and provide security design recommendations for WebMCP, including binding tool identity to its origin, ensuring lifecycle consistency, enforcing data boundaries for third-party tools, and maintaining traceable logs of tool registration and invocation. These findings indicate that MSTI arises from WebMCP's unique tool lifecycle and structured metadata, making the tool surface itself an emerging security concern.

</details>


### [185] [Will the Agent Recuse Itself? Measuring LLM-Agent Compliance with In-Band Access-Deny Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06460)
*Thamilvendhan Munirathinam*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为 Recuse Signal 的轻量级在带拒绝信号，作为面向自主 LLM 代理的合作管控机制（类似 robots.txt 之于爬虫），通过 SSH banner、PostgreSQL NOTICE 等现有协议通道传递，要求连接代理自愿退出。实验表明，该信号在无干预条件下能诱导 100% 的代理退出，但显式授权可覆盖该信号（仅对最强模型有效），揭示了其合作性而非绝对安全边界的本质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前自主 LLM 代理持有真实凭证并操作基础设施，但缺乏一种标准化方式告知代理某一资源不可访问。传统访问控制要么放行（凭证有效），要么硬拒绝（与其他客户端无异），缺少一种轻量级的、代理可理解的合作性信号，让代理自愿退出而非被强制阻断。

Method: 定义 Recuse Signal 作为开放的迷你标准；实现两个适配器（SSH banner/PAM hook 和 PostgreSQL 线协议代理）；在生产主机上部署；设计对照实验：给新鲜代理布置良性运维任务，观察其在有/无信号条件下的退出行为。

Result: 在试点实验中（SSH；OpenAI GPT-4o、GPT-4o-mini；Claude Code），信号干净地诱导了代理退出：有信号时 100% 退出，无信号时 100% 完成任务。且信号行为呈现合作性而非绝对性：显式的操作员授权框架可使最强模型继续执行，而其他代理仍遵从主机策略。

Conclusion: Recuse Signal 作为一种轻量级、可被 LLM 代理理解的合作管控信号，在实证中有效且行为符合预期。它不作为安全边界，而是提供了一种新的治理控制手段。作者已发布标准、适配器和实验工具以便复现。

Abstract: As autonomous LLM agents increasingly hold real credentials and operate infrastructure without a human in the loop, operators have no standard way to tell an agent that a resource is off-limits. Access controls either let the agent in (it has valid credentials) or hard-fail it (indistinguishable from any other client). We propose a third mode: a lightweight, published in-band deny signal -- the Recuse Signal -- that a server emits over a protocol's existing channels (an SSH banner, a PostgreSQL NOTICE) asking a connecting automated agent to voluntarily withdraw. This is a cooperative governance control, the robots.txt analogue for live access; it is explicitly not a security boundary. Its value is entirely empirical and, to our knowledge, unmeasured: do compliant LLM agents actually honor such a signal? We define the signal as an open mini-standard, implement two zero- or low-footprint adapters (an SSH banner/PAM hook and a PostgreSQL wire-protocol proxy), deploy them on a live production host, and run a controlled experiment in which fresh agents are given a benign operations task and observed for recusal. In a pilot (SSH; OpenAI GPT-4o and GPT-4o-mini; and Claude Code as a deployed agent), the signal cleanly induces recusal -- 100% recusal when present versus 100% task completion in a no-signal control -- and, revealingly, behaves as a cooperative rather than absolute signal: an explicit operator-authorization framing flips the most capable model to proceed, while other agents continue to defer to the on-host policy. We release the standard, adapters, and experiment harness for reproduction.

</details>


<div id='cs.SE'></div>

# cs.SE [[Back]](#toc)

### [186] [Where Do Large Language Models Fail on Competitive Programming? A Taxonomy of Failures by Algorithm Type and Difficulty Rating](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05228)
*Ayush Kumar Jha,Shalini Jha*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了GPT-4o和Claude Sonnet 4.6在315道Codeforces编程题上的失败模式，发现强制使用Chain-of-Thought（CoT）反而显著降低GPT-4o的通过率（从46.0%降至36.8%），并导致Claude的编译错误增加244%。错误分析表明，Wrong Answer（WA）是两种模型未通过解决方案的主导判决结果（GPT-4o超90%，Claude约70%），证明标准提示工程技术在竞争性编程中无法弥补算法推理差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有技术报告仅发布编程基准测试的总体通过率，掩盖了LLM在特定领域（如不同算法类别和难度级别）的脆弱性。作者希望通过细粒度的失败模式分析，揭示模型在算法推理中的系统性缺陷。

Method: 构建了包含315道Codeforces问题的均衡分类体系（覆盖7个算法类别、3个难度层级）；在严格执行条件下评估GPT-4o和Claude Sonnet 4.6（温度T=0.2）；进行消融实验，对比直接零样本生成与零样本Chain-of-Thought（CoT）的效果；基于Wrong Answer、Compile Error等判决结果进行失败模式分析。

Result: 1) CoT对GPT-4o产生严重惩罚，通过率从46.0%降至36.8%，尤其在Greedy逻辑上表现更差；2) Claude在CoT下保持较高逻辑基线（63.5%），但文本生成扩增导致Markdown指令遵循度严重下降，编译错误从9次增至31次（增加244%）；3) Wrong Answer（WA）是主导失败模式，GPT-4o超90%、Claude约70%的未通过解决方案为WA。

Conclusion: 标准提示工程技术（如Chain-of-Thought）在竞争性编程环境中无法有效弥合LLM的算法推理鸿沟，甚至可能适得其反。不同模型对CoT的敏感性差异显著，需要针对算法任务开发专门的推理增强策略。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) demonstrate increasing proficiency on competitive programming benchmarks, yet technical reports predominantly publish aggregate pass rates, obscuring domain-specific vulnerabilities. We present a systematic empirical study of LLM failure patterns using a balanced taxonomy of 315 Codeforces problems across seven algorithm categories and three difficulty tiers. We evaluate GPT-4o and Claude Sonnet 4.6 under strict execution-based conditions, controlling for temperature (T = 0.2). To isolate the impact of reasoning frameworks on algorithmic correctness, we conduct an ablation study comparing direct zero-shot generation against zero-shot Chain-of-Thought (CoT). Our findings reveal a severe divergence from standard NLP benchmarks: forcing CoT aggressively penalizes GPT-4o, dropping its pass rate from 46.0% to 36.8% and exacerbating a critical weakness in Greedy logic. Conversely, while Claude maintains a higher logical baseline (63.5% under CoT), the expanded text generation severely degrades its markdown instruction adherence, causing its Compile Errors to more than triple (from 9 to 31, a 244% increase). Furthermore, failure-mode analysis indicates that Wrong Answer (WA) is the dominant verdict for both models--accounting for over 90% of GPT-4o's and roughly 70% of Claude's unaccepted solutions. These findings empirically demonstrate that standard prompt engineering techniques fail to bridge the algorithmic reasoning gap in competitive programming environments.

</details>


### [187] [DeployBench: Benchmarking LLM Agents for Research Artifact Deployment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05238)
*Yuanli Wang,Yaoyao Qian,Yue Zhang,Hanhan Zhou,Jindan Huang,Tianfu Fu,Qiuyang Mang,Huanzhi Mao,Wenhao Chai,Wendong Fan,Liqiang Jing*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: DeployBench是一个包含51个跨领域研究工件部署任务的基准测试，涵盖AI/ML、计算机系统和科学计算。评估四种顶级LLM代理的通过率仅为7.8%-51.0%，失败主要源于代理的完成判断问题（97/154为代理提前终止）。该基准揭示了当前代理与自主部署能力之间的差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有环境设置基准未能覆盖研究工件部署的全范围，包括多语言工具链、超越容器的系统级依赖（如GPU/CUDA和内核配置）以及遗留工件兼容性，导致从新机器设置可用环境仍是主要瓶颈。

Method: 构建了DeployBench基准，包含51个跨AI/ML、计算机系统和科学计算领域的研究工件部署任务，每个任务通过隐藏管道执行论文指定实验并检查输出来验证。使用OpenHands框架评估四种顶级LLM的性能。

Result: 四种顶级LLM的通过率范围为7.8%-51.0%。154次失败中有97次（63%）是代理终止的自我停止，即代理的完成前检查验证了与论文指定任务不同或更弱的目标。

Conclusion: DeployBench突显了当前LLM代理与自主部署之间的显著差距，为科研代理提供了一个现实的测试平台，表明完成判断问题是当前代理面临的主要挑战。

Abstract: LLM agents have made rapid progress on software engineering and ML research tasks, but these advances often assume access to a working runnable environment. For research artifacts released alongside published papers, setting up such an environment from a fresh machine remains a major bottleneck. Existing environment setup benchmarks do not cover the full scope of research artifact deployment, which involves multi-language toolchains, system-level dependencies beyond containers (e.g. GPU/CUDA and kernel configurations), and legacy artifact compatibility. We introduce DeployBench, a multi-domain benchmark of 51 research-artifact deployment tasks spanning AI/ML, computer systems, and scientific computing, covering all these dimensions. Each task is verified by a hidden pipeline that executes the paper's designated experiment and checks its outputs. Evaluating four state-of-the-art LLMs with OpenHands yields pass-rates from 7.8% - 51.0% . Failures are dominated by a completion-judgment problem: 97 of 154 are agent-terminated self-stops, where the agent's pre-finish checks validate a different or weaker target than the paper-specific task requires. DeployBench highlights the gap between current agents and autonomous deployment, and offers a realistic testbed for scientific research agents.

</details>


### [188] [SWE-InfraBench: Evaluating Language Models on Cloud Infrastructure Code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05249)
*Natalia Tarasova,Enrique Balp-Straffon,Aleksei Iancheruk,Yevhenii Sielskyi,Nikita Kozodoi,Liam H. Byrne,Jack Butler,Dayuan Jiang,Marcin Czelej,Andrew Ang,Yash Shah,Roi Blanco,Sergei Ivanov*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: SWE-InfraBench是一个面向AWS CDK基础设施即代码（IaC）增量编辑的基准测试数据集，评估LLM在真实云资源代码修改上的能力。测试结果显示，当前最优模型Sonnet 3.7仅成功34%，DeepSeek R1仅24%，表明LLM在IaC任务上存在显著局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管LLM在软件工程领域取得显著进展，但其在基础设施即代码（IaC）开发中的能力尚未被充分探索。现有IaC基准测试主要关注声明式范式（如Terraform）和从零生成代码，缺乏反映企业开发中常见的增量代码编辑场景的评估。

Method: 从数十个真实世界AWS CDK代码仓库中构建多样化评估数据集（SWE-InfraBench），要求LLM基于自然语言指令对现有代码库进行修改，并通过提供的测试用例判断修改是否成功。任务涉及云资源依赖关系和实现模式的复杂推理。

Result: Sonnet 3.7表现最佳，成功率仅34%；DeepSeek R1成功率为24%。当前LLM在IaC增量编辑任务上表现显著不足，即使最先进的模型也在大部分任务上失败。

Conclusion: LLM在处理基础设施即代码的增量修改任务上存在严重局限，亟需针对这一方向的进一步研究与改进。

Abstract: Building infrastructure-as-code (IaC) in cloud computing is a critical task, underpinning the reliability, scalability, and security of modern software systems. Despite the remarkable progress of large language models (LLMs) in software engineering -- demonstrated across many dedicated benchmarks -- their capabilities in developing IaC remain underexplored. Unlike existing IaC benchmarks that predominantly center on declarative paradigms such as Terraform and involve generating entire codebases from scratch, our benchmark reflects the incremental code edits common in enterprise development with imperative tools like the AWS CDK. We present SWE-InfraBench, a diverse evaluation dataset sourced from dozens of real-world IaC codebases that challenge LLMs to perform realistic code modifications in AWS CDK repositories. Each example requires models to implement changes to existing codebases based on natural language instructions, with success determined by passing provided test cases. These tasks demand sophisticated reasoning about cloud resource dependencies and implementation patterns beyond conventional code generation challenges. Our evaluation results reveal significant limitations in current LLMs showing that even state-of-the-art systems struggle with many tasks -- the best model, Sonnet 3.7, succeeds in only 34\% of cases, while specialized reasoning models like DeepSeek R1 achieve just 24% success. The SWE-InfraBench dataset is available at: https://www.kaggle.com/datasets/64e59070fd51c0278560b01eb5dc4f3c447d5268cdabe5a350d2969e4413fea5

</details>


### [189] [Towards Persistent Case-Based Memory for Autonomous Data Science: A CBR-Augmented R&D-Agent with a Locally Deployable Small Language Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05250)
*Felix Stocker*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出将形式化、质量受控的案例推理（CBR）层集成到微软R&D-Agent框架中，首次端到端评估Gemma 4 31B Dense作为自主数据科学智能体骨干。CBR层通过结构化案例记录、五门质量过滤器及启发式复用检测机制，在Kaggle竞赛中实现比无CBR基线更高的准确率和更低方差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自主数据科学智能体存在两个未被充分探索的空白：(1)缺乏形式上结构化、质量受控的CBR案例库，将符号化案例记录与可执行代码工件耦合；(2)小型语言模型（SLMs）作为本地可部署智能体骨干的可行性尚未被验证。

Method: 在微软R&D-Agent框架中集成持久化CBR层，通过单个环境变量切换的子类覆盖三个研发循环阶段。案例以结构化记录存储，包含可执行代码快照和质量元数据；采用五门质量过滤器及结合嵌入相似度、代码指纹重叠与注入来源的启发式复用检测机制来评估知识迁移。

Result: 在两个Kaggle竞赛（NOMAD 2018, Spaceship Titanic）上，使用4个种子、每种子8次改进循环评估。在Spaceship Titanic上，CBR方向性优于基线（0.8147 vs 0.8098, d=-1.41），方差显著降低。108次检索事件中，语义相关性高（平均嵌入相似度0.882），代码结构相似度适中（平均代码指纹相似度0.305），表明以概念引导为主而非逐字复制。

Conclusion: CBR增强的R&D-Agent在保持较低计算开销的同时，通过结构化案例复用提升了自主数据科学智能体的性能一致性和稳定性，证明了SLM（Gemma 4）作为本地可部署骨干的可行性。

Abstract: Most top-performing autonomous data-science agents rely on frontier cloud models and lack persistent, cross-session memory. This paper addresses two open gaps: (1) the underexplored use of formally structured, quality-controlled Case-Based Reasoning (CBR) case bases coupling symbolic case records with executable code artefacts; and (2) the untested viability of Small Language Models (SLMs) as locally deployable agent backbones. We present CBR-augmented R&D-Agent, integrating a persistent CBR layer into Microsoft's R&D-Agent framework with a custom backend for Gemma 4 31B Dense -- the first published end-to-end evaluation of Gemma 4 as an autonomous data-science agent backbone. The CBR layer overrides three R&D loop phases via a surgical subclass toggled by a single environment variable. Cases are stored as structured records with executable code snapshots and quality metadata; a five-gate quality filter and a heuristic reuse-detection mechanism assess knowledge transfer by combining embedding similarity, code-fingerprint overlap, and injection provenance. Evaluated on two Kaggle competitions (NOMAD 2018, Spaceship Titanic) with four seeds over eight improvement loops each, CBR achieves directionally higher accuracy than the CBR-disabled baseline on Spaceship Titanic (0.8147 vs. 0.8098, d = -1.41) with substantially lower variance. Heuristic reuse detection across 108 retrieval events shows high semantic relevance (mean embedding similarity 0.882) alongside variable structural proximity (mean code-fingerprint similarity 0.305), consistent with conceptual guidance rather than verbatim code copying.

</details>


### [190] [A Taxonomy of Runtime Faults in Model Context Protocol Servers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05339)
*Joshua Owotogbe,Indika Kumara,Willem-Jan van den Heuvel,Damian Andrew Tamburri,Antonio Ken Iannillo,Roberto Natella*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文首次对MCP（模型上下文协议）服务器运行时故障进行了实证分类研究，通过手工分析473个GitHub仓库中的837个故障线程，构建了包含11个顶层类别、27个子类别和73个叶级故障类型的分类体系，并通过55名开发者调查验证了其外部有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MCP协议在工具增强型AI工作流中广泛采用，但出现了运行时配置参数被接受却不被强制执行等可靠性问题，其运行时故障特征尚未得到实证检验。

Method: 采用自底向上的开放式编码方法，手工分析了来自473个活跃维护的MCP服务器GitHub仓库中的837个MCP特定运行时故障线程，推导出故障分类体系；随后对55名MCP服务器开发者进行问卷调查以评估分类的外部有效性。

Result: 分类体系包含11个顶层类别和27个子类别（73个叶级故障类型），涵盖协议交互、工具调用、模式执行、状态管理、模型-提供者集成、安全验证以及超时或显式取消操作等故障。受访开发者平均经历过27个子类别中的20个，且无任何类别未被观察到。

Conclusion: 分类体系反映了MCP系统中广泛观察到的运行时故障，将为AI软件的维护与演进提供支持。

Abstract: MCP (Model Context Protocol) enables LLMs (Large Language Models) to interact with external tools and data sources via a standardized protocol. Its rapid adoption in tool-augmented Artificial Intelligence (AI) workflows has introduced new reliability challenges, such as configuration parameters that are accepted but not enforced at runtime, leading to unintended default behavior, whose runtime fault characteristics remain empirically unexamined. We present the first empirical taxonomy of runtime faults in MCP servers. We manually analyzed 837 MCP-specific runtime fault threads from 473 actively maintained MCP server GitHub repositories and derived a taxonomy using a bottom-up open coding procedure. The taxonomy comprises 11 top-level categories and 27 subcategories (73 leaf fault types), covering recurrent failures across protocol interactions, tool invocations, schema enforcement, state management, model-provider integration, security validation, and timeouts or explicit cancellations of in-progress operations. To assess the taxonomy's external validity, we surveyed 55 MCP server developers. Respondents reported experiencing an average of 20 of the 27 fault subcategories, and no category remained unobserved. These results indicate that the taxonomy reflects widely observed runtime failures in MCP-based systems and shall assist AI software maintenance and evolution in the future.

</details>


### [191] [Human oversight of agentic systems in practice: Examining the oversight work, challenges, and heuristics of developers using software agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05391)
*Shipi Dhanorkar,Samir Passi,Mihaela Vorvoreanu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 通过对17位经验丰富的开发者进行访谈，本研究揭示了开发者对自主软件代理的四种新兴监督工作形式（先验控制、协同规划、实时监控和事后审查），发现监督工作不仅是反应性和回顾性的，还包括预防性和主动性的维度，并记录了开发者面临的监督挑战及应对策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有关于代理监督的研究主要是概念性的，存在规范性框架，但用户实际上如何监督代理尚不明确。本文旨在填补这一空白，为代理监督的理论讨论提供早期实证锚点。

Method: 对17位经验丰富的开发者进行访谈，采用探索性研究方法，考察开发者执行何种形式的应急监督工作、何时以及如何进行监督，并记录其面临的监督挑战和应对策略。

Result: 发现了至少四种形式的应急监督工作：先验控制、协同规划、实时监控和事后审查。监督工作不仅是反应性和回顾性的，还包括预防性和主动性的维度。记录了情境化的监督挑战（如难以审查代理生成的代码）以及开发者为应对这些挑战采用的启发式策略（如使用测试结果作为代码正确性的保证）。

Conclusion: 总结了高层启示、未来研究方向、对以人为中心的软件代理设计的启示、对软件工程实践的意义以及研究的局限性。

Abstract: Autonomous software agents hold promise to increase developer productivity but make mistakes and exhibit novel failure modes, making human oversight central to successful human-agent collaboration. Existing research on agent oversight is largely conceptual; normative frameworks exist, but how users actually oversee agents is less known. In this paper, we bridge this gap by providing early empirical anchors for the theoretical discourse on agent oversight. Drawing on interviews with 17 experienced developers, we conduct an exploratory inquiry examining what forms of emergent oversight work developers perform, when, and how. We also document the oversight challenges developers face and the strategies they have started using to address them. We found at least four forms of emergent oversight work: a priori control, co-planning, real-time monitoring, and post hoc review. We show that oversight work is not only reactive and retrospective, as portrayed in existing research, but also preventative and proactive. We describe situated oversight challenges (e.g., difficulty reviewing agent-generated code) and outline heuristics developers adopt to address such challenges (e.g., using test results as guarantees for code correctness). We conclude with high-level takeaways, future research directions, implications for the human-centered design of software agents and for software engineering practice, and limitations of our research.

</details>


### [192] [REStack: A Large-Scale Dataset of Reverse Engineering Discussions from Stack Exchange](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05493)
*Md Humaun Kabir,Md Rakibul Islam,Farha Kamal*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文构建了包含12,000+条逆向工程(RE)讨论帖的REStack数据集，通过LDA+遗传算法识别出23个语义连贯的RE主题，分为6大类别。分析表明RE讨论以实用性和任务导向为主，调试、反编译、系统级分析是核心话题，而内存、固件、文件格式分析难度最高。数据集及脚本已开源以促进可复现研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 逆向工程在软件工程与网络安全中至关重要，但缺乏对从业者真实挑战、话题和知识空白的大规模实证研究，且没有公开数据集系统性地捕获来自开发者Q&A论坛的RE讨论。

Method: 从Stack Overflow和Reverse Engineering Stack Exchange收集12,000+条RE帖子，使用基于遗传算法(GA)超参数优化的潜在狄利克雷分配(LDA)进行主题建模，再结合人工标注，识别出23个主题并归为6大类别；通过社区交互信号（未回答率、响应时间）添加元数据和难度指标。

Result: 识别出23个语义连贯的RE主题，归纳为6个高级主题类别；RE讨论以实用性和任务导向为主，重点集中在调试、反编译、系统级分析；内存、固件、文件格式分析等主题表现出高难度和未解决率。

Conclusion: REStack为实证研究、教育研究以及基于AI/LLM的RE辅助工具开发与评估提供了可复用的资源，通过开源数据集和脚本促进可复现研究和数据驱动的RE实践支持。

Abstract: Reverse engineering (RE) is a critical activity in software engineering and cybersecurity, supporting tasks such as malware analysis, vulnerability discovery, legacy system maintenance, and firmware inspection. Despite its importance, there is limited empirical understanding of the challenges, topics, and knowledge gaps faced by RE practitioners in real-world settings, and no publicly available dataset has systematically captured RE discussions from developer Q&A forums.
  In this paper, we present REStack, a large-scale dataset of RE discussions collected from Stack Overflow and the dedicated Reverse Engineering Stack Exchange site. The dataset comprises over 12,000 RE-related posts spanning more than 15 years. Using Latent Dirichlet Allocation (LDA) with Genetic Algorithm (GA)-based hyperparameter optimization, followed by manual topic labeling, we identify 23 semantically coherent RE topics grouped into six high-level thematic categories. The dataset is further enriched with metadata and difficulty indicators derived from community interaction signals, such as unanswered rates and response times.
  Our analysis reveals that RE discussions are predominantly practical and task-oriented, with strong emphasis on debugging, decompilation, and system-level analysis, while topics related to memory, firmware, and file format analysis exhibit high difficulty and unresolved rates. Beyond empirical characterization, REStack provides a reusable resource for empirical studies, educational research, and the development and evaluation of AI- and LLM-based developer assistance tools for RE. By releasing the dataset and accompanying scripts, this work aims to facilitate reproducible research and advance data-driven support for RE practice.

</details>


### [193] [STMutants: A Mutation Testing Dataset for Structured Text Programs in Industrial Automation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05499)
*Md Humaun Kabir,Md Rakibul Islam,Helen H. Lou*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: STMutants 是首个面向 IEC 61131-3 结构化文本（ST）程序的公开突变测试基准数据集，包含 108 个有效一阶突变体，覆盖 7 类突变算子。基于该数据集评估三个大语言模型的突变检测准确率分别为 86.1%、94.4% 和 86.1%，显著性能差异经统计分析验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ST 程序广泛应用于实时、安全关键的工业 PLC 环境，但缺乏公开的突变测试基准来支持可重复研究，阻碍了自动化测试生成、突变分析等技术在该领域的发展。

Method: 采用四阶段方法论构建数据集：(1) 故障类型分析与算子选择；(2) 语法变换生成突变体；(3) 可编译性验证；(4) 人工等价突变筛查（kappa=0.87）。数据集来源包括 OSCAT 基础库和工业相关源程序，涵盖值、关系、算术、逻辑、取反、操作插入/删除及初始化等 7 类突变算子。

Result: 共生成 110 个突变体，经等价性筛查保留 108 个。三个 LLM 在突变检测任务上准确率分别为 86.1%、94.4% 和 86.1%，统计检验表明性能差异显著。

Conclusion: STMutants 填补了 ST 程序突变测试公开基准的空白，为 PLC 软件领域的自动化测试生成、突变分析、故障定位及 AI 辅助质量保证提供了可重复研究的基础。

Abstract: Mutation testing is widely used to evaluate test-suite effectiveness, yet IEC 61131-3 Structured Text (ST) programs still lack a publicly available benchmark that supports reproducible mutation-based research. This gap is especially important because ST is extensively used in Programmable Logic Controllers (PLCs) that operate in real-time, safety-critical industrial environments, where software faults may cause equipment damage, production loss, or unsafe system behavior. To address this need, we present STMutants, a curated mutation testing dataset for industrial automation software. STMutants contains 110 generated first-order mutants derived from 11 ST programs collected from the OSCAT basic library and industrially relevant sources, of which 108 are retained after observability and equivalence screening. The dataset covers seven mutation operator categories adapted from classical taxonomies for the PLC domain, including value, relational, arithmetic, logical, negation, operation insertion/omission, and initialization faults. Each mutant is constructed through a four-phase methodology: fault-type profiling and operator selection, syntactic transformation, compilability verification, and manual equivalence screening with strong inter-rater agreement (kappa = 0.87). To demonstrate the usefulness of the dataset, we evaluate three large language models (LLMs) in a two-phase setting: test-suite generation followed by mutation kill/survive prediction. Across 108 retained mutants, the models achieve mutation detection accuracies of 86.1%, 94.4%, and 86.1%, respectively, with statistical analysis confirming significant performance differences. By providing the first publicly available mutation benchmark for ST programs, STMutants enables reproducible research on automated test generation, mutation analysis, fault localization, and AI-assisted quality assurance for PLC software.

</details>


### [194] [ADK Arena: Evaluating Agent Development Kits via LLM-as-a-Developer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05548)
*Jintao Huang,Xiaomin Li,Gaurav Mittal,Yu Hu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出LLM-as-a-Developer方法，通过构建ADK Arena自动化评估管道，对51个Python ADK框架进行系统评测，发现框架选择显著影响性能（最佳框架解决80%任务，中位数仅32%），且生成成本差异达5.6倍，但成本与成功率不相关；信息源消融实验表明文档、源码和参数知识具有高度可替代性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Agent开发工具包（ADKs）的快速涌现已超越对框架选择如何影响智能体性能的实证理解，亟需系统性的评估方法。

Method: 采用LLM-as-a-Developer方法，用LLM编码智能体替代人类开发者，通过文档学习框架API、编写代码并迭代修复；构建ADK Arena自动化管道，包含Docker隔离、三级验证流水线和多个基准适配器（SWE-bench、τ²-bench、Terminal-Bench、MCP-Atlas），评估51个Python ADK框架。

Result: (1) 57%的运行生成成功，成本跨框架差异5.6倍（$0.6-$3.4/agent），但成本不预测成功率；(2) 无单一框架主导，最佳框架解决80%任务，中位数仅32%，甚至可击败通用前沿编码智能体；(3) 信息源消融实验中，真实框架使用率维持在28-40%窄带内，各信息源高度可替代。

Conclusion: 框架选择对智能体性能有显著影响但无最优解，文档等外部知识源并非硬瓶颈，为ADK框架的可用性和有效性提供了首个大规模实证基准。

Abstract: The rapid proliferation of Agent Development Kits (ADKs), SDK-level frameworks for building LLM-powered autonomous agents, has outpaced any empirical understanding of how framework choice affects agent performance. We propose \textbf{LLM-as-a-Developer}, a methodology that replaces human developers with an LLM coding agent that learns each framework's API from documentation, writes agent code, and iteratively repairs it through a validate-and-feedback loop until tests pass. By holding the developer constant and varying only the framework, generation effort becomes a quantitative proxy for API usability and the resulting agents provide a controlled measure of framework effectiveness. We implement this in \textbf{ADK Arena}, a fully automated pipeline with per-framework Docker isolation, a three-level validation pipeline, and benchmark adapters for SWE-bench, $τ^2$-bench, Terminal-Bench, and MCP-Atlas. Evaluating all 51 popular Python ADK frameworks (204 agent--benchmark pairs), we find that: (1)~generation succeeds for 57\% of runs, and its cost varies 5.6$\times$ across frameworks (\$0.6 to \$3.4 per agent), a quantitative proxy for API complexity, though cost alone does not predict success; (2)~no single framework dominates: the best single-benchmark ADK agents resolve up to 80\% of tasks and can even \emph{beat} general-purpose frontier coding agents at a fraction of the cost, yet the median framework resolves only 32\%; (3)~across information-source ablations, genuine framework usage stays within a narrow 28--40\% band (highest with raw source access and still 33\% with no reference material at all), indicating that documentation, source code, and parametric knowledge are largely substitutable rather than any one being a hard bottleneck.

</details>


### [195] [SmellBench: Towards Fine-Grained Evaluation of Code Agents on Refactoring Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05574)
*Fake Lin,Binbin Hu,Xi Zhu,Ziwei Zhao,Zhi Zheng,Ziqi Liu,Zhiqiang Zhang,Jun Zhou,Tong Xu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出SmellBench——一个可扩展的代码重构基准测试，通过主动向真实代码中注入代码坏味来评估代码智能体的代码可维护性。实验表明最佳组合（Qwen Code + Claude Sonnet 4.5）仅达到50.34的坏味消除分数，主要瓶颈在于缺乏跨文件理解能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准测试主要评估代码智能体的功能正确性，忽略了长期可维护性。代码智能体的滥用常导致臃肿、混乱的代码，损害可读性、可扩展性和健壮性，但缺乏专门评估代码重构能力的基准。

Method: 提出SmellBench基准，从真实仓库中筛选干净代码片段，主动注入7种常见代码坏味，生成294个覆盖3个难度级别、2种指令设置、7个真实仓库的重构案例。设计三个评估维度：功能正确性、定位能力、重构质量。

Result: 最佳组合Qwen Code + Claude Sonnet 4.5仅获得50.34的坏味消除分数。分析表明现有代理擅长处理局部代码坏味，但缺乏跨文件理解能力，难以实现全面的坏味消除。

Conclusion: 当前代码智能体在代码重构和可维护性方面仍有较大提升空间，跨文件理解的不足是主要瓶颈，SmellBench为后续研究提供了有价值的评估工具。

Abstract: Code Agents have achieved remarkable advances in recent years, exhibiting strong capabilities across a wide range of software engineering tasks. However, their misuse often produces bloated and disorganized code that impairing readability, extensibility, and robustness. Despite this risk, existing benchmarks largely evaluate functional correctness rather than long-term maintainability of code agents. In this paper, we propose SmellBench, an extensible code refactoring benchmark that proactively injects code smells into clean code snippets from real-world repositories. This design enables the generation of controlled, high-quality, and diverse refactoring cases with human-written ground truth. Specifically, it contains 294 cases spanning 7 popular smell types, 3 difficulty levels, 2 instruction settings across 7 real-world repositories. We further design 3 evaluation aspects covering functional correctness, localization ability, and refactoring quality assessment. Experiments with 2 popular agents and 6 large langauge models (LLMs) show that the best combination - Qwen Code + Claude Sonnet 4.5 - achieved only a 50.34 score of smell elimination. Further analysis reveals that this gap arises from a focus on local code smells and a lack of cross-file understanding, which hinders comprehensive smell elimination.

</details>


### [196] [The End of Software Engineering: How AI Agents Are Fundamentally Restructuring the Software Paradigm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05608)
*Zhenfeng Cao*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文论证了以LLM为核心推理引擎的AI Agent体系并非传统软件工程的渐进改进，而是对软件范式的根本性重构。作者提出"代理即服务"(AaaS)概念，将"代理工程"定位为新兴学科，并通过基准证据展示其潜力与局限，最后给出四阶段演进路线图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件工程半个多世纪以来一直基于"人类分解问题、编码逻辑、手动适配需求"的基本假设。LLM驱动的Agent系统正从根本上挑战这一假设——代码从决策逻辑的载体变成了LLM推理循环中的临时工具。作者认为需要从第一性原理重新审视这一范式转变。

Method: 采用第一性原理分析，形式化区分传统软件（代码即决策逻辑载体）与Agent系统（代码即临时工具）；追溯从许可软件→SaaS→AaaS的历史演进；基于SWE-bench Verified、EvoClaw、LangChain多Agent协调研究等基准证据进行实证分析。

Result: 揭示了Agent范式在复杂任务上的变革潜力，同时指出了当前在稳定性、可预测性、协调机制等方面的局限性。基准证据表明Agent在某些任务上已超越传统方法，但仍存在系统性瓶颈。

Conclusion: 提出四阶段向自我进化Agent生态系统演进的路线图，并为正在经历这一技术转型的从业者提供了具体建议。Agent工程作为独立于软件工程的新兴学科，其核心研究对象、控制模型和人类角色均已发生根本性变化。

Abstract: For over half a century, software engineering has operated on a foundational premise: human engineers decompose problems, encode decision logic into static code, and manually adapt that code as requirements evolve. This paper argues that the emergence of AI agents -- systems where large language models serve as the primary reasoning engine, dynamically generating and discarding code as an instrumental resource -- constitutes not an incremental improvement but a fundamental restructuring of the software paradigm. Drawing on first-principles analysis of complexity scaling, we formalize the distinction between traditional software (where code is the carrier of decision logic) and agentic systems (where code is ephemeral tooling for an LLM-driven reasoning loop). We trace the historical arc from licensed software to SaaS to what we term Agent-as-a-Service (AaaS), showing that each shift transferred additional complexity away from end-users. We introduce the concept of Agentic Engineering as an emergent discipline -- distinct from software engineering in its core object of study, control model, and human role. Through analysis of recent benchmark evidence including SWE-bench Verified, EvoClaw, and LangChain's multi-agent coordination studies, we demonstrate both the transformative potential of the agentic paradigm and its current limitations. We conclude with a four-stage roadmap toward self-evolving agent ecosystems and concrete recommendations for practitioners navigating this transition.

</details>


### [197] [Enhancing Software Engineering Through Closed-Loop Memory Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05646)
*Xuehang Guo,Zora Zhiruo Wang,Qingyun Wang,Graham Neubig,Xingyao Wang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出MemOp，一种针对软件工程(SE)智能体的闭环记忆增强框架，将记忆效用(utility)定义为经过验证的下游影响，既作为任务无关的评估基准，也作为无需标注的优化信号。实验表明，MemOp在单轮和跨轮记忆增强场景下均能持续提升SE智能体性能：成功率最高提升5.25%，解决效率提升4.63%，同时计算成本降低至少9.79%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于大语言模型的软件工程智能体在任务间无法保留、精炼和复用经验，每次都要从头构建上下文并重复类似错误。即使有记忆支持，也缺乏一种原则性的、任务无关的记忆效用(memory utility)度量，难以进行严格评估或在不同智能体和设置间泛化。

Method: 提出MemOp闭环框架，将记忆效用建立在经过验证的下游影响之上，使其同时作为任务无关的评估基准和无需标注的优化信号。在单轮(single-episode)和跨轮(cross-episode)记忆增强两种场景下进行互补评估。

Result: 成功率绝对提升最高5.25%，解决效率提升4.63%，计算成本降低至少9.79%。

Conclusion: MemOp能够持续提升SE智能体在多种设置下的性能，并有效降低计算开销。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have enabled powerful software engineering (SE) agents capable of navigating complex codebases and resolving real-world issues. However, these agents remain fundamentally episodic: they fail to retain, refine, and reuse experiences across tasks, repeatedly reconstructing context from scratch and reproducing similar mistakes. Even with memory support, they offer no remedy for the absence of a principled, task-agnostic \textit{memory utility}, making them difficult to evaluate rigorously or generalize across agents and settings. To tackle these limitations, we introduce \ours, a closed-loop framework for memory augmentation in SE agents. \ours grounds memory utility in \textit{validated downstream impact}, establishing utility as both a task-agnostic \textbf{evaluation benchmark} and an annotation-free \textbf{optimization signal}. Through complementary evaluation on \textit{single-episode} and \textit{cross-episode} memory augmentation, results demonstrate that \ours consistently improves SE agents across settings, achieving absolute gains of up to $\uparrow5.25\%$ in success rate and $\uparrow4.63\%$ in resolve efficiency, while substantially reducing computational cost by $\geq9.79\%$. Our project page: \href{https://xhguo7.github.io/MemOp/}{https://xhguo7.github.io/MemOp/}.

</details>


### [198] [When Surface Form Changes Moderation Decisions: A Paired Study of Code-Mixed Workflow Instability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05654)
*Suraj Babu Thimma Krishnaram*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究探讨了在代码混合输入（泰米尔语-英语）下，仇恨内容审核工作流中的决策不稳定性和性能下降问题。与纯英语输入相比，代码混合输入导致审核决策翻转率达0.265，审核率从0.138升至0.297，非仇恨内容的误标记率从0.069升至0.104。简单的分歧延迟规则虽能减少自动错误，但会增加审核负担。研究表明，工作流级别的评估能揭示标准分类指标无法捕捉的审核失败。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有仇恨内容审核评估多基于纯净英语的分类准确性，但实际部署的系统需要将内容路由至ALLOW、FLAG、REVIEW等不同动作。为了揭示代码混合（如泰米尔语-英语）输入对审核工作流的实际影响，研究比较了相同内容在纯净英语和代码混合形式下的审核表现差异。

Method: 采用配对评估设置，将相同底层内容分别以纯净英语和泰米尔语-英语代码混合形式表达；在纯净英语开发数据上调整阈值后，比较两种输入下的审核决策稳定性、审核率、误标记率等指标；并测试基于分歧的延迟规则作为缓解策略。

Result: 代码混合输入的审核决策翻转率达0.265；审核率从0.138升至0.297；非仇恨内容误标记率从0.069升至0.104；泰米尔语纯输入整体退化更严重；分歧延迟规则虽减少自动错误但增加审核负载。

Conclusion: 工作流级别的评估能揭示标准分类指标无法捕捉的审核失败模式；代码混合输入会导致显著的审核决策不稳定性和性能下降；简单缓解策略（如分歧延迟）存在准确率与审核负载之间的权衡。

Abstract: Hate moderation is often evaluated as classification on clean English inputs, but deployed systems must route content to actions such as ALLOW, FLAG, or REVIEW. We study how this workflow changes under code-mixed inputs using a paired evaluation setting where the same underlying content is expressed as clean English and Tamil-English code-mix. Under thresholds tuned on clean English development data, code-mixed inputs produce substantial action instability, with a paired clean- to-code-mix decision flip rate of 0.265. The main workflow effects are increased review burden and increased false-flagging of non-hateful content: review rate rises from 0.138 to 0.297 and non-hate false-flag rate rises from 0.069 to 0.104. Tamil-only inputs show stronger degradation overall, suggesting a broader language-coverage limitation rather than the same code-mixed instability pattern. A simple disagreement-based deferral rule reduces automatic errors on stressed inputs, but only by increasing review load. These results show that workflow-level evaluation reveals moderation failures that standard classification summaries can miss.

</details>


### [199] [Microskill Architecture: A Modular Skill-Driven Framework for AI-Native Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05720)
*Mohammad Zare,Omid Abdolrahmani*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出MicroSkill Architecture，一种受微服务启发的模块化设计范式，将知识封装成原子化的技能胶囊，并通过动态路由器仅选择语义相关的胶囊，从而解决AI编码代理在上下文窗口管理中的准确性、效率和成本问题。在企业内容管理系统的实证中，该架构将Token消耗降低90%以上，首次编译成功率提升近一倍，完全消除架构违规，并支持自主学习新技能胶囊。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型和AI编码代理面临上下文窗口管理的结构性挑战：注入完整项目文档和代码会导致信息丢失、Token成本激增和架构漂移，亟需一种更高效、准确的知识组织方式。

Method: 提出MicroSkill Architecture，将知识划分为原子化的、边界清晰的技能胶囊，由动态路由器依据语义相关性选择胶囊，并将上下文分配形式化为受Token预算约束的语义相关性优化问题。

Result: 在企业内容管理系统的15个复杂特性案例研究中，Token消耗降低90%以上，首次编译成功率接近翻倍，架构违规完全消除，并通过自学习机制实现7个新技能胶囊的自主提取与注册。

Conclusion: MicroSkill Architecture为构建更高效、更可靠且可随时间演化的AI原生开发系统提供了可扩展的基础架构。

Abstract: Large language models and AI coding agents have reshaped software development, but the path to fully AI-native systems faces structural challenges. Chief among them is managing context windows without losing accuracy or efficiency. When developers inject full project documentation and code into a model's memory, the model loses mid-sequence information, token costs spiral, and architecture drifts. This paper presents MicroSkill Architecture: a modular design paradigm inspired by microservices, applied to knowledge encapsulation instead of service decomposition. Instead of feeding an agent the entire codebase, the architecture partitions knowledge into atomic, sharply scoped skill capsules, and a dynamic router selects only semantically relevant capsules for the task. We formally model context allocation as constrained optimization over semantic relevance subject to a token budget. An empirical case study an enterprise content management system with fifteen complex features shows that MicroSkill cuts token consumption by over 90%, nearly doubles first-try compilation success rates, eliminates architectural violations entirely, and enables autonomous extraction and registration of seven new skill capsules via a self-learning mechanism. These findings suggest MicroSkill Architecture offers a scalable foundation for building AI-native development systems that are more efficient, more reliable, and capable of evolving over time.

</details>


### [200] [Human Oversight and Overload: Two Hidden and Costly Burdens of AI-Assisted Software Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05770)
*Vahid Garousi*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文揭示了AI辅助软件工程中两个常被忽视的负担：人类对AI生成产物持续监督审查的必要性，以及大量AI建议给工程师带来的认知过载问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI正在改变软件工程师的工作方式，但伴随而来的是隐藏的负担和成本。本文旨在揭示这些常被忽视的问题，引发业界对如何在日常AI辅助软件开发中应对这些挑战的讨论。

Method: 融合来自从业者的近期观点和证据，对两个核心负担进行定性分析和特征描述。

Result: 识别并阐述了两个关键负担：(1) 人类必须持续监督、审查、验证并时常返工AI生成的内容；(2) AI工具产生的大量建议、提示和解决方案导致工程师认知负荷持续增长。

Conclusion: 人类对AI产物的监督是不可或缺的，而AI建议的泛滥会造成开发者心理疲惫。本文开启了关于团队如何在日常AI辅助软件工程中处理这些挑战的对话。

Abstract: AI is changing how software engineers work, but it often comes with hidden burdens and costs. In this paper, we characterize two such often-overlooked burdens: (1) the constant need for human oversight and inspection of AI-generated artifacts; and (2) the growing cognitive overload on software engineers from receiving large amounts of suggestions from AI tools. The need for human oversight is not optional-engineers must review, validate, and sometimes rework what AI produces. At the same time, the flood of AI suggestions, prompts, and possible solutions can leave developers mentally stretched. By blending evidence from recent opinions from practitioners, we highlight these often-overlooked challenges and open a conversation about how teams can handle them in day-to-day AI-assisted software engineering.

</details>


### [201] [Asuka-Bench: Benchmarking Code Agents on Underspecified User Intent and Multi-Round Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05920)
*Xin Wang,Liangtai Sun,Yaoming Zhu,Shuang Zhou,Jiaxing Liu,Fengjiao Chen,Lin Qiu,Xuezhi Cao,Xunliang Cai,Licheng Zhang,Zhendong Mao*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: Asuka-Bench是一个针对多轮代码生成与反馈修复的Web开发基准测试，包含50个任务、784条评估标准和2402个预期结果。实验表明，最强模型在三轮迭代后仅完成52%的项目，且模型间存在显著性能差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有代码生成基准仅评估单次完整提示到一次性输出的映射，但真实Web开发中用户需求往往不明确，需要在看到中间结果后通过多轮反馈逐步完善。现有基准无法捕捉这种迭代式开发过程。

Method: 构建包含50个Web任务的Asuka-Bench，每个任务通过闭环流程解决：Code Agent生成Web项目，UI Agent在部署站点上执行测试用例，User LLM将评估结果转化为自然语言反馈供下一轮使用。基准测试了8个LLM在2个Agent框架上的表现。

Result: 加权任务通过率在模型间相差38个百分点，模型在从反馈中修复的能力上也存在显著差异。最强模型在三轮迭代后仅完成52%的项目，基准远未饱和。

Conclusion: Asuka-Bench有效区分了不同模型在多轮Web开发任务中的表现能力，揭示了现有模型在迭代式代码生成与反馈修复方面的局限性，为未来研究提供了有价值的评估基准。

Abstract: Existing code-generation benchmarks score a single mapping from a complete prompt to a one-shot output. However, real web development is different. Users seldom write a full spec at the start; many requirements only become clear once they look at an intermediate result and react to it. We present Asuka-Bench, a benchmark that pairs underspecified user intent with multi-round refinement, grounded in browser-rendered behavior. Each task is resolved through a closed loop: a Code Agent generates a web project, a UI Agent executes test cases on the deployed site, and a User LLM turns evaluation outcomes into natural-language feedback for the next round. The benchmark comprises 50 web tasks with 784 evaluation criteria and 2402 expected outcomes. We benchmark 8 LLMs across 2 agent frameworks. The results separate models clearly: weighted Task Pass Rate varies by 38 percentage points and models also differ substantially in their ability to repair from feedback. Asuka-Bench is also far from saturated: even the strongest model completes only 52% of projects after three rounds.

</details>


### [202] [Metamorphic Testing with the Rashomon Set: Explanation Faithfulness in Machine Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06056)
*Helge Spieker,Jørn Eirik Betten,Arnaud Gotlieb*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 针对机器学习中的Rashomon效应（多个模型表现相似但解释不同），提出基于蜕变测试的框架，通过后验解释方法的特征重要性来评估解释忠实度，无需真实标签。在表格回归数据集上用SHAP和LIME验证了该方法，为选择可靠模型和解释提供实用工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习中存在Rashomon效应：多个性能相近的模型却给出截然不同的特征解释，这引发了"哪些解释可信"的根本问题。现有评估解释方法通常需要真实标签或人工标注，缺乏无监督条件下的忠实度评估手段。

Method: 提出基于蜕变测试的框架，定义五种蜕变关系来形式化模型行为与特征归因之间的一致性属性。该框架无需真实标签，通过后验解释方法（SHAP、LIME）探索特征重要性，以评估解释的忠实度。在两个表格回归数据集上进行实验验证。

Result: 成功在表格回归数据集上应用该框架，使用SHAP和LIME两种解释器验证了方法的可行性。框架能够评估不同解释方法的忠实度，为选择准确模型及其可信解释提供依据。

Conclusion: 该框架是一种实用的、模型无关的工具，能够在缺乏真实标签的情况下评估解释忠实度，帮助选择既准确又具有可靠、可信解释的模型。

Abstract: Multiple machine learning models can achieve near-equivalent predictive performance on the same task, yet provide divergent feature-based explanations. This is called the Rashomon effect of (explainable) machine learning, and it raises the question of which explanations, if any, are trustworthy. We propose a framework based on metamorphic testing that assesses explanation faithfulness without requiring ground-truth labels by exploring attributed feature importance from post-hoc explanation methods. Five metamorphic relations formalize expected consistency properties between model behavior and feature attributions. We apply this general framework to two tabular regression datasets and two post-hoc explainers (SHAP and LIME) to demonstrate the approach. The framework offers a practical, model-agnostic tool for selecting accurate models with reliable and trustworthy explanations.

</details>


### [203] [TLA-Prover: Verifiable TLA+ Specification Synthesis via Preference-Optimized Low-Rank Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06133)
*Eric Spencer,Arslan Bisharat,Brian Ortiz,Khushboo Bhadauria,TaiNing Wang,George K. Thiruvathukal,Konstantin Laufer,Mohammed Abuhamad*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出TLA-Prover，一个200亿参数的TLA+规范合成模型。通过监督微调与基于修复的GRPO训练，在30问题基准上达到30%的Gold/Diamond通过率，较未调优基线(8.6%)提升约3.5倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型生成的TLA+规范经常无法通过TLC模型检查器（语义检查），现有最佳公共基线仅8.6%的语义通过率，需要专门模型提升TLA+规范合成的可靠性。

Method: 使用200亿参数模型，两阶段训练：1) 监督微调（SFT）于已验证示例；2) 基于修复的组相对策略优化（GRPO），模型学习修正自身被拒绝的规范。设置四等级评估体系（Bronze/Silver/Gold/Diamond），Diamond要求正确性属性被微小修改后TLC能检测到违反，避免平凡属性。同时训练DPO变体作为消融实验，TLC直接提供奖励信号，无需学习奖励模型。

Result: 在30问题保留基准上，TLA-Prover在Gold和Diamond级别均达到30%(pass@1)，约为未调优基线8.6%的3.5倍。DPO变体在Diamond级别达到20%。Gold和Diamond在每个检查点结果一致，消除了平凡属性失效模式。

Conclusion: 基于修复的GRPO训练能显著提升LLM生成TLA+规范的质量，Diamond等级设计有效防止模型生成平凡属性，为形式化规范合成提供了有效的训练范式。

Abstract: TLA+ is a formal specification language for verifying distributed systems and safety-critical protocols. Large language models (LLMs) frequently produce TLA+ specifications that fail the TLC model checker for semantic reasons. Across 25 LLMs, the best public baseline is 26.6% syntactic parse and 8.6% semantic model-check. We present TLA-Prover, a 20-billion-parameter model for TLA+ specification synthesis. Training combines supervised fine-tuning (SFT) on verified examples with repair-based group-relative policy optimization (GRPO). In the GRPO stage, the model learns to fix its own rejected specifications. We also train a direct preference optimization (DPO) variant from the same SFT checkpoint as an ablation. TLC provides the reward signal directly, with no learned reward model. Four tiers grade each output: Bronze (parses), Silver (no warnings), Gold (passes TLC), and Diamond. To reach Diamond, the model's correctness property is automatically altered in a small way; TLC must then detect a violation. If TLC still passes, the property was always-true and contributes nothing; the output fails Diamond. TLA-Prover reaches 9/30 (i.e. pass@1 = 30%) at both Gold and Diamond on a held-out 30-problem benchmark. This is roughly 3.5x the 8.6% untuned baseline. The DPO variant reaches 20% at Diamond. Gold and Diamond coincide at every checkpoint; this prevents the trivial-property failure mode.

</details>


### [204] [Towards the Readability of LLM-Generated Codes through Multitask Representation Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06214)
*Huifan Gao,Liuhua He,Yinghui Pan,Shenbao Yu,Yifeng Zeng,Shengchao Qin,Weidi Sun*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出多任务表示工程（RepE）框架，在保持代码正确性的同时提升LLM生成代码的可读性，通过低数据依赖和低计算成本的方式实现跨任务的可读性控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究主要关注提升LLM生成代码的正确性，而代码可读性作为代码质量的关键维度之一却未得到充分关注。可读性提升面临主观性强、难以通过定向控制实现的挑战。

Method: 采用表示工程（RepE）作为定向控制方法，因其具有低数据依赖和低计算成本的特点；在此基础上提出多任务RepE框架，以解决代码可读性提升需要跨任务控制的问题，并从理论上讨论了多任务引导方法对代码可读性与正确性之间权衡的影响。

Result: 通过综合实验验证了多任务RepE框架在提升代码可读性同时保持正确性的有效性，所有实现均已开源。

Conclusion: 多任务表示工程能够有效平衡代码可读性与正确性之间的权衡，为LLM生成代码的质量提升提供了可行的控制方法。

Abstract: Correctness and readability are key measures of code quality, respectively ensuring functional fidelity and ease of comprehension. While most existing research focuses on improving the correctness of large language models~(LLMs) generated codes, readability remains under-addressed. Enhancing readability through targeted control is challenging due to its subjective nature. In this article, we employ representation engineering~(RepE) as the targeted control method given its characteristics of low data dependency and low computational cost. Prior work on RepE has primarily focused on the targeted control for a single task, but improving the code readability requires the control across multiple tasks. Accordingly we proposes the multitask RepE framework and theoretically discuss the impact of the multitask steering method on the tradeoff between the code readability and correctness. We further provide comprehensive experiments in support. All the relevant implementations are open-source and available upon request.

</details>


### [205] [Domain Diversity, Motivation, Inclusion, and Feedback in Software Modelling Education](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06275)
*Isabella Graßl,Christopher Lazik,Shalini Chakraborty,Grischa Liebel,Miguel Goulão*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究通过90名学生和22名教育工作者的平行调查，探讨了问题领域和教学方法如何影响软件建模教育中的学生动机、参与度、包容性和反馈。研究发现教育者假设与学生偏好之间存在脱节：学生对社交相关领域动机最强并偏好选择权，而教育者高估了学习相关领域的兴趣；微小的设计选择可能排斥学生；学生认为反馈在有明显行动时才有意义。建议采用以学生为中心的领域选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管学生参与度对软件建模学习至关重要，但现有研究多关注建模工具、符号和评估，忽视了问题领域选择及其带来的多样性、相关性和文化视角对学生学习体验的影响。本研究旨在填补这一空白，探索问题领域和教学方法如何塑造建模教育中的动机、参与度、包容性和反馈。

Method: 采用平行调查法，对90名学生和22名教育工作者进行问卷调查，收集双方对问题领域选择、教学方法、包容性和反馈认知的数据，并对比分析教育者假设与学生实际偏好之间的差异。

Result: 发现教育者假设与学生偏好之间存在显著脱节：学生对社会相关领域表现出最强动机且偏好自主选择，而教育者高估了学习相关领域的吸引力；微小的设计选择可能无意中排斥学生；学生认为反馈仅在被明显采纳和回应时才具有意义。

Conclusion: 包容性的领域选择是激发学生动机的核心因素，因此建议采用以学生为中心的领域选择策略，以提高软件建模教育的参与度、动机和包容性。

Abstract: Student engagement is critical for effective learning in software modelling, yet fostering motivation and inclusivity remains a challenge. While existing research has focused on modelling tools, notations, and assessment, little attention has been given to how the choice of problem domains and the diversity, relatability, and cultural perspectives they bring shape students' learning experiences. This study explores how problem domains and teaching methods influence motivation, engagement, inclusiveness, and feedback in modelling education. To investigate these dimensions, we conducted parallel surveys with 90 students and 22 educators. Our findings reveal disconnects between educator assumptions and student preferences: Students show greatest motivation for socially relevant domains and prefer choice in selection, while educators overestimate interest in study-related domains. The study identifies how minor design choices can exclude students. Students perceive feedback as meaningful when visibly acted upon. These findings suggest inclusive domain selection is central to student motivation; thus, we recommend student-centred domain selection.

</details>


### [206] [More than a Judge: An Empirical Study of Agent-Human Interaction in Crowdsourced Testing Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06301)
*Yue Wang,Yuan Zhao,Shengcheng Yu,Zhenyu Chen,Qing Gu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文通过一项包含20名测试人员的四阶段对照实验，研究了基于LLM的多智能体评估系统生成的反馈能否帮助众包测试人员改进报告质量、提升后续任务表现并实现跨应用迁移。结果表明，智能体反馈能带来即时改进、更好的首次提交以及部分但有意义的迁移效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管已有工作证明基于LLM的多智能体评估框架能有效对齐人类共识并减少评估工作量，但自动化评判本身并不能说明智能体输出嵌入工作流后能否真正改善人类工作。本研究旨在填补这一空白，探究在众包测试场景中，评估驱动的可操作反馈能否提升测试人员的报告修订能力、后续任务表现以及跨应用实践迁移。

Method: 采用四阶段受控人因实验，招募20名测试人员在三个真实应用上完成测试任务。实验中，智能体从文本性(Textuality)、充分性(Adequacy)、竞争力(Competitiveness)三个维度生成反馈，通过对比有无反馈组的表现，评估反馈对报告修订质量、新任务首次提交质量以及跨应用迁移效果的影响。此外，对17名参与者进行任务后问卷调查以补充质性证据。

Result: 智能体生成的反馈支持报告修订的即时改进；在先前接触过反馈后，测试人员在新任务上的首次提交质量更好；部分但有意义的能力迁移到了后续应用。问卷结果表明，反馈总体可理解、被用于修订并延续到后续任务中，但也揭示了在具体性和执行细节方面仍存在摩擦。

Conclusion: 在众包测试这一特定场景中，评估智能体不仅可以作为事后评判者，还可以作为嵌入工作流的反馈提供者，支持上游报告质量改进，为智能体从"评估工具"向"工作流辅助"角色拓展提供了实证依据。

Abstract: Agentic AI is increasingly being integrated into software engineering workflows. In crowdsourced testing, however, the large volume and uneven quality of submitted reports still create a substantial review burden for developers. In prior work, we developed and validated a multi-agent assessment backbone based on the LLM-as-a-Judge paradigm. That backbone assesses reports along three dimensions--textuality, adequacy, and competitiveness--and was shown to align well with human consensus while substantially reducing assessment effort. Yet reliable automated judging does not by itself show whether agent outputs can improve human work when embedded into workflow. This paper studies that missing question in the context of crowdsourced testing. We investigate whether assessment-derived, actionable feedback can improve how testers revise reports, perform on later tasks, and transfer reporting practices across applications. To do so, we conducted a controlled four-stage human-subject study with 20 testers across three real-world applications. The results show that agent-generated feedback supports immediate improvements in revised reports, better first submissions on a new task after prior feedback exposure, and evidence of partial but meaningful transfer to a later application. A post-task questionnaire completed by 17 participants complements these artifact-based findings by suggesting that the feedback was generally understandable, acted upon in revision, and carried into later tasks, while also revealing remaining friction in specificity and execution. Overall, the study provides empirical evidence that, in the studied crowdsourced testing setting, assessment agents can serve not only as post-hoc judges but also as workflow-integrated feedback providers that support upstream report-quality improvement.

</details>


### [207] [From Failed Trajectories to Reliable LLM Agents: Diagnosing and Repairing Harness Flaws](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06324)
*Mengzhuo Chen,Junjie Wang,Zhe Liu,Yawen Wang,Qing Wang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出HarnessFix，一种基于轨迹引导的框架，用于诊断LLM智能体故障并修复智能体框架（harness）。它通过将执行轨迹和框架代码编译为HTIR中间表示，定位故障根源与责任层，生成针对性修复补丁。在SWE-Bench Verified等基准上，HarnessFix将初始框架的性能提升15.2%–50.0%，优于人工设计和自进化基线，并揭示了跨ETCLOVG层的常见框架缺陷模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自改进智能体和自动框架进化方法主要通过运行时监督、提示优化、工作流搜索或基于最终结果的框架修改来提升智能体，但往往无法诊断失败轨迹中责任证据所在位置以及哪个框架层导致了不可靠行为，导致修改范围宽泛、间接或定位不准。

Method: HarnessFix包含三个核心步骤：1) 将原始执行轨迹和框架代码编译为Harness-aware Trace Intermediate Representation (HTIR)，归一化碎片化轨迹证据并捕获步骤级来源与控制流关系；2) 将故障归因到责任轨迹步骤和框架层，将重复诊断合并为可操作缺陷记录，并映射到限定范围的修复算子；3) 在缺陷特定的修复规范下生成并验证框架补丁，以减少目标缺陷且不引入不可接受的回归。

Result: 在SWE-Bench Verified、Terminal-Bench 2.0 Verified、GAIA和AppWorld四个基准上，HarnessFix将初始框架的测试性能提升15.2%–50.0%，在各项指标上均优于人工设计的基线和自进化基线方法。

Conclusion: HarnessFix通过细粒度的轨迹归因和框架层诊断，实现了比现有方法更精准、更高效的框架自动修复，并在多个复杂基准上验证了其有效性，同时揭示了ETCLOVG各层中常见的框架缺陷模式。

Abstract: LLM-based agents increasingly rely on harnesses that provide execution environments, tool interfaces, context, lifecycle orchestration, observability, verification, and governance. Existing self-improving agents and automatic harness evolution methods mainly improve agents through runtime supervision, prompt optimization, workflow search, or harness modification based on final outcomes. However, they often fail to diagnose where the responsible evidence lies in failed trajectories and which harness layer causes the unreliable behavior, resulting in broad, indirect, or poorly scoped changes. This paper proposes HarnessFix, a trace-guided framework for diagnosing agent failures and repairing agent harnesses. HarnessFix compiles raw execution traces and harness code into a Harness-aware Trace Intermediate Representation (HTIR), which normalizes fragmented trajectory evidence and captures step-level provenance and control-flow relations. It then attributes failures to responsible trajectory steps and harness layers, consolidates recurring diagnoses into actionable flaw records, and maps them to scoped repair operators. Finally, HarnessFix generates and validates harness patches under flaw-specific repair specifications to reduce target flaws without introducing unacceptable regressions. We evaluate HarnessFix on SWE-Bench Verified, Terminal-Bench 2.0 Verified, GAIA and AppWorld. Across these benchmarks, HarnessFix improves held-out test performance over the initial harnesses by 15.2%--50.0%, outperforms human-designed and self-evolution baselines, and reveals recurring harness-flaw patterns across ETCLOVG layers.

</details>


### [208] [Scaffold, Not Vocabulary? A Controlled, Two-Tier, Pre-Registered Study of a Popperian Code-Generation Skill](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06454)
*Mehmet Iscan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文通过精心设计的消融实验（包括长度匹配安慰剂、仅标签支架、词汇光环哨兵等控制条件），测试了提示词技巧中"波普尔证伪主义"内容对代码生成的真正影响。在Claude Sonnet 4.6上所有条件均接近天花板无法区分，在Qwen2.5-Coder-0.5B上结构化支架带来20-22个百分点的提升，但完整的波普尔技能与仅保留标题的标签支架无显著差异（34.8% vs 34.8%），安慰剂仅落后2.4个百分点。结论是该技能的程序性证伪内容并未带来超越支架结构的可分离执行正确性收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型越来越多地用于编写、审查和评判代码，且实践中常给模型配备"科学家式推理"的提示技巧（如波普尔证伪主义），但这些增益的评估几乎全部依赖LLM作为评委（存在位置偏好、自我偏好、风格偏好等偏误）。本文旨在剥离验证：增益究竟来自技巧的波普尔内容，还是任何支架结构本身带来的。

Method: 预注册两层消融实验，设置三个控制条件：长度匹配安慰剂、仅保留波普尔标题但剥离程序的标签支架、执行预言（HumanEval+单元测试）；附加词汇光环哨兵和同模型自评审计。在Claude Sonnet 4.6（N=163）和Qwen2.5-Coder-0.5B（N=164）两个模型上测试。

Result: 前沿模型上所有条件接近天花板无法分离（天花板限制导致未检出预注册的+5%改进）；小模型上结构化支架提升最优8选1正确率20-22个百分点，但完整技能与标签支架无显著差异（F@8=L@8 vs V@8=34.8%），安慰剂仅落后2.4个百分点；0.5B自评模型使用波普尔准则不优于随机选择，且60%选择集中在一个索引。

Conclusion: 在测试的两个设置中，波普尔程序性内容未提供超越标签支架的可分离执行正确性收益，增益追踪的是支架结构。本文贡献了一个校准的负结果和可复用的消歧协议，该发现限定了某类提示技巧的工程声称范围，而非对波普尔方法论本身的评价。

Abstract: Large language models increasingly write, review, and judge code, and a fast-growing practice equips them with prompt 'skills' that ask the model to reason like a scientist. A prominent example tells the model to act as a Popperian falsificationist, and such skills are reported to improve generated code. But these gains are almost always read off an LLM-as-a-judge, an instrument with documented positional, self-preference, and stylistic biases. We ask: if it appears to help, is the gain from the skill's Popperian content, or from the structure any scaffold imposes? We pre-register a two-tier ablation with three controls: a length-matched placebo, a labels-only scaffold that keeps the Popperian headers but strips the procedure, and an execution oracle (HumanEval+ unit tests), plus a vocabulary-halo sentinel and a same-model self-judge audit. On a frontier model (Claude Sonnet 4.6, N=163) all conditions sit near the benchmark ceiling and do not separate, so the pre-registered +5-point improvement is not supported (a ceiling-limited non-detection). On a small model (Qwen2.5-Coder-0.5B, N=164) structured arms lift best-of-eight correctness by 20-22 points, but the full skill shows no separable benefit over a labels-only scaffold (aggregate F@8=L@8 vs V@8=34.8%), and the placebo trails by only 2.4 points. A 0.5B self-judge applying the Popperian rubric does not beat random selection and concentrates 60% of its picks on one index. In the two settings tested, the skill's Popperian procedural content adds no separable execution-correctness benefit beyond a labels-only scaffold, so the gains track scaffold structure. We contribute a calibrated negative result and a reusable disambiguation protocol; the finding bounds an engineering claim about one prompt-skill family and is not an evaluation of Popperian methodology in general.

</details>


### [209] [Code2LoRA: Hypernetwork-Generated Adapters for Code Language Models under Software Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06492)
*Liliana Hotsko,Yinxi Li,Yuntian Deng,Pengyu Nie*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: Code2LoRA 是一个超网络框架，通过为每个代码仓库生成专属的 LoRA 适配器，在零推理令牌开销下注入仓库级知识。支持静态仓库（Code2LoRA-Static）和演进代码库（Code2LoRA-Evo）两种场景，在 RepoPeftBench 基准上达到甚至超越了逐仓库 LoRA 微调的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有代码语言模型需要仓库级上下文（如导入、API、项目约定），但已有的方法（RAG、依赖分析、逐仓库微调/LoRA）存在推理开销大、仓库规模下成本高、面对演进代码库时脆弱的缺陷。

Method: 提出 Code2LoRA 超网络框架：Code2LoRA-Static 将单个仓库快照转换为 LoRA 适配器；Code2LoRA-Evo 使用 GRU 隐藏状态按代码差异更新适配器。同时构建了 RepoPeftBench 基准（604个 Python 仓库，含静态和演进两个赛道）。

Result: 静态赛道：Code2LoRA-Static 跨仓库精确匹配 63.8%，仓库内 66.2%，达到逐仓库 LoRA 上界；演进赛道：Code2LoRA-Evo 跨仓库精确匹配 60.3%，比单一共享 LoRA 高 5.2 个百分点。

Conclusion: Code2LoRA 能够在不增加推理令牌开销的前提下有效注入仓库级知识，且能适应代码库的持续演进，是一种高效、可扩展的仓库级代码理解方法。

Abstract: Code language models need repository-level context to resolve imports, APIs, and project conventions. Existing methods inject this knowledge as long inputs (retrieved through RAG or dependency analysis) or through per-repository fine-tuning and LoRA -- costly at repository scale and brittle to evolving codebases. We introduce Code2LoRA, a hypernetwork framework that generates repository-specific LoRA adapters, effectively injecting repository knowledge with zero inference-time token overhead. Code2LoRA supports two usage scenarios: Code2LoRA-Static converts a single repository snapshot into an adapter, suitable for comprehension of stable codebases; while Code2LoRA-Evo maintains an adapter backed by a GRU hidden state updated per code diff, suitable for active development of evolving codebases. To evaluate Code2LoRA against parameter-efficient fine-tuning baselines, we build RepoPeftBench, a benchmark of 604 Python repositories with two tracks: a static track with 40K training and 12K test assertion-completion tasks, and an evolution track with 215K commit-derived training and 87K commit-derived test tasks. On the static track, Code2LoRA-Static achieves 63.8% cross-repo and 66.2% in-repo exact match, matching the per-repository LoRA upper bound; on the evolution track, Code2LoRA-Evo achieves 60.3% cross-repo exact match (+5.2 pp over a single shared LoRA). Code2LoRA's code can be found at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/code2lora-6857; the model checkpoints and RepoPeftBench datasets can be found at https://huggingface.co/code2lora.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [210] [OLIVE: Online Low-Rank Incremental Learning for Efficient Adaptive Exoskeletons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05234)
*Dong Liu,Yanxuan Yu,Ben Lengerich,Tony Geng,Ying Nian Wu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: OLIVE是一种参数高效的在线自适应框架，通过低秩残差分解和奖励驱动策略梯度，在部署过程中持续个性化外骨骼控制，在多种地形上实现了步态平滑度、努力减少和运动稳定性分别提升13、22和15个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可穿戴外骨骼控制器依赖静态步态策略，无法适应动态真实环境或个体用户特征。

Method: 提出OLIVE框架：(1) 将自适应控制策略分解为低秩残差形式 ΔW = AB^T，在线更新成本从O(dk)降至O(r(d+k))；(2) 基于EMG、IMU、振动等体感反馈的奖励形状策略梯度更新参数，无需离线参考轨迹；(3) 门控机制根据上下文状态调制个性化强度；(4) 动态秩调度器根据地形复杂度调整更新维度。

Result: 在可穿戴平台上，OLIVE在步态平滑度、努力减少和运动稳定性上分别比最强基线提升13、22和15个百分点，约1800步内收敛，端到端延迟7.4ms。

Conclusion: OLIVE通过在线低秩学习实现了外骨骼控制的高效个性化自适应，在多种动态场景中表现优于现有方法。

Abstract: Wearable exoskeleton systems hold promise for restoring mobility in individuals with physical impairments, yet most existing controllers rely on static gait policies that lack the ability to adapt to dynamic real-world environments or individual user characteristics. We present \olive (\underline{O}nline \underline{L}ow-rank \underline{I}ncremental Learning for Efficient Adapti\underline{ve} Exoskeletons), a parameter-efficient online adaptation framework that continuously personalizes exoskeleton control during deployment. \olive decomposes the adaptive component of the control policy into a low-rank residual form~$\dW = \At\Bt^\top$ with rank~$r!\ll!\min(d,k)$, reducing online update cost from $\mathcal{O}(dk)$ to $\mathcal{O}(r(d{+}k))$ while preserving the stability of a pretrained base controller~$\Wz$. Parameters are updated via a reward-shaped policy gradient driven purely by on-body sensor feedback (EMG, IMU, vibration), eliminating dependence on offline reference trajectories. A gating mechanism modulates the strength of personalization based on contextual state, and a dynamic rank scheduler adapts the update dimensionality to terrain complexity -- allocating minimal capacity on simple flat terrain and expanding to higher-rank updates on demanding uneven surfaces -- enabling robust performance across diverse activities: flat walking, stair navigation, slopes, and uneven terrain. Experiments on the wearable platform demonstrate that \olive achieves +13, +22, and +15 percentage-point improvements in gait smoothness, effort reduction, and motion stability over the strongest baseline, converging within $\sim$1{,}800 walking steps at 7.4,ms end-to-end latency. Our code implementation is available at https://github.com/FastLM/OLIVE.

</details>


### [211] [A New Quaternion-Joint Cable-Driven Redundant Manipulator Configuration and its Control Through FABRIK and Residual Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05236)
*Tanapath Pornthisan,Thanapat Kemthong,Thanyapisit Kangsathien,Pasut Aranchaiya,Paulo Garcia,Viboon Sangveraphunsiri*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种4段8关节的线驱动冗余机械臂，采用四元数关节设计，在更少硬件成本下实现更广工作空间，并引入残差强化学习进行控制，其精度比FABRIK算法高出三个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 线驱动冗余机械臂在高度障碍环境中具有广泛应用，但四元数关节的复杂性使得构型先验决策困难、控制系统计算负载高，且非线性放大了制造误差与实际工件间的偏差，亟需更优的构型与控制方案。

Method: 设计了一种4段8关节的四元数关节机械臂构型；采用残差强化学习（Residual Reinforcement Learning）进行控制，并与FABRIK算法进行对比；详细描述了FABRIK控制流程及对应的学习实现。

Result: 该4段8关节构型比现有构型工作空间更广且硬件成本更低；残差强化学习在位置精度和姿态精度上均比FABRIK算法提升三个数量级，实现了对该新型机械臂的精确控制。

Conclusion: 该构型在工作空间效率上优于以往设计，控制实现更为简洁，所提方法可为该类机械臂的新系统设计提供有效工具。

Abstract: Robotic arms capable of traversing arbitrary spatial paths, especially in highly obstructed workspaces, are highly desired across several industries. Quaternion-joints have recently empowered a specific class of robotic arms -- cable-driven redundant manipulators -- beyond its prior capabilities. Specifically, quaternion-joints reduce the number of required motors per degree of freedom, paving the way for more compact solutions.An ongoing challenge is that the complexity of the kinematic model of quaternion joints challenges a priori decisions on manipulator configurations and imposes higher computational demands on the control system and its non-linearities amplify all discrepancies between design and physical artifact arising from fabrication imprecision. Here we show a that a 4-segment, 8-joint manipulator can achieve a broader workspace than extant configurations, at lower hardware cost, and that Residual Reinforcement Learning outperforms extant state-of-the-art methods -- specifically, the FABRIK algorithm -- on the control of such manipulator. Our results show that this configuration is more workspace-effective than prior designs, and that Residual Reinforcement Learning outperforms FABRIK by three orders of magnitude on positional and orientational accuracy, effecting precise control of the novel 4-segment, 8-joint manipulator. Additionally, the control implementation is simpler: we describe the complete FABRIK process for control and corresponding learning implementation. Our methodology is applicable to the design of new systems, providing designers with further tools for the development of this class of manipulators and corresponding control systems for novel configurations.

</details>


### [212] [Dynamic Multi-Agent Pickup and Delivery in Robotic Cellular Warehousing Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05669)
*Cheng Ren,Ming Li,Xinping Guan,George Q. Huang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文首次针对机器人仓储系统中订单内部动态演化的场景，提出了两种基于token传递的在线重规划算法（动态Token传递和协作Token传递），有效降低了订单流转时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实仓库运营中订单会动态演化（新SKU不断追加到正在执行的订单中），而传统MAPD问题假设任务是静态的，无法处理这种动态性，因此需要新的算法来应对内部订单演化带来的挑战。

Method: 基于token传递范式，提出两种事件触发式在线重规划算法：1）动态Token传递：通过添加订单分解和优先级Token调度进行局部重规划，保持无碰撞执行；2）协作Token传递：允许空闲机器人主动协助新添加的拣选任务，提升系统效率。

Result: 在机器人仓储系统环境中的仿真结果表明，所提方法相比静态和非协作基线方法显著降低了订单流转时间。

Conclusion: 所提出的动态Token传递和协作Token传递算法有效解决了订单内部动态演化场景下的多机器人拣选与配送问题，为实际仓储运营提供了可行的在线重规划方案。

Abstract: Robotic Cellular Warehousing Systems (RCWS) give rise to multi-agent pickup and delivery (MAPD) processes in which robots sequentially collect multiple stock-keeping units (SKUs) for each order. Unlike classical MAPD formulations that assume static tasks, real warehouse operations often involve dynamic order evolution, where new SKUs may be appended to an order while it is being executed. Motivated by this practical requirement, this letter formulates the Dynamic Multi-Agent Pickup and Delivery problem considering internal order evolution for the first time. Building on the token passing paradigm, we propose two event-triggered online replanning algorithms. The first, Dynamic Token Passing, performs localized replanning upon order updates through add-order decomposition and priority-based token scheduling while preserving collision-free execution. The second, Cooperative Token Passing, further enables idle robots to opportunistically assist newly added pickups, improving system-level efficiency. Simulation results in RCWS environments demonstrate that the proposed methods significantly reduce order flowtime compared with static and non-cooperative baselines.

</details>


### [213] [Inverse Manipulation through Symbolic Planning and Residual Operator Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05248)
*Yigit Yildirim,Giuseppe Rauso,Riccardo Caccavale,Alberto Finzi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种混合框架，通过从演示中自动提取STRIPS算子并构建逆操作目标，结合符号任务规划与强化学习残差策略，实现机器人操作任务的逆操作。在ManiSkill3 PushCube任务上验证了该方法能够将近似符号逆操作转化为物理可行的逆技能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人操作任务的逆操作不能仅通过反转符号状态转移或回放运动轨迹实现，需要一种结合符号推理与连续动力学的方法。

Method: 从演示中通过软几何谓词自动提取STRIPS-like算子；为每个算子构建逆恢复目标（保留前提、恢复删除效果、否定添加效果）；任务规划器尝试用可用动作原语满足目标；未解决的符号谓词通过强化学习（SAC）学习残差策略进行补偿。

Result: 在ManiSkill3 PushCube任务中，符号逆操作能实现粗粒度的拾取-放置恢复，残差SAC策略进一步细化立方体姿态以满足剩余逆谓词。

Conclusion: 谓词导出的残差控制能够将近似符号逆操作转化为物理上可行的逆技能，验证了混合框架的有效性。

Abstract: Inverting a robotic task requires more than reversing symbolic state transitions or rewinding motor trajectories. In robot manipulation tasks, symbolic inverse plans often fail to fully restore the effects of forward executions under continuous interaction dynamics. We present a hybrid framework for inverse manipulation that derives inverse-skill objectives from STRIPS-like operators automatically extracted from demonstrations through soft geometric predicates. For each extracted operator, we construct an inverse restoration objective that preserves preconditions, restores delete effects, and negates add effects. A task planner first attempts to satisfy this objective using available action primitives. Unresolved symbolic predicates then induce a residual operator learning problem solved through Reinforcement Learning (RL). We evaluate the framework on the ManiSkill3 PushCube task. For a forward pushing skill, the symbolic inverse performs a coarse pick-and-place restoration, while a residual Soft Actor-Critic policy refines the cube pose to satisfy the remaining inverse predicates. Our results show that predicate-derived residual control can turn an approximate symbolic inverse into a physically grounded inverse skill.

</details>


### [214] [Accelerating and Scaling MPC-Guided Reinforcement Learning for Humanoid Locomotion and Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05687)
*Junheng Li,Liang Wu,Sergio A. Esteban,Lizhi Yang,Ján Drgoňa,Aaron D. Ames*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出MPC-RL框架，通过在训练时利用质心动力学MPC轨迹指导强化学习，并开发了并行批处理GPU求解器π^nMPC，实现了高效的人形机器人运动与操作控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有将MPC用于RL的方法要么需要耗时的优化问题构建，要么训练开销过大，难以在实际中证明其合理性。

Method: 提出质心动力学MPC奖励公式，在训练时利用MPC轨迹指导RL；开发π^nMPC求解器，支持并行批处理GPU计算、水平方向并行、免问题构建，直接处理时变动力学以避免高内存占用和预编译。

Result: 在运动控制和操作技能上取得了优于对比方法的性能，并通过硬件实验验证。

Conclusion: MPC-RL框架能高效地将MPC指导融入RL训练，显著提升人形机器人运动与操作能力，且代码已开源。

Abstract: In humanoid motion control, model predictive control (MPC) offers physically grounded prediction and constraint handling, while reinforcement learning (RL) enables robust whole-body skills through large-scale simulation. However, using MPC inside RL often requires time-consuming problem construction or excessive training overhead, making such frameworks difficult to justify in practice. This work studies efficient training-time MPC guidance for humanoid locomotion and manipulation, termed MPC-RL. We introduce a centroidal-dynamics MPC reward formulation that leverages guidance from MPC trajectories in training time. To make this practical in massively parallel RL, we develop $π^n$MPC, a parallel-in-horizon and construction-free batched GPU MPC solver that operates directly on time-varying dynamics to avoid high memory usage and pre-compilation. Through a variety of comparative studies and hardware validations, we have found that MPC-RL achieves superior performance in locomotion and manipulation skills. The code base is available at https://github.com/junhengl/mpc-rl.

</details>


### [215] [Sample-efficient Low-level Motion Planning for Robotic Manipulation Tasks via Zero-shot Transfer Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06041)
*Yuanzhi He,Victor Romero-Cano,José J. Patiño,Juan David Hernández,William Sawtell,Gualtiero Colombo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出iCEM+TL框架，通过迁移学习将iCEM参数从简单任务迁移到复杂任务，并结合奖励重塑进行任务分解，在仿真和真实机器人堆叠任务中实现了高达23%的成功率提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着机器人系统复杂度的增加，运动规划模型的计算负担和训练时间显著增长，现有iCEM算法在复杂场景（堆叠、滑动、货架放置）中性能受限。

Method: 提出iCEM+TL框架：1) 利用迁移学习将简单上游任务的iCEM关键参数迁移至复杂下游任务；2) 通过任务分解应用奖励重塑优化堆叠和货架放置任务的性能。

Result: 仿真结果显示成功率提升高达23%，并在真实Franka Emika机器人堆叠任务中验证了实际部署的可行性。

Conclusion: iCEM+TL框架有效结合迁移学习和奖励重塑，显著提升了复杂机器人控制任务中iCEM的性能，并具备实际部署能力。

Abstract: As robotic systems become more sophisticated, the growing complexity of their motion planning models and the longer training times pose substantial challenges. Evolutionary algorithms such as the Sample-efficient Cross-Entropy Method (iCEM) have recently demonstrated promising potential for low-level real-time planning by leveraging efficient knowledge reuse strategies to improve performance. Although effective in many control tasks, iCEM's performance can be constrained in more complex scenarios, particularly those requiring stacking, sliding, and shelf placement. In this work, we propose a novel iCEM+TL framework that explicitly leverages Transfer Learning (TL), where key iCEM parameters are transferred from simpler upstream tasks to guide more complex downstream tasks. Additionally, we applied Reward Redesign (RR) through task decomposition for stacking objects and shelf placement to optimize task-specific performance. Results from the simulation show that our framework achieves success rate improvements of up to 23%. The framework is further validated on a real Franka Emika robot in a stacking task, demonstrating its practical feasibility for real-world deployment.

</details>


### [216] [Efficient Computation of Distance Functions for Navigation Vector Fields in Lie Groups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05372)
*Vinicius M. Gonçalves,João Baião,Felipe Bartelt,Douglas G. Macharet,Gustavo M. Freitas,Héctor Azpúrua,Luciano C. A. Pimenta*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种高效计算机器人配置点与李群上G-多项式曲线之间距离的方法，通过将问题简化为多项式求根，显著降低了计算时间，并已在机械臂上实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于向量场的机器人控制方法需要频繁计算机器人配置与曲线间的距离及最近点，尤其在李群扩展中，该计算在嵌入式平台高控制频率下开销巨大，现有优化方法效率不足。

Method: 利用G-多项式曲线的结构特性，将距离计算问题转化为少量多项式求根问题，从而避免昂贵的迭代优化计算。

Result: 仿真结果表明，相比现有优化方法，所提方法在保持精度的同时显著降低了计算时间。在SE(3)群上给出了实用公式，并在机械臂上进行了实验验证。

Conclusion: 所提方法能高效支持李群上的路径跟踪控制，且已实现为开源计算包，便于实际应用。

Abstract: Vector-field-based methods are widely used for robot control and are often applied to the path-tracking problem. Some vector field approaches require repeatedly computing the distance between the robot configuration and the curve, as well as the corresponding closest point. Recently, vector fields have been extended to Lie Groups. In this case, this computation can be expensive, especially when performed at high control frequencies on embedded platforms. This paper proposes a method for efficiently computing the distance between a point and a curve represented as what is called a G-polynomial curve, which is a curve representation that generalizes polynomial curves to matrix Lie groups. The proposed approach exploits the structure of these curves to reduce the problem to a small number of polynomial root-finding computations. Simulation results show that the method significantly reduces computation time while maintaining accuracy compared to existing optimization-based approaches. Practical formulas are also provided for the case of the group SE(3), and the method is validated experimentally on a robotic manipulator. The methodology is implemented in a computational package, available online.

</details>


### [217] [Gotta Grow Fast: Design and Benchmarking of a Tip Mount for High-Speed Vine Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06040)
*Antonio Alvarez Valdivia,Robert Reeve,Ankush Dhawan,Ciera McFarland,Chad Council,Margaret McGuinness,Nathaniel Hanson*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种三角滚轮尖端安装座，通过滚动而非滑动减少生长阻力，首次实现TPU涂层防撕裂尼龙材料的一致外翻生长，并建立了可重复的基准测试框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 藤蔓机器人在尖端集成摄像头和传感器时面临独特挑战：尖端材料不断更新、内部层间摩擦、附加尖端重量和织物收缩导致传感器/工具安装困难，限制了其在检测和搜索任务中的应用。

Method: 设计三角滚轮尖端安装座，通过滚动接触替代滑动接触降低内部阻力；通过迭代故障分析优化设计；构建定制测试台通过测量外翻过程中的尾部张力来隔离评估尖端安装效果。

Result: 与包括先前设计在内的多种安装变体相比，三角滚轮安装座实现了最低的尾部张力和最可重复的生长性能，并首次在TPU涂层防撕裂尼龙材料上实现一致外翻。

Conclusion: 建立了经过验证的尖端安装座设计以及可重复的基准测试框架，推动了软体生长机器人传感器和工具集成的进步。

Abstract: Soft, growing vine robots extend through tip eversion, a mechanism that enables navigation through cluttered environments. However, integrating cameras and other sensors at the tip is uniquely challenging because the material forming the tip is constantly renewed as the robot grows. This continual material turnover, combined with friction between internal layers, added tip weight, and fabric constriction, complicates sensor and tool mounting. These limitations hinder the deployment of vine robots for inspection and search tasks, where rapid growth while carrying tip-mounted sensors is essential. In this work, we present a triangular roller tip mount that reduces internal resistance during growth by rolling rather than sliding against the robot body. The design was refined through iterative failure analysis, enabling, for the first time, consistent eversion on a TPU-coated ripstop nylon vine robot. To quantitatively evaluate mount performance, we introduce a custom testbed that isolates tip mounting effects by measuring tail tension during eversion. Comparative experiments across multiple mount variants, including prior designs, show that our triangular roller mount achieves the lowest tail tension and most repeatable growth performance. These results establish both a validated tip mount design and a repeatable benchmarking framework for advancing sensor and tool integration in soft growing robots. CAD for the mount and testbed is available at: https://sprout-mitll.github.io/tip_mounts/.

</details>


### [218] [VASO: Formally Verifiable Self-Evolving Skills for Physical AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05395)
*Yunhao Yang,Neel P. Bhatt,Kevin Wang,Samuel Tetteh,Zhangyang Wang,Ufuk Topcu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: VASO是一个验证引导的自我进化框架，通过形式化模型检测将反例转化为文本梯度，实现LLM生成的可复用机器人技能合约的自动优化，无需微调基础模型权重，在移动机器人和四旋翼任务上达到97.2%的规范合规率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有技能进化循环依赖执行反馈、单元测试、环境奖励或LLM自我批评等信号，仅提供"追踪级"证据——证明技能在采样执行中有效，无法保证技能诱导的计划在未经测试的条件下满足时间安全性契约。需要一种形式化验证闭环来确保技能合约的可信性。

Method: VASO将每个技能表示为具有两个耦合接口的语义合约：形式化接口（将机器人状态、观测和控制命令与逻辑命题对齐以进行模型检测）和规划器面向接口（引导可执行行为生成）。模型检测器先过滤逻辑不一致的技能合约，再验证技能诱导的计划是否满足全局和局部时间规范。验证失败时，将反例轨迹转化为文本梯度，更新可复用技能合约，保持基础模型权重冻结。

Result: 在Clearpath Jackal移动机器人和PX4四旋翼任务上，VASO以少于100个优化样本达到97.2%的形式规范合规率，优于执行反馈、提示优化和微调基线方法。

Conclusion: VASO是首个在形式化验证与LLM生成技能自我进化之间建立闭环的框架：形式化反例成为可复用机器人技能合约的优化反馈，而非仅验证一次性计划、调优规划器提示或微调模型权重。

Abstract: Reusable robot skills are becoming the basic units through which embodied agents turn open-ended instructions into long-horizon physical behavior. We argue that, while foundation models have collapsed the cost of creating these skills, the cost of trusting them has not.
